Dealer Management System — Layered Process Spec

Agentic, domain-centric end-to-end flow · Lead → Sale → Delivery → Service · with Personas & granular APIs/agents/HITL
CAM Motors & GT Automotive (Guyana) · Living document · v3.0 (enterprise: platform + integration + validation) · Companion to DMS_Master_Spec_v3.md. Governance/NFRs excluded here (handled later).

Agentic operating model. An Orchestrator agent runs each deal as a state machine (enforces gates, advances stages, fires notifications). Specialist agents (A1–A27) do the layer work. Humans are approvers, not typists. "🧠 AI for the persona" = the recommendations & proactive assistance the AI gives the specific human working that layer.

Personas & Capabilities — what the website shows each role

Roles per the GT Automotives BRD (12 business roles) + platform Admin/Super-Admin. Two portals: Dealer Workspace (all roles below) and Platform Console (Super Admin). Enforce on API and UI; the client's field-level access matrix overrides these defaults once received.

Leadership / General Manager Vishok · Aditya

Lands on
Executive Dashboard — company-wide, both divisions (CAM + GT).
Website shows
Consolidated KPIs across Sales/Delivery/Service; CAM vs GT comparison; revenue by model/division, conversion, SLA compliance, channel performance, lost-leads.
Can do
View / drill / export every report & dashboard. Read-only across everything (per BRD).
Scope
All data, both divisions.
Cannot
Day-to-day edits or configuration (unless also granted Admin).

Sales Manager

Lands on
Team Dashboard (division pipeline by advisor + SLA-breach flags).
Website shows
All division leads & advisors, pipeline funnel, SLA dashboard, performance dashboards, team calendar.
Can do
Oversee sales; approve/reject cancellations; approve discounts above threshold; manual reassign/override; create Accounts; view team reports.
Scope
All leads in their division + their advisors.
Cannot
Cross-division data (unless Leadership); dealer config (taxes/templates).

Sales Advisor

Lands on
My Daily Briefing (own leads + today's tasks) — never a raw list.
Website shows
Own leads (New→Vehicle Allocated), Tasks (Call 1st–4th, Send Quotation, Schedule Test Drive, Follow-up on Payment), Calendar, Quotes, Documents, Call logs.
Can do
Create leads; click-to-call; generate/resend Code; schedule test drive; upload docs; capture reservation; allocate VIN; create Accounts; close as "Not Interested".
Scope
Own leads only.
Cannot
Configure taxes; delete leads; see other advisors' leads.

Delivery Advisor

Lands on
Delivery Queue (assigned deliveries).
Website shows
Assigned deliveries, handover/warranty docs, delivery calendar; Tasks (Request Vehicle IDs, Confirm Delivery Date & Time).
Can do
Conduct PDI; enter registration number; generate/print pre-filled handover; upload signed docs; enter Actual Delivery Date.
Scope
Assigned deliveries.
Cannot
Pipeline stages before Pre-Delivery; sales config.

Service Manager

Lands on
Service Operations Dashboard (active cases, SLA breaches, technician load).
Website shows
All division cases & work orders, appointments, warranty/recall queue, advisor performance.
Can do
Approve service requests; review Cases, consult technician, record notes, authorize Work Orders; reports.
Scope
Service operations (division).
Cannot

Service Advisor

Lands on
Service Dashboard (assigned assets, upcoming cadences, open WOs).

Assigned assets/customers, Cases, Work Orders, Service History, service calendar, cadences; Tasks (Book/Confirm Appointment, Update Repair Status, Notify Pickup, Follow-up after service).

Validate customer info; create/close Cases; open Work Orders; book appointments; intake/outtake checklists; prepare quotations & invoices.

Cannot

Financials, pipeline, configuration.

The Layered Process

Layer 0 — Cross-cutting All personas

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Auth resolve. Every request hits Clerk JWT verify → users.clerkId lookup → GET /api/auth/me hydrates {userId, dealerIds[], activeDealerId, roleId, divisionId}. Missing/expired token → 401.
  • 2. Tenant bind. Middleware reads x-dealer-id header, asserts it ∈ caller's dealer_users memberships, then pins res.locals.activeDealerId. Header not in membership set → 403 cross_tenant (never a 404 leak). Corrections(1): this activeDealerId is injected into EVERY Drizzle query builder — leads, customers, audit-logs, object-storage ACL checks, and concierge grounding — no unscoped read exists.
  • 3. RBAC gate. role_permissions(module×category) checked for the route's (module, action). Deny → 403. On pass, role_field_permissions resolved to a hidden|view|edit mask applied at the serializer (read) and validator (write) boundary.
  • 4. Idempotency (mutations). Money/state mutations require Idempotency-Key header → idempotency_keys upsert. Replay of a completed key returns the stored response verbatim; in-flight duplicate → 409 in_progress.
  • 5. State-machine dispatch. Stage advance goes through the orchestrator: POST /leads/:id/advance loads the active stage_checklists version for the dealer, evaluates gates; unmet → 422 {unmet:[]}; met → transition + write stageEnteredAt + timeline_events.
  • 6. Agent governance wrap. Before any LLM call the orchestrator checks kill-switch agents.status==='active' → PII-minimize inputs → guardUntrusted() wraps every untrusted string → invoke sonnet-4-6 → recordAgentRun() into agent_runs with confidence. Corrections(3): confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → suppress auto-act, route to human queue.
  • 7. Side-effects. Notifications enqueue to notifications + unified email_logs outbox (email+WhatsApp) with dedupeKey, retry/backoff by a background worker. tasks created for human follow-ups.
  • 8. Observe. Every request appends audit_logs (dealer-scoped), per-entity timeline_events, and (if agentic) isAgent=true markers. Daily-Briefing and global search read only within activeDealerId.

POST /api/storage/uploads/request-url{objectKey, contentType, scope:{entity,id}}{putUrl, expiresIn} presigned, ACL bound to dealer · 200/403GCS sidecar, documentsdocuments·upload
⚙️ Agents in play
  • A2 Round-robin assign → trigger: new lead (public enquiry / manual create) or POST /leads/:id/assign without advisor → inputs: dealer_users rows scoped to activeDealerId + matching divisionId, ordered by lastLeadAssignedAt ASC → output: {assignedTo} then stamps lastLeadAssignedAt=now() → HITL: no (deterministic, non-LLM); manager may re-assign manually.
  • Governance wrapper (all LLM agents) → trigger: any agent invocation from any layer → inputs: kill-switch row in agents, PII-minimized payload, guardUntrusted()-wrapped untrusted text → output: agent_runs row {runType,inputSummary,outputSummary,confidence,status,latency} → HITL: conditional — below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE auto-routes to human via tasks/gate.
  • A15 Pipeline suggestions → trigger: GET /pipeline/suggestions (dashboard load) → inputs: real leads in each phase (scoped) → output: {leadId, suggestedAction, reason, confidence}[] → HITL: yes — advisory only, human clicks the action.
  • Sentiment digest → trigger: GET /dashboard/sentiment → inputs: ≤40 real comm_notes/call_logs (scoped, guardUntrusted) → output: {overall, movers[], atRisk[]} → HITL: read-only surface.
  • Concierge (nav-only) → trigger: /copilotkit tool calls + /anthropic SSE → inputs: grounding restricted to activeDealerId (Corrections 1); tools are navigation-only, no mutations → output: chat + nav intents → HITL: n/a (cannot write).
  • Autonomous-write agents declared here (Corrections 5): A1 Gmail intake and A6 call-sentiment are the only auto-writers — each registered in agents, kill-switchable, and every run audited in agent_runs. Their per-layer behavior is specified in L1/L2.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Daily Briefing / Next-Best-Action (dashboard): a ranked worklist — "Advance 3 leads past readiness", "2 gates awaiting your approval", "1 booking expires today" — each row deep-links to the single Workflow console.
  • Agent transparency panel: for any AI-touched record the human sees the agent_runs row (confidence, input/output summary) with Approve / Override — disambiguated as the "Approve/Override run" flavor of Review (Corrections 7).
  • Confidence badges: outputs below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE render as "Needs human review" rather than a suggested action, so nothing low-confidence is one-click actionable.
  • Global search assist: natural-language query → typed results, all within tenant; concierge can navigate but never mutate.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Tenant scope: res.locals.activeDealerId mandatory on every query; x-dealer-id must ∈ dealer_users. Second axis divisionId ∈ {CAM, GT}.
  • RBAC: role_permissions(module, category) boolean; role_field_permissions(entity, field, level ∈ hidden|view|edit) — hidden fields stripped from response AND rejected on write.
  • Idempotency: idempotency_keys(key, requestHash, responseSnapshot, status); key required on all money + state mutations; TTL 24h; hash mismatch on same key → 422 key_reuse_mismatch.
  • Round-robin clock: dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt — single source of turn order; updated atomically inside assign txn.
  • Agent governance: agents.status (active|paused kill-switch); MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor (config); agent_runs.confidence compared per run; guardUntrusted() mandatory on notes, inbound WhatsApp/email, uploaded doc text (Corrections 4).
  • Money/dates: stored USD-scale; GYD display = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209); timestamps America/Guyana GMT-4. GRA = Guyana Revenue Authority; server computes tax from dealer_taxes, AI never fabricates TIN/VIN/CIF/duty (Corrections 2).
  • Config touched: versioned stage_checklists (advance gates), dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14%, duty, excise, EV exclusions), lead_sources, roles.
  • SLA timers: outbox retry backoff (exp, max attempts then failed); Gmail IMAP poll every 2 min; booking lazy-expiry checked on read/remind; notification unread aging feeds Daily-Briefing.
  • One CTA: the primary action is always 'Advance to {stage}'; the former standalone "Run Review" is merged into the Workflow console as a readiness view (Corrections 6).

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Audit export (CSV/PDF) — generator: server stream from audit_logs (scoped); layout: header (dealer name, division, date range, exported-by, GMT-4 stamp); columns [timestamp, actor, action, entity, entityId, before→after]; footer (row count, page x/y). Delivery: authenticated download via /storage/objects/* presigned URL.
  • Agent-run report — generator: table view / optional PDF from agent_runs; layout: header (agent key + domain + kill-switch state), rows [runType, at, confidence, status, latency, inputSummary, outputSummary, reviewedBy]; footer (avg confidence, %below-floor). Read-only governance artifact.
  • Daily Briefing — rendered in-app (no PDF); HTML email variant via nodemailer template through outbox: header (dealer + date), sections [Next-Best-Actions, Gates awaiting you, Expiring bookings, Overdue tasks], each line deep-linking to the Workflow console.
  • Notification/outbox payloads — HTML email templates (Gmail SMTP) and WhatsApp text (Meta/Twilio); every payload carries dedupeKey; layouts owned by their originating layer (quote PDF, delivery invoice, etc.) — this layer only governs the outbox envelope, retry/backoff, and per-object ACL on any attached document.
  • Note: all PDFs at feature layers use pdfkit; money lines render GYD = USD-scale × usdExchangeRate; tax lines sourced only from dealer_taxes, never model-invented (Corrections 2).

Layer 1 — Lead Capture (New) Sales Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Channel arrives. Five real intake paths, all resolved to a dealerId + divisionId before any write: (a) public site form → POST /api/enquiries; (b) Meta Lead Ads → POST /api/webhooks/meta; (c) WhatsApp guided bot → POST /api/webhooks/whatsapp or /api/webhooks/twilio/whatsapp; (d) Gmail inbound → IMAP poll every 2 min → AI classify; (e) walk-in / phone → advisor manual POST /api/leads.
  • 2. Tenant + division resolve. Public/webhook routes have no session, so dealerId is derived from the form target / Meta page-id / WhatsApp phone_number_id / inbox address mapping (NOT from a header). Every subsequent query is scoped by this activeDealerId(res) — Correction (1). Unresolvable target → reject, no cross-tenant leak.
  • 3. Guard untrusted text. Inbound free-text (message body, email body, subject, form "message", uploaded doc text) is wrapped with guardUntrusted() before it ever reaches an LLM prompt — Correction (4). Raw text is still stored verbatim; only the model-facing copy is fenced.
  • 4. Agent A1 (Intake/Dedup) fires. Kill-switch check isAgentEnabled('A1') → if paused, lead is created raw with no enrichment. A1 normalizes phone (E.164 Guyana +592), lowercases email, classifies channel/source, extracts selectedModel/interest, and runs dedup.
  • 5. Dedup (email + phone). Scoped lookup within dealerId: match on normalized email OR phone against open leads and customers. Exact match on an open lead → merge (append timeline_events, add interest, do not create duplicate). Match on closed/won lead → reopen (new lead, parentAccountId/prior lead linked, status=new, phase reset). No match → create new.
  • 6. Confidence floor. If A1 classification confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE (dedup ambiguous, model unrecognized, business vs person unclear) → do NOT auto-merge/auto-classify; create lead with status=new, flag needsReview, route to human queue — Correction (3).
  • 7. Round-robin assign (A2, deterministic). Pick eligible dealer_users in the matching divisionId with role permission, ordered by oldest lastLeadAssignedAt; set assignedTo/ownerUserId, bump that user's lastLeadAssignedAt=now(). Not an LLM.
  • 8. Auto-quote / code (A3, deterministic). If interestedVehicleId or selectedModel resolves to priced inventory, A3 runs the tax engine off dealer_taxes and pdfkit to produce a first quote version (quoteNumber, status=current). If nothing resolves, quote is skipped (advisor generates later at Layer 2).
  • 9. Notify. Owner notification via notifications + optional acknowledgement to the customer through the unified email_logs outbox (email or WhatsApp, retry/backoff/dedupeKey). Auto-reply is a template send, never an LLM free-write.
  • 10. Timeline + audit. timeline_events row per action (isAgent=true for A1/A2/A3). Every A1 run recorded via recordAgentRun into agent_runs with PII-minimized inputSummary/outputSummary — Correction (5). Advisor now sees the lead in the Workflow console with the single primary CTA Advance to Contacted.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A1 Intake / Dedup (claude-sonnet-4-6, autonomous-write → declared, audited, kill-switchable). Trigger: POST /enquiries, Meta/WhatsApp webhook, Gmail IMAP classify. Grounded inputs: guarded inbound text + scoped leads/customers (email+phone) for dedup, vehicles for model resolution, lead_sources for source. Output: {channel,source,selectedModel?,interestedVehicleId?,dedupDecision:"new|merge|reopen",matchLeadId?,confidence}. HITL: no above floor (autonomous create/merge); yes below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → needsReview queue. Audited via recordAgentRun.
  • A2 Round-robin assign (deterministic, not LLM). Trigger: on lead create / POST /leads/:id/assign. Inputs: dealer_users filtered by divisionId + role, ordered by lastLeadAssignedAt. Output: {assignedTo} + clock bump. HITL: no (manager may reassign manually).
  • A3 Quote / Code (deterministic tax engine + pdfkit). Trigger: creation with resolved priced vehicle. Inputs: vehicles.price, dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14%, duty, EV exclusion), dealer.usdExchangeRate. Output: quotes row + PDF. HITL: no to generate; human sends.
  • A15 Pipeline suggestions (read-only). Trigger: GET /pipeline/suggestions. Inputs: real leads in phase=new. Output: ranked next-action hints. HITL: advisory only.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • New-lead card shows A1-derived channel/source badge, resolved selectedModel, and a confidence chip; low-confidence leads carry a "Needs review — verify match" banner instead of an auto-merge.
  • aiScore (lead quality) surfaced as a sortable column so the advisor works hottest leads first; score is a hint, never a gate.
  • Pipeline suggestion strip: "3 new leads unworked > 2h — call now" from GET /pipeline/suggestions.
  • Detected preferredLanguage (EN / Guyanese Creole) shown so the advisor picks the matching outbound template; A1 never machine-translates the stored text, only tags it.
  • Concierge (/anthropic SSE, /copilotkit nav-only) can answer "show my new leads today" — grounding is dealer-scoped, no cross-tenant rows, no mutations.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • leads: channel, source+sourceDetail, phase=new, status=new, priority, interestedVehicleId, selectedModel, ownerUserId, assignedTo, aiScore, stageEnteredAt, preferredLanguage, needsReview.
  • Validations: email RFC + lowercased; phone → E.164 +592; at least one of email/phone required; fullName non-empty; source ∈ active lead_sources; interestedVehicleId must exist and be same-tenant + not sold/delivered; selectedModel free-text allowed but flagged if not in inventory; divisionId ∈ dealer's CAM/GT.
  • Money/dates: quote money stored USD-scale, GYD display = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209); stageEnteredAt, validUntil in America/Guyana GMT-4.
  • Config touched: stage_checklists (versioned) defines "new→contacted" gate keys (e.g. contact_attempted); dealer_taxes feeds A3; MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor; A1 kill-switch in agents.
  • SLA timers: first-response target (e.g. 15 min business hours) measured from stageEnteredAt; breach raises a task + owner notification. WhatsApp bot session idle → auto-close conversation after configured window. Gmail poll = 2 min. Outbox retry with backoff on send failure.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Auto-Quote PDF — generator pdfkit (quote-pdf.ts), stored via quotes, delivered through unified email_logs outbox (email or WhatsApp) only on human "Send". Layout: Header — dealer logo (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division, quoteNumber, quote date + validUntil (GMT-4), advisor name. Customer block — name, phone, email. Vehicle block — make/model/trim/year, VIN if allocated, accessories[]. Line items — columns: Description · Qty · Unit (GYD) · Amount (GYD), with base price, accessories, discount as negative line. Tax section — taxLines[] from dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14% by model, import duty, excise; EV duty excluded) computed server-side deterministically, never model-invented. Totals — subtotal, total tax, total (GYD display = USD × usdExchangeRate). Footer — validity note, dealer TIN, terms, "quote not an invoice", page number.
  • Inbound uploaded docs (e.g. licence/ID at capture) — stored polymorphic documents (lead), text run through guardUntrusted; A5 vision may pre-fill allowlisted PREFILL_FIELDS with extractionStatus:"proposed" — HITL confirm at /documents/extraction/review. No GRA/tax figures fabricated here — GRA filing is a later gated step.
  • Auto-acknowledgement message — templated HTML (email) / text (WhatsApp) from emails/templates, language-matched (EN/Creole), sent via outbox with dedupeKey; not an LLM free-write.

Layer 2 — Contacted (24h SLA) Sales Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Lead lands in phase contacted-eligible. On A2 round-robin assign (Layer 1), leads.stageEnteredAt is stamped and the 24h contact SLA clock starts. Advisor opens GET /api/leads/:id/review (the single merged Workflow console — the old standalone "Run Review" readiness view is folded in here, not a separate page). All reads are activeDealerId(res)-scoped via x-dealer-id; a lead outside the active dealer/division 404s.
  • 2. Advisor sees SLA countdown derived from stageEnteredAt + 24h (America/Guyana GMT-4). Amber at <6h remaining, red on breach. A7 Outreach composer has pre-drafted a first-touch message; advisor reads the agent-brief panel.
  • 3. Click-to-call. Advisor hits Call → frontend requests GET /api/telephony/token then GET /api/telephony/config to pick provider. If Twilio Voice browser calling is configured → dial in-browser; else StubTelephonyAdapter logs a manual-call shell. Every attempt creates a call_logs row (provider twilio|stub) via POST /api/leads/:id/calls.
  • 4. Log the call outcome + note. Advisor writes disposition (connected / no-answer / voicemail / wrong-number / callback) and a free-text summary. The summary is stored and any inbound-quoted customer text is wrapped with guardUntrusted before it ever reaches an agent.
  • 5. A6 Call sentiment fires (fire-and-forget). On note save it auto-runs; advisor may also force POST /api/leads/:id/calls/:cid/suggest-sentiment. A6 is a declared, audited, kill-switchable autonomous-write agent — writes call_logs.sentiment only if agents.status='active' and confidence ≥ MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE; below floor it routes to human (leaves sentiment null + flags for review).
  • 6. Conversation notes. Non-call touches (WhatsApp/email recap) go to POST /api/leads/:id/notes → comm_notes/timeline_events. A call_log and a note are distinct rows.
  • 7. 4-call cadence scheduling. On each unconnected attempt the system schedules the next task per cadence 24h → +3d → +3d → +7d (attempts 1-4). Each due task is dealer-scoped and shows in the advisor queue.
  • 8. Branch A — connected & interested: advisor advances via the single primary CTA "Advance to Qualified" → POST /api/leads/:id/advance. The versioned stage_checklists gate for this transition requires ≥1 logged call with a disposition; if absent → 422 {unmet:[...]} and the CTA stays disabled with reasons listed inline (this is the "Readiness check", not manager Approvals, not an AI run).
  • 9. Branch B — 4 attempts exhausted / not interested: advisor closes with reason → POST /api/leads/:id/decision (or PATCH /api/leads/:id status=lost) with mandatory reason + reasonDetail. Timeline event recorded.
  • 10. Every action appends a timeline_events row (isAgent true for A6/A7, false for human) visible in GET /api/leads/:id/timeline.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A6 Call sentiment (claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger: fire-and-forget on call_logs note save + manual /suggest-sentiment → grounded inputs: the single call_logs.note row (PII-minimized, wrapped in guardUntrusted) for this lead within active dealer only → output: {sentiment, confidence} written back to call_logs.sentiment → HITL: autonomous-write BUT gated — declared in agents, kill-switch isAgentEnabled, every run in agent_runs(runType='sentiment'); if confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE it does NOT write, routes to human (advisor sets sentiment manually).
  • A7 Outreach composer → trigger: advisor opens console / GET /leads/:id/agent-brief → grounded inputs: lead row, prior comm_notes, call_logs dispositions, aiScore (all dealer-scoped) → output: {headline, riskLevel, actions[], draftMessage} → HITL: yes, always — never auto-sends; advisor must "Approve & Send" via /outreach or /whatsapp.
  • A15 Pipeline suggestions (read-only) → surfaces this lead in the advisor's "act now" list via GET /pipeline/suggestions when SLA nears breach or a callback is due → HITL: advisory only, no write.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Draft first-touch message (A7 draftMessage) tuned to channel and lead source/interestedVehicle — advisor edits then approves.
  • Risk + next-best-action: riskLevel and actions[] (e.g. "call now, SLA 3h left", "send spec sheet for selectedModel").
  • Sentiment chip per call (A6) — positive/neutral/negative badge on each call_logs row; low-confidence runs show "needs your read" instead of a guessed value.
  • Sentiment digest across recent notes via GET /dashboard/sentiment (≤40 real dealer-scoped notes) for the advisor's book.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • SLA: contact within stageEnteredAt + 24h (GMT-4). Breach → amber<6h, red at 0; breach fires notify-owner to reportingManagerUserId. Timer resets on stage change only, not on each call.
  • Cadence tasks: attempt 1 = +24h from stageEntered, attempt 2 = +3d, attempt 3 = +3d, attempt 4 = +7d. After attempt 4 with no connect → auto-suggest close reason no_contact.
  • call_logs: direction, provider∈{twilio,stub}, disposition (required, enum), durationSec (≥0), sentiment (nullable), callbackAt (must be future, GMT-4).
  • Advance gate (versioned stage_checklists, contacted→qualified): requires ≥1 call_logs row with a non-null disposition. Missing → 422 unmet[]. This is the "Readiness check" — distinct from manager gates (Approvals) and agent agent_runs review (Approve/Override).
  • Close reason mandatory on decision: enum reason + free reasonDetail.
  • guardUntrusted wraps every quoted customer utterance / inbound text before A6/A7 ingestion; PII minimized on the agent call.
  • Config: MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor; agents.status kill-switch; telephony provider auto-select.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • No PDF is generated at Layer 2 — this stage produces communications and logs, not documents. (Quote PDF is a Layer 3+ artifact via quote-pdf.ts / pdfkit.)
  • Outreach message (consumed/produced): A7 draftMessage → advisor-approved → delivered via email_logs unified outbox (nodemailer Gmail SMTP for email; Meta Cloud API v21 / Twilio auto-select for WhatsApp), with retry/backoff/dedupeKey. Body = greeting + selectedModel/interestedVehicle reference + CTA + advisor signature block. HTML email template rendered server-side; WhatsApp = plain text within session window.
  • Call recording / transcript (if Twilio): recordingUrl stored on call_logs, served via dealer-scoped presigned GCS URL (per-object ACL) — never a public link.
  • Timeline export (screen artifact, not PDF): chronological timeline_events list — call dispositions, sentiment chips (isAgent-flagged), notes, outreach sends — dealer-scoped.

Layer 3 — Engaged (test drive · financing · negotiation) Sales Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Enter Engaged phase — lead is in phase=engaged. Advisor opens the single Workflow console for the lead; the merged Readiness check panel (formerly "Run Review") shows stage_checklists gates for the next stage inline. ONE primary CTA reads Advance to Negotiation. Every read is scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id.
  • 2. Offer test drive — advisor clicks "Schedule test drive". First calls GET /leads/:id/availability-check (agent A10, checks customer + vehicle free/soft-lock windows — NOT advisor calendar). Returns candidate slots.
  • 3. Create Test-Drive (first-class entity) — POST /leads/:id/test-drive mints testDriveToken + soft-locks the single unit (vehicles.status=reserved, holdUntil, holdReason='test_drive'). Token link sent to customer via WhatsApp/email outbox.
  • 4. Customer self-books — public GET /test-drive/:token (no auth) renders slot + vehicle; POST /test-drive/:token/book requires licence upload + waiver accept. Slot guard + vehicle-availability guard fire; 409 if unit re-booked. On success testDriveLicence, testDriveWaiver persist; timeline_events row written.
  • 5. Financing doc pre-fill — advisor uploads customer docs; A5 (vision) proposes only allowlisted PREFILL_FIELDS with extractionStatus:'proposed'. Advisor confirms via POST /documents/extraction/review (HITL). Confirmed values seed POST /finance-applications.
  • 6. Outreach nudge — advisor opens GET /leads/:id/agent-brief; A7 returns {headline,riskLevel,actions[],draftMessage}. Advisor edits, then "Approve & Send" via POST /leads/:id/outreach or /whatsapp — never auto-sent. Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE the brief is flagged "route to human", no draft auto-populated.
  • 7. Quote regenerate/resend — after negotiation, advisor calls POST /leads/:id/quote (A3 deterministic tax engine → new versioned quoteNumber, prior marked superseded), then POST /leads/:id/quotes/:qid/send.
  • 8. Negotiation notes — POST /leads/:id/notes; any customer-sourced text (inbound WhatsApp/email quotes) is wrapped with guardUntrusted before display/AI use.
  • 9a. Advance — POST /leads/:id/advance; if checklist gates unmet returns 422 {unmet:[]}, else phase progresses and soft-lock/reservation flow continues into Layer 4.
  • 9b. Not-Interested close — POST /leads/:id/decision {decision:'not_interested', reason}; releases soft-lock (vehicles.status=available, clears holdUntil), sets status=lost, cancels any pending outbox drafts.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A10 Test-drive availability → trigger GET /leads/:id/availability-check → grounded on vehicles(status/holdUntil), bookings, existing test-drive slots for that customer + vehicle only (deterministic, not LLM) → output slots[]{start,end,vehicleFree,customerFree} → HITL yes: advisor picks the slot; no auto-booking.
  • A5 Document pre-fill (vision, claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger financing doc upload → grounded on uploaded customer_documents pixels only; outputs restricted to allowlist PREFILL_FIELDS (name, national ID, address, income line) → output {field,value,confidence,extractionStatus:'proposed'} → HITL yes: /documents/extraction/review confirm; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE field is blanked + flagged. Declared, kill-switchable (agents.status), audited via recordAgentRun.
  • A7 Outreach composer (claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger GET /leads/:id/agent-brief → grounded on this-dealer leads, comm_notes, sentiment; all inbound text passed through guardUntrusted → output {headline,riskLevel,actions[],draftMessage,confidence} → HITL yes, always: human "Approve & Send"; never auto-sends. If confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → routedToHuman:true, no draft.
  • A3 Quote/Code → trigger POST /leads/:id/quote → grounded on dealer_taxes(VAT 0/14% by model, duty, EV exclusion) + vehicle price/discount → output versioned quote + pdfkit PDF → HITL no for compute (deterministic), yes to send.
  • A6 Call sentiment (if a negotiation call is logged) → fire-and-forget on POST /leads/:id/calls note → grounded on guardUntrusted transcript → writes call_logs.sentiment, agent_runs → HITL no (advisory) + manual /suggest-sentiment. Declared + audited + kill-switchable.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Agent brief card: headline + riskLevel (e.g. "Went cold 4 days, competitor mention") + ranked actions[] ("resend quote with 3% accessory bundle", "offer Saturday test-drive slot").
  • Editable draft message pre-loaded into the outreach composer — advisor tweaks tone/price, sees a confidence badge; low-confidence shows "AI unsure — write manually".
  • Pre-fill diff view: extracted financing fields shown side-by-side with blanks, each with confidence; advisor confirms/edits before the finance application is created.
  • Sentiment chip on logged negotiation calls (positive/neutral/negative) to prioritise follow-up.
  • All grounding is dealer-scoped — brief never surfaces another dealer's leads/notes.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Test-drive: testDriveToken single-use; testDriveLicence + testDriveWaiver both required before book (422 otherwise); soft-lock holdUntil default +2h from slot end, holdReason='test_drive'; single-unit inventory locked exclusively.
  • Registration display validated ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$; VIN & engineNumber 17 chars (read-only here).
  • Finance: amount > downPayment; termMonths 12–84; apr populated by LOS not human; PREFILL_FIELDS allowlist strictly enforced (403 on out-of-list field).
  • Quote: taxes computed server-side from dealer_taxes; money stored USD-scale, GYD shown = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate; validUntil default +14d; sending a new quote sets prior status='superseded'.
  • SLA: agent-brief follow-up timer on stageEnteredAt; outbox retry/backoff on send failure; availability slots expire when holdUntil passes (lazy-expiry via /bookings/:id/remind pattern).
  • Config: stage_checklists (versioned) drives Readiness gates for Advance; MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor; agents.status kill-switch; idempotency_keys on advance.
  • Every inbound/customer text (notes, WhatsApp, uploaded doc OCR) → guardUntrusted before render or AI use.
🔀 Edge-of-edge cases → handling
ScenarioDetectionHandlingOwnerHITL?

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Quote PDF (quote-pdf.ts, pdfkit; delivered via WhatsApp/email outbox on /quotes/:qid/send):
    • Header: dealer logo + name (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division badge, quoteNumber + version, issue date (America/Guyana GMT-4), validUntil.
    • Customer / vehicle block: customer name, contact; vehicle make/model/trim/year, registration, VIN (masked), selected accessories.
    • Line-item columns: Description · Qty · Unit price (GYD) · Amount (GYD). Rows: vehicle price, accessories, trade-in credit (negative), discount (negative).
    • Tax section: itemised taxLines[] from dealer_taxes — VAT (0% or 14% by model), import duty, excise; EV duty-excluded flagged. Server-computed, never AI.
    • Totals: Subtotal, Total tax, Grand total (GYD) = USD-scale × usdExchangeRate, plus USD reference line.
    • Footer: validity terms, "not a tax invoice", dealer TIN, page number.
  • Test-drive waiver — presented on GET /test-drive/:token; accepted copy + timestamp stored (testDriveWaiver); licence image stored in documents/customer_documents (GCS, per-object dealer-scoped ACL).
  • Financing documents — uploaded to finance_documents; A5 vision extraction JSON stored in documents.extraction (jsonb) with extractionStatus; consumed (not generated) — no PDF produced at this layer.
  • Outreach message — HTML email template (nodemailer) or WhatsApp template; rendered from human-approved body, queued in email_logs outbox with dedupeKey, retry/backoff by background worker.

Layer 4 — Pre-Book (Order Confirmed / reservation) Sales Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Lock the model. Advisor sets leads.selectedModel and confirms interestedVehicleId via PATCH /api/leads/:id. Every write is scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; division (CAM/GT) carried on the lead.
  • 2. Resolve the buyer to an account. Advisor searches existing customers (GET /api/customers?q=). Agent A1 Intake/Dedup proposes matches (phone/email/name/business TIN). Advisor either links an existing customers.id or creates one via POST /api/customers (accountType person|business). Household/business hierarchy set via parentAccountId; relationships confirmed at PUT /api/customers/:id/relations.
  • 3. Attach primary contact. POST /api/customers/:id/contacts with isPrimary=true (person = self; business = signatory). Merge duplicates only after human confirm (A1 is HITL here — never auto-merges accounts with assets/deals).
  • 4. Soft-lock the vehicle. On booking intent the vehicle moves available → reserved with holdUntil + holdReason='booking' (soft-lock), preventing another advisor from double-booking the same VIN.
  • 5. Take the reservation fee (mandatory) OR trusted bypass. POST /api/bookings creates the RESERVATION (bookingAmount, expiresAt). Fee is collected via POST /api/payments under an idempotency_keys guard → recomputes invoice + emits receipts. On success leads.reservationFeePaid=true. Trusted bypass (fleet/repeat VIP, manager-approved) records a gate-backed waiver instead of cash and still sets reservationFeePaid=true with bookingAmount=0 + waiverReason.
  • 6. Create deal desking. POST /api/deals at stage='desking' linking leadId + customerId + vehicleId; seeds vehiclePrice, tradeInValue (from appraisal passthrough), discount, otdPrice. Discount >5% will later trip the below_floor_price gate (Layer 5) — flagged now, not blocked.
  • 7. Advance the stage — the ONE primary CTA. Single button "Advance to Booked" calls POST /api/leads/:id/advance. Readiness (the merged former "Run Review") is shown inline in the Workflow console; if stage_checklists gates fail it returns 422 {unmet:[]} (e.g. fee unpaid, no primary contact, no selectedModel).
  • 8. Verbal-commit follow-up. A tasks row is created for the advisor with an SLA timer; Agent A7 Outreach composer drafts a confirmation message; advisor "Approve & Send" via POST /api/leads/:id/whatsapp or /outreach (never auto-sent). Outbound queued to the email_logs outbox.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A1 Intake/Dedup (claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger: advisor opens customer resolver / creates account → inputs: customers, contacts, leads rows within activeDealerId only (dealer-scoped grounding) → output: {dedupCandidates:[{customerId, score, matchedOn:['phone','email','tin']}]} → HITL: yes — advisor confirms link/merge; A1 never merges accounts that own assets/deals autonomously. Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → shown as "possible match, review" not applied.
  • A3 Quote/Code (deterministic tax engine + pdfkit) → trigger: deal desking created / fee receipt → inputs: dealer_taxes (VAT 0%|14%, duty), vehicles.price, dealer.usdExchangeRate → output: server-computed otdPrice, GYD figures, receipt PDF → HITL: no (deterministic; no LLM invents tax).
  • A7 Outreach composer → trigger: verbal-commit follow-up task created → inputs: leads, bookings.expiresAt, customer_personas (dealer-scoped) → output: {headline, riskLevel, actions[], draftMessage} → HITL: yes — advisor "Approve & Send"; never auto-sends. Untrusted prior inbound text wrapped with guardUntrusted before prompting.
  • A2 Round-robin → deterministic (dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt); no LLM. Already-assigned lead re-checked for ownership only.
  • Every AI call: isAgentEnabled kill-switch check → recordAgentRun to agent_runs (runType, inputSummary, outputSummary, confidence, latency) → PII-minimized inputs.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Dedup suggestions: "This buyer matches an existing CAM Motors account (Sharma Household) — link instead of create?" with match reasons and confidence badge.
  • Persona-fit nudge: from /customers/:id/persona — "Repeat buyer, loyaltyTier=Gold, LTV GYD 4.2M — eligible for trusted fee bypass" (advisor decides).
  • Agent brief on the console: headline + riskLevel (e.g. "Verbal commit, fee unpaid 26h — reservation expires in 22h") and a ready draftMessage for one-click approve.
  • Readiness inline: the merged Workflow console lists exactly which stage_checklists gates are unmet before the single "Advance to Booked" CTA.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • leads.selectedModel (required to advance), interestedVehicleId, reservationFeePaid (bool gate), stageEnteredAt.
  • customers.accountType ∈ {person, business}; business requires tin; parentAccountId must be same-dealer (cross-tenant rejected 403).
  • contacts.isPrimary — exactly one per account (422 primary_exists on second).
  • bookings.bookingAmount ≥ dealer min (or 0 with waiverReason on trusted bypass); expiresAt default now+72h (dealer-config); status active|converted|expired|cancelled.
  • payments.method enum (no gateway — internal ledger); idempotencyKey mandatory; recompute invoice → receipts. Money stored USD-scale; GYD display = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209).
  • deals.discount >5% sets belowFloorFlag (gate enforced at commit, Layer 5), otdPrice server-computed from dealer_taxes.
  • SLA: vehicle soft-lock holdUntil = fee-window; verbal-commit follow-up tasks.dueAt = +24h; reservation lazy-expiry checked on any GET /bookings/:id or /remind. Dates America/Guyana GMT-4.
  • Config touched: stage_checklists (versioned; booked-entry gates), dealer_taxes (VAT/duty for fee invoice).

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Reservation Receipt (PDF) — generator: pdfkit via A3; delivery: email_logs outbox → WhatsApp/email (dedupeKey). Layout: Header dealer logo + name (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division, address, TIN, receipt no. + date (GMT-4). Buyer block customer name/account, primary contact. Body single line: "Vehicle Reservation — {make/model/trim/year}, VIN {vin}". Line-item columns Description · Amount (USD) · Amount (GYD @ rate). Totals booking amount, method, balance-due-on-purchase note, expiresAt. Footer "Reservation fee applied to final OTD price; refundable per dealer policy" + payment/idempotency ref.
  • Reservation / Booking Confirmation — WhatsApp/email template (nodemailer HTML): model, VIN, fee paid, hold-until date, advisor name, next steps. Human-approved via A7 before send.
  • Trusted-Bypass Waiver note — no PDF; recorded as gates evidence + customer_notes with waiverReason and approving manager.
  • Deal Desking Sheet (screen, not PDF here) — internal; vehiclePrice, discount, tradeInValue, server-computed otdPrice; PDF quote issued in Layer 5 (/leads/:id/quote.pdf).

Layer 5 — Vehicle Allocated Sales Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Enter layer. Lead is in phase where selectedModel (make/model/trim/year) is set but no physical interestedVehicleId VIN is bound yet. Sales Advisor opens the single Workflow console (Readiness-check view merged in — no separate "Run Review" screen per CORRECTION 6).
  • 2. Inventory match. GET /api/vehicles?status=available&make=&model=&trim=&year= — dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id, filtered to divisions the advisor may see (CAM/GT). Advisor sees the multi-inventory list of physical units matching selectedModel.
  • 3. A11 VIN-allocation proposal. On the deal draft, agent A11 proposes the best available VIN for selectedModel (oldest holdUntil-free unit, division-matched). Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → no auto-pick, routed to human list (CORRECTION 3).
  • 4. Advisor selects VIN. Advisor picks one physical unit; server validates vin (17) + engineNumber (17) + registration ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$ against the vehicle row.
  • 5. Soft-lock. PATCH /api/vehicles/:id sets status: reserved (soft-lock) with holdUntil (SLA timer) + holdReason. VEHICLE_STATUS_TRANSITIONS guard rejects illegal jumps. Binds leads.interestedVehicleId.
  • 6. Recall / damage monitor. Selected VIN is checked against recall/damage flag on the vehicle row; if flagged, a gates row (advisory) surfaces and blocks the deal-commit CTA until cleared.
  • 7. Readiness gate. POST /api/leads/:id/advance — checklist gates (config-driven stage_checklists). Returns 422 {unmet:[]} if VIN not allocated / recall open. Success advances stage and firms toward Vehicle status → booked at deal commit (Layer 6 VIN-lock via A11).
  • 8. Timeline + audit. Allocation writes a timeline_events row (per-lead + per-vehicle); A11 run recorded in agent_runs.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A11 VIN allocation → trigger: deal draft / advisor opens allocation on a lead with selectedModel → grounded inputs: vehicles rows WHERE dealer_id=active AND status='available' AND make/model/trim/year match, division-scoped; leads.selectedModel, leads.divisionId → output shape: {vehicleId, vin, rationale, confidence}, plus on commit performs VIN-lock (vehicles.status→booked). HITL: yes — advisor confirms the VIN; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE the agent proposes nothing and routes the full candidate list to the human. Deterministic post-step (VIN-lock) is not LLM. Kill-switchable via agents.status=paused; recorded in agent_runs.
  • A10 Test-drive / vehicle availability → trigger: GET /availability-check → inputs: customer + selected vehicles row status/holdUntil (not advisor calendar) → output: {vehicleAvailable,conflicts[]}. HITL: n/a (read-only signal into the console).
  • A15 Pipeline suggestions → trigger: GET /pipeline/suggestions → inputs: real leads in this phase → output: next-best-action cards ("allocate VIN for lead X"). HITL: yes — advisory only.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Ranked VIN candidate list for selectedModel with plain rationale ("oldest in stock, no hold, CAM division") — advisor clicks to confirm.
  • Recall/damage badge on any candidate VIN so the advisor never allocates a flagged unit.
  • Confidence chip on the A11 pick; when below floor the console shows "review manually — {n} candidates" instead of a pre-selected VIN.
  • Availability signal (A10) inline so the advisor sees if the physical unit is already soft-locked elsewhere.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • vin = exactly 17 chars; engineNumber = exactly 17 chars; registration = ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$ — server-validated on the vehicle row before lock.
  • leads.interestedVehicleId bound to the chosen vehicles.id; leads.selectedModel must match the vehicle make/model/trim/year.
  • VEHICLE_STATUS_TRANSITIONS: available → reserved → booked → delivered/sold (also in_transit, service); reverse/illegal jumps rejected 422.
  • Soft-lock SLA: holdUntil timer (dealer-configured, America/Guyana GMT-4) with holdReason; on expiry lazy-release back to available.
  • Config touched: stage_checklists (versioned) gate vin_allocated + recall_clear; dealer_taxes not touched here (tax at quote/deal). Money fields untouched (USD-scale; GYD = ×usdExchangeRate).
  • Every read/write dealer-scoped (CORRECTION 1); untrusted inbound text on the lead (notes/WhatsApp) wrapped with guardUntrusted before display (CORRECTION 4).

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Vehicle allocation summary (screen card, no PDF at this layer) — VIN, engine number, registration, make/model/trim/year, price (USD-scale, GYD display ×usdExchangeRate), holdUntil, holdReason, division, recall/damage badge. Consumed in the Workflow console.
  • Quote PDF (produced upstream by A3 via pdfkit quote-pdf.ts, delivered via email/WhatsApp outbox email_logs) is now VIN-bound. Layout: header dealer name/logo, quoteNumber, date (GMT-4); vehicle section VIN / engineNumber / registration / make-model-trim-year; line-items columns Description · Qty · Unit (GYD) · Amount; tax lines from dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14%, duty — GRA Guyana, server-computed, never AI-invented); totals subtotal → tax → total in GYD; footer validUntil, status, dealer TIN.
  • No delivery invoice / sales-order PDF is generated at Layer 5 — those are auto-created downstream at deal commit and delivery advance.

Layer 6 — Payment (Payment in Progress) Finance / Sales Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Deal snapshot & below-floor gate. Advisor opens the deal (stage desking) and enters negotiated vehiclePrice, discount, tradeInValue. On PATCH /deals/:id the server recomputes otdPrice via the dealer_taxes engine. If discount > 5% of vehiclePrice, server refuses to advance and opens a gates row type=below_floor_price (evidence = price math snapshot). Advance is blocked until a manager runs POST /gates/:id/resolve (this is an Approval, not a Readiness check — see disambiguation). Every query is scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; division CAM/GT carried from the lead.
  • 2. Financing branch (optional). If not cash, advisor creates finance_applications (amount, downPayment, termMonths, apr, bankId). POST /finance-applications/:id/submit pushes to LOS via losConnector (live = Demerara Bank REST, else sandbox DTI underwriting); los_submissions.mode records which. Status walks pending→submitted→under_review→approved|declined→disbursed. A declined result opens gates type=credit_decline for human handling. lead.financingQualified flips on approved.
  • 3. Reservation invoice (dual-invoice #1). When a booking (reservation) exists, server issues the first invoices row for the bookingAmount/reservation fee (status issued). lead.reservationFeePaid tracks its settlement.
  • 4. Take payment (idempotent). Advisor records money via POST /payments with an Idempotency-Key header (row in idempotency_keys). Method ∈ cash|card|bank_transfer|cheque|mobile_money|financing — no gateway, this is an internal ledger only. Server recomputes the target invoice's paid total, transitions issued→partially_paid→paid, and emits a receipts row + timeline_event.
  • 5. Final invoice (dual-invoice #2). On deal commit, VIN-lock fires (A11) and the server auto-generates the final invoices for otdPrice minus reservation fee minus tradeInValue minus downPayment/financed portion. Tax lines mirror quotes.taxLines[] recomputed at commit time.
  • 6. Balance & reconcile. GET /outstanding-balances surfaces net due per customer/deal. When invoice hits paid and (cash) or disbursed (financing), deal.depositPaid/settlement satisfied — this feeds the Layer-7 delivery checklist gate (invoice step). Over/under/bounced handled per edge table.
  • 7. Refund / release path. Any refund (cancelled booking, void invoice) opens gates type=refund_release; ledger reversal only after resolve. Voids never delete — invoice moves to void and a reversing receipt is written.
🔌 APIs & data (request → response)

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A3 Quote/Code (deterministic tax engine) → trigger: PATCH /deals/:id price change & invoice creation → inputs: deals.vehiclePrice/discount/tradeInValue, dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14% by model, import duty, excise; threshold, excludeEv), dealer.usdExchangeRate → output: {taxLines[], otdPrice, total} written to quotes/invoices. Not an LLM — pure server math. HITL: no (but below-floor result routes to a human gate).
  • A11 VIN allocation → trigger: deal commit → inputs: vehicles row for interestedVehicleId, current status → output: VIN-lock, vehicles.status→booked, final invoice seeded. HITL: no; concurrency guarded (edge table).
  • LOS connector (Demerara live / sandbox DTI) → trigger: /submit+/sync → inputs: finance_applications + banks; declared, audited via recordAgentRun, kill-switchable (agents.status). Output: underwriting decision. HITL: yes — a declined raises gates type=credit_decline; server never auto-approves financing.
  • A6 Call sentiment (fire-and-forget, autonomous-write) → only if advisor logs a payment-negotiation call note → wrapped in guardUntrusted, isAgentEnabled checked, recordAgentRun written. Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → no write, routed to human. HITL: implicit (advisor edits).
  • No AI touches money. Tax, APR selection floor, and invoice totals are deterministic. AI never invents amounts, TIN, CIF, or duty (GRA correction) — any GRA/tax doc extraction is Layer-elsewhere and server-computed.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Agent brief on the deal (GET /leads/:id/agent-brief) surfaces {headline, riskLevel, actions[], draftMessage} — e.g. "Balance GYD 1,463,000 outstanding 6 days; draft payment reminder." Advisor must Approve & Send via /outreach or /whatsapp — never auto-sent.
  • Pipeline suggestions (GET /pipeline/suggestions) flags deals stalled at partial-payment so finance can nudge, grounded on real invoices.status=partially_paid rows only.
  • Below-floor context: the below_floor_price gate card shows the deterministic margin math for the manager — AI is advisory only, the number is server-computed.
  • All inbound customer text used in drafts (WhatsApp/email replies about payment) is wrapped with guardUntrusted before reaching the model.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Money scale: all amounts stored USD-scale; GYD display = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209). Round GYD to whole; never store the GYD figure.
  • Below-floor: discount / vehiclePrice > 0.05 → mandatory gates type=below_floor_price; advance/commit returns 422 until resolved.
  • Idempotency: every POST /payments requires Idempotency-Key; replay returns the original 200 result, never double-posts the ledger.
  • dealer_taxes engine: per line — VAT (0% EV / 14% std), import duty, excise; percent|fixed, threshold, excludeEv. Recomputed at deal edit and again at commit (values may drift).
  • Invoice status machine: issued→partially_paid→paid (or void). void is terminal + writes a reversing receipt.
  • Finance validations: termMonths > 0, downPayment ≤ amount, apr within bank band; bankId must be dealer-scoped active.
  • Dual invoice: reservation invoice (bookingAmount) + final invoice (otdPrice − reservation − tradeIn − down/financed). Sum of applied payments across both = total settlement.
  • SLA: partial-payment reminder at +72h; booking soft-lock (holdUntil/expiresAt) lazy-expiry on /bookings/:id/remind; LOS /sync poll cadence until decision, credit gate SLA 24h.
  • Currency stamping: receipts & invoices persist currency:"USD" and snapshot exchangeRate used, so historical GYD display is reproducible even after rate change.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Reservation Invoice (PDF, pdfkit) — delivery: email outbox + WhatsApp link. Layout: Header dealer logo + name (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division, invoiceNumber, issue date (America/Guyana), status badge. Party block customer + contact. Line items columns [Description | Qty | Unit (USD) | VAT | Amount (USD)] + GYD equivalent column at stored rate. Totals subtotal, tax lines (VAT/duty/excise), reservation fee due. Footer "USD ledger — GYD @ {rate}", terms, TIN placeholder left blank (never AI-filled).
  • Final Sales Invoice (PDF, pdfkit) — auto-generated on commit. Same skeleton; line items show vehiclePrice, discount (as negative line, gate-approved), tradeInValue (negative), tax lines from dealer_taxes, then otdPrice. Credits row for reservation fee + downPayment + financed amount → net balance due. VIN/registration printed only from the locked vehicles row. Feeds Layer-7 delivery invoice step.
  • Receipt (PDF, pdfkit) — per payments row. Header receiptNumber, linked invoiceNumber. Body: amount (USD + GYD), method, reference, receivedAt, running balance after this payment. Reversing receipts render amount in parentheses (negative) with reason. Delivery: email/WhatsApp outbox, downloadable via /receipts/:id.
  • Outstanding-balance statement — from GET /outstanding-balances, on-screen + optional PDF: per-deal otdPrice, paid, financed, balance, currency + GYD display; used for the +72h reminder draft (human-approved before send).

Layer 7 — Pre-Delivery (Ready for Delivery) Delivery Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Auto-provision on commit. When Layer 6 fires PATCH /deals/:id with stage=committed, the server VIN-locks the vehicle (agent A11), sets vehicles.status='booked', and auto-creates the delivery row with the 11-step steps jsonb all in pending. Delivery inherits dealId, customerId, vehicleId, activeDealerId, divisionId.
  • 2. Ownership handoff. Delivery lands in the GET /delivery-advisors pool; a Delivery Advisor is picked (or round-robin via dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt) and set on the delivery. Timeline event written (per-entity, isAgent=false).
  • 3. Step 1 sales_order. Advisor opens the single Workflow console; primary CTA is "Advance to PDI Checklist" (one CTA only — the old "Run Review" readiness view is merged in as an inline Readiness check panel). POST /deliveries/:id/advance validates prior-step completeness; returns 422 {unmet:[]} if the sales order isn't confirmed.
  • 4. Step 2 pdi_checklist. Advisor works the PDI via PATCH /deliveries/:id/pdi, ticking each pdiItems[] (exterior, interior, fluids, tyre pressure, road-test, accessories present). Every item must be pass or explicitly waived+reason before advance.
  • 5. Step 3 registration. Advisor records the 3rd-party GRA/registration submission status (pending|submitted|issued) and the plate registration (validated ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$). GRA duty/VAT is server-computed deterministically from dealer_taxes at invoice step — never invented by AI; a gates.type='gra_filing' gate confirms before any GRA submit.
  • 6. Step 4 insurance. Advisor attaches the insurance cover note (customer_documents / polymorphic documents), which may be AI pre-filled (agent A5, extractionStatus:'proposed') then HITL-confirmed via POST /documents/extraction/review.
  • 7. Step 5 invoice. Advance triggers auto-invoice: server recomputes OTD from deals.otdPrice + dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14% by model, import duty, EV exclusions) in GYD (USD-scale × dealer.usdExchangeRate), issues invoices (status='issued'), and pdfkit renders the delivery invoice.
  • 8. Readiness gate. Inline Readiness check aggregates: PDI 100% pass, registration issued, insurance attached, invoice issued, deposit/balance status. Handover cannot advance to appointment/delivery until all green — 422 {unmet:[]} otherwise. This is the Layer-7 exit; Layers 8+ (appointment→signature→feedback) follow.
  • 9. Handover pack pre-fill. Handover checklist / delivery note PDF fields (owner name, VIN, engine no., plate, accessories) are pre-filled from grounded rows; advisor reviews before the appointment step.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A11 VIN allocation → trigger: deal commit in Layer 6 → inputs: deals.vehicleId, vehicles(status,holdUntil) scoped to dealer → output: VIN-lock (vehicles.status='booked', holdReason='deal_committed') + auto-created deliveries row → HITL: no (deterministic); audited via recordAgentRun, kill-switchable via agents.status.
  • A5 Document pre-fill (vision) → trigger: insurance cover note / registration doc uploaded → inputs: uploaded documents.extraction (untrusted text wrapped with guardUntrusted) → output: {fields} restricted to allowlist PREFILL_FIELDS, extractionStatus:'proposed', confidence → HITL: yes — advisor confirms via /documents/extraction/review; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE routes to human, no auto-fill.
  • A3 Quote/Code (tax engine) → trigger: invoice step advance → inputs: deals.otdPrice, dealer_taxes rows (VAT/duty/excise, excludeEv, threshold), dealer.usdExchangeRate → output: deterministic taxLines[] + pdfkit invoice PDF → HITL: no (pure compute, no LLM); GRA figures never LLM-generated.
  • GRA vision extract → trigger: POST /gra/extract on registration/import doc → inputs: doc image, dealer-scoped → output: legible fields ONLY (never invents TIN/VIN/CIF/duty); server computes tax; gates.type='gra_filing' → HITL: yes, manager resolves gate before /gra/filings.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Readiness check panel surfaces a plain-language "3 items blocking handover" summary derived from the unmet[] array — deterministic, not generative.
  • Pre-filled handover/insurance fields shown as proposed (yellow) chips the advisor taps to confirm; extraction confidence displayed; sub-floor confidence auto-hidden and flagged "manual entry required".
  • GRA extract shows only fields the vision model could actually read off the document, each with a "verify against source" prompt; computed duty/VAT shown separately with the dealer_taxes rule that produced it.
  • No auto-send: any customer-facing "your car is ready" outreach is drafted by A7 (agent-brief) and requires human Approve & Send.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Fields: deliveries.steps (jsonb 11-step, this layer covers sales_order→invoice), pdiItems[], registration, registrationStatus, signatureData(later), serviceAdvisorUserId, invoiceId.
  • Validations: plate ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$; VIN & engineNumber = 17 chars (read-only, from vehicle); PDI item waived requires waiveReason; advance blocked unless prior step complete (422 unmet[]).
  • Tax/config: dealer_taxes (VAT percent 0|14 by model, import duty, excise, threshold, excludeEv) computed server-side; money USD-scale → GYD × dealer.usdExchangeRate(~209); dates America/Guyana GMT-4.
  • SLA: PDI complete within 48h of handoff; registration follow-up reminder at 72h; invoice must be issued before appointment scheduling; stageEnteredAt/step timestamps drive aging.
  • Scoping: every query filtered by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; object-storage cover-note fetch enforces per-object ACL.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Delivery Invoice PDF — generator pdfkit (delivery invoice module) via GET /deliveries/:id/invoice.pdf; channel: download + email outbox (email_logs, retry/backoff/dedupe) / WhatsApp on customer opt-in. Layout: Header (dealer logo, dealer name + division CAM/GT, TIN, invoiceNumber, issue date America/Guyana). Bill-To (customer/business, address). Vehicle block (make/model/trim/year, VIN 17, engine no. 17, registration plate, colour, accessories). Line-item table columns: Description | Qty | Unit (GYD) | VAT% | Amount (GYD) — vehicle price, accessories, then tax lines from dealer_taxes (VAT 0/14%, import duty, excise). Totals block: subtotal, total tax, OTD total GYD, deposit paid, balance due. Footer: server-computed note "Taxes computed per GRA schedule", payment terms, dealer contact.
  • PDI / Handover Checklist — generated from pdiItems[]; sections: Exterior, Interior, Mechanical/Fluids, Road-test, Accessories, Documents. Columns: Item | Pass/Fail/Waived | Notes; footer: advisor name, date, customer signature block (populated at signature step). Delivered in handover pack.
  • Registration / GRA filing doc — consumed via POST /gra/extract (vision, legible fields only); produced filing summary shows server-computed duty/VAT with source dealer_taxes rule and gates.type='gra_filing' evidence trail; no AI-invented figures.
  • Insurance cover note — consumed (uploaded to documents, polymorphic), A5 pre-fills allowlisted fields extractionStatus:'proposed', HITL-confirmed; stored under dealer-scoped object ACL.

Layer 8 — Delivered (Closed Won) Delivery Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Open handover console. GET /api/deliveries/:id loads the delivery created at deal commit (Layer 7). Steps jsonb render as the 11-step tracker: sales_order→pdi_checklist→registration→insurance→invoice→appointment→delivery→signature→feedback. All reads scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; cross-division (CAM/GT) delivery is 404, not 403 (tenant isolation).
  • 2. Confirm prior steps closed. Advance is strictly sequential: server rejects POST /:id/advance with 422 {unmet:[]} if registration (reg plate ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$), insurance, or invoice steps are incomplete. Invoice must already be auto-issued and reconciled against payments (no outstanding-balances row for this deal).
  • 3. Pre-handover damage / PDI re-verify. Advisor walks the vehicle with customer; if a defect appears since PDI, PATCH /:id/pdi re-opens pdiItems[] — this blocks the delivery step until re-passed (see edge table).
  • 4. Identity / authorized-rep check. Advisor confirms the person taking delivery is the customers account holder or a recorded authorized rep (contact on account with power-of-attorney doc). Mismatch → hold, do not sign.
  • 5. Capture actual delivery date. Set deliveredAt (America/Guyana GMT-4) on the delivery step advance — this is the real handover timestamp, not scheduled appointment date.
  • 6. Signature capture + upload. Customer signs on-device → signatureData (base64 PNG) written; a photographed signed sales-order/handover sheet is uploaded via POST /api/storage/uploads/request-url then registered as documents (polymorphic delivery). A5 vision OCR verifies the uploaded sheet's printed name/VIN/reg against the delivery — extractionStatus:'proposed', HITL confirm.
  • 7. Advance signature→feedback. POST /:id/advance. On the terminal delivery/feedback transition the server atomically: (a) sets vehicles.status='delivered' (VIN-locked since commit); (b) ensures the auto-invoice is issued→paid; (c) creates the Asset row (assets: accountId, vehicleId, dealId, deliveryId, deliveredAt); (d) assigns a Service Advisor (assets.serviceAdvisorUserId) via round-robin over the delivering division's advisors; (e) writes timeline_events and flips deals.stage='delivered' + lead phase closed-won.
  • 8. Feedback / CSAT survey within 24h. Advance enqueues a survey to the customer's channel via email_logs outbox (email + WhatsApp), dedupeKey=delivery:{id}:csat. Survey landing writes deliveries.feedbackRating + first-class Review/CSAT record. SLA timer starts at deliveredAt.
  • 9. Deliver invoice PDF. GET /:id/invoice.pdf (pdfkit) attached to the delivery-confirmation email and offered on-screen; WhatsApp gets a link (outbox).

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A5 Document pre-fill (vision, claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger: signed-handover upload registered as documents → grounded inputs: the uploaded object bytes only, allowlist PREFILL_FIELDS (printed name, vin, registration) compared against deliveries.dealId→deals.vehicleId and customers — extracts legible fields only, never invents VIN/reg → output {extraction:{name,vin,registration},confidence} with extractionStatus:'proposed' → HITL: yes, advisor confirms via /documents/extraction/review; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE the extraction is flagged for manual entry, never auto-accepted. Untrusted OCR text wrapped in guardUntrusted. Run recorded to agent_runs; kill-switch agents.status='paused' falls back to manual field entry.
  • A2 Round-robin assign (deterministic, not LLM) → trigger: terminal advance creating the Asset → inputs: dealer_users filtered to Service Advisor role in the delivering division, ordered by lastLeadAssignedAt → output: assets.serviceAdvisorUserId set + lastLeadAssignedAt bumped → HITL: no (deterministic), but reassignable by manager.
  • A6 Call sentiment (fire-and-forget) → not primary here; if a delivery-day call note is logged it scores call_logs.sentiment. Declared, audited, kill-switchable; guardUntrusted on transcript.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Signature-sheet verification banner: "OCR name Ravi Persaud and VIN …4821 match this delivery" — or a red mismatch flag prompting manual review before the advisor can advance.
  • Readiness check (not "Run Review"): inline console shows which of registration/insurance/invoice are green and exactly what is unmet, so the single "Advance to Delivered" CTA is either enabled or explains why.
  • Concierge (SSE, nav-only): "show this customer's other assets / warranty coverage" — dealer-scoped grounding, no mutations.
  • Suggested Service-Advisor intro draft for the CSAT/welcome message — human Approve & Send only (A7 pattern, never auto-send).
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • deliveries.steps jsonb — 11-step, sequential; server enforces order, advance returns 422 unmet[] otherwise.
  • signatureData — required non-empty base64 PNG before signature step closes; deliveredAt — required, must be ≥ registration date, GMT-4.
  • vehicles.registration ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$; vin/engineNumber exactly 17 chars, VIN-locked since commit — immutable at delivery.
  • assets created exactly once (idempotent on deliveryId); serviceAdvisorUserId mandatory on create.
  • SLA: CSAT survey enqueued ≤ 24h from deliveredAt (outbox dedupeKey=delivery:{id}:csat, retry/backoff); invoice PDF delivered same session.
  • Money: invoices stored USD-scale, PDF shows GYD = amount × dealers.usdExchangeRate; tax lines from dealer_taxes (VAT 0/14%, duty) computed server-side — never AI.
  • All mutations carry Idempotency-Key; every write emits timeline_events (isAgent flagged when agent-sourced).
🔀 Edge-of-edge cases → handling
ScenarioDetectionHandlingOwnerHITL?

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Delivery Invoice PDF (pdfkit, GET /:id/invoice.pdf) — Header: dealer logo + name (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division, dealer TIN, invoiceNumber, issue date (GMT-4), status stamp. Bill-to: customer name, account no, address. Vehicle: make/model/trim/year, vin, engineNumber, registration, odometer. Line items (cols: Description · Qty · Unit GYD · Amount GYD): vehicle price, accessories[], trade-in credit (negative), discount. Tax block: VAT (0%/14% per dealer_taxes), import duty, excise — server-computed, EV exclusions noted. Totals: subtotal, tax total, otdPrice, deposit/payments applied, balance = 0. Footer: payment methods received, GYD/USD rate used, "Guyana Revenue Authority" note, terms, advisor signature line. Delivery: attached to confirmation email + WhatsApp link (outbox).
  • Signed Handover / Sales-Order sheet — uploaded scan stored as documents (scope delivery, dealer-ACL object); A5 OCR-verified; carries signatureData + printed name + VIN.
  • Delivery Confirmation email/WhatsApp — HTML template via nodemailer/outbox: vehicle summary, Asset created, assigned Service Advisor name + contact, coverage plan, CSAT survey link (24h SLA).
  • CSAT Survey — first-class Review record writing deliveries.feedbackRating; results feed /dashboard/sentiment digest.

Layer 9 — Cancellation & Refund Sales Manager / Finance

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Initiate cancellation. Sales user opens the deal/booking in the Workflow console and clicks the single secondary CTA Cancel & Refund (the primary CTA is always Advance to {stage}). A modal loads a config-driven cancellation reason picklist (from lead_sources-style admin config; see Key fields). Requires reasonCode + free-text note.
  • 2. Guard note text. The free-text reasonNote and any customer-supplied WhatsApp/email cancel request are passed through guardUntrusted() before persistence and before any AI summarization — untrusted text is never executed as instruction.
  • 3. Refundability check (deterministic, server-side). Server evaluates refundable-until-registration rule: refund is allowed only while the linked deliveries.steps.registration step is NOT completed (i.e., no GRA registration filed / no plate assigned). If registration done → refund path blocked, only goodwill/manual path (Approvals gate) remains.
  • 4. Money gate raised. If any funds were captured (bookings.amountPaid > 0, or deals.depositPaid/payments exist), server creates a gates row type=refund_release in pending, attaching evidence[] (payment ids, receipt ids, invoice status, refundable window computed). No money moves yet.
  • 5. Manager approval (Approvals, not Readiness). Sales Manager opens Gates queue, reviews computed refundable amount + retained fees, then POST /gates/:id/resolve with resolution=approved|rejected. This is the Approvals disambiguation of "Review", distinct from stage Readiness check and AI Approve/Override.
  • 6. Finance executes refund. On gate approved, Finance records the refund via POST /payments as a negative/refund-method ledger entry against the invoice, sent with an idempotency_keys token. Server recomputes invoice status (→ partially_paid/void) and issues a refund receipts row. NO external payment gateway — internal ledger only.
  • 7. Release inventory. Server cancels the reservation: bookings.status=cancelled, clears vehicle soft-lock (vehicles.holdUntil/holdReason nulled) and sets vehicles.status=available (unless another active booking/deal holds it). Deal moves to cancelled; delivery (if auto-created) is voided.
  • 8. Retain history. Lead is NOT deleted — leads.status=cancelled/lost with lostReason; all timeline_events, quotes (superseded), payments, receipts, gate resolution retained for audit. recordAgentRun/audit-logs capture the release.
  • 9. Notify. Notification + customer comms fired: internal notifications/tasks to owner & manager; customer refund confirmation queued to email_logs outbox (email + WhatsApp) with dedupeKey.
  • 10. Multi-interest cancel-one. If the customer had multiple interested vehicles / multiple bookings, only the selected bookingId/dealId is cancelled and released; sibling interests, quotes, and the parent lead remain active.
🔌 APIs & data (request → response)

GET /api/outstanding-balancesquery customerId200 {netDue, refundsPending, retainedFees}invoices, paymentsfinance, sales_manager
⚙️ Agents in play
  • A7 Outreach composer → trigger: cancellation recorded → grounded inputs: leads row, bookings.reasonCode, customers.loyaltyTier/lifetimeValue, retained-fee amount (never the raw money math — server supplies numbers) → output shape {headline, riskLevel, actions[], draftMessage} via GET /leads/:id/agent-brief (e.g. win-back or refund-empathy draft) → HITL: YES — human "Approve & Send" via /outreach or /whatsapp; never auto-sends. Confidence below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → no draft, routed to human.
  • A6 Call sentiment → trigger: if cancellation captured from a call note → grounded inputs: call_logs note text (wrapped in guardUntrusted) → output: sentiment tag on the note → HITL: fire-and-forget + manual /suggest-sentiment; declared, audited via recordAgentRun, kill-switchable via agents.status.
  • No autonomous money agent. Refund amount, retained fees, and tax reversal are computed deterministically server-side from dealer_taxes/ledger — the model NEVER invents refund figures, TIN, VIN, or duty. AI only drafts human-facing prose.
  • Kill-switch + audit. Every agent call here checks isAgentEnabled, PII-minimizes inputs, and writes agent_runs (runType, inputSummary, outputSummary, confidence, latency, status).
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Sales Manager sees an A7 agent-brief on the gate: {headline:"Refund $X, GYD equiv, within window", riskLevel, actions:["Approve refund","Offer alt-vehicle hold","Retain deposit per policy"]} — advisory only; the Approve button is the human gate action.
  • Finance sees a computed refund breakdown card (deposit − retained fees − non-refundable admin) with tax reversal lines from dealer_taxes; AI does not compute these, it only annotates ("VAT reversed on refundable portion").
  • Win-back suggestion: A7 optionally drafts a retention message for high-lifetimeValue/loyaltyTier customers, surfaced as a draft the manager can Approve & Send.
  • All monetary display shows USD-scale value × dealer.usdExchangeRate for GYD; dates in America/Guyana GMT-4.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Cancellation picklist (admin config, versioned): reasonCode ∈ {customer_changed_mind, financing_declined, found_elsewhere, price, delivery_delay, vehicle_defect, duplicate, other}; reasonNote required (max 1000 chars, guardUntrusted).
  • Refundable-until-registration: refund allowed iff deliveries.steps.registration.status != completed AND vehicles.status ∉ {delivered, sold}. Otherwise gate blocked → manual goodwill via separate Approvals path.
  • Gate type=refund_release: evidence[] must include payment ids + invoice status + computedRefund; resolution mandatory; approver role ≥ sales_manager; finance sets approvedAmount ≤ computedRefund.
  • Retained fees: non-refundable admin/reservation fee configurable per dealer; server subtracts before computedRefund. Money stored USD-scale.
  • Idempotency: refund POST /payments requires Idempotency-Key; replay returns original receipt, never double-refunds.
  • Dealer-scope: every query (gates, payments, vehicles, audit-logs, object-storage ACL on refund docs) scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; division axis (CAM/GT) enforced.
  • SLA timers: refund_release gate target resolution ≤ 24h (task + reminder to manager); Finance refund execution ≤ 48h post-approval; customer refund confirmation queued immediately on ledger post. Vehicle soft-lock auto-expiry (lazy) via bookings.expiresAt unaffected — cancel releases immediately.
  • History retention: no hard delete; leads.status=cancelled, quotes → superseded, all timeline_events + audit-logs retained.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Refund Receipt (PDF, pdfkit) — generated on refund ledger post. Delivered via email_logs outbox (email + WhatsApp attachment) and stored in GCS with dealer-scoped per-object ACL. Layout: Header — dealer logo/name (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division, dealer address, receiptNumber, refund date (GMT-4). Parties — customer name/account, original invoiceNumber, related bookingId/dealId, vehicle (make/model/year/VIN). Line items — columns: Description | Original Amount | Retained Fee | Refunded Amount, with tax-reversal rows (VAT/duty per dealer_taxes). Totals — Deposit paid, Less retained fees, Net Refund (USD + GYD @ usdExchangeRate), refund method. Footer — approving manager, gate id/resolution reference, "Internal ledger — no payment gateway", cancellation reasonCode, disclaimer.
  • Cancellation Confirmation letter/email — HTML nodemailer template refund_confirmation; includes vehicle released, reason, refund amount + method, expected settlement window; dedupeKey on outbox.
  • Voided Invoice — original invoices PDF re-stamped VOID/partially_paid watermark; retained for audit, not re-issued to customer unless requested.
  • Gate resolution record — no separate PDF; captured in gates.resolution + timeline_events + audit-logs (approver, amount, timestamp) as immutable history.

Layer 10 — Service handoff & Intro Service Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • Trigger — Asset born on delivery: when POST /deliveries/:id/advance reaches the final 11th step (feedback) it fires the delivered-side side-effects: auto-invoice, assets row created (accountId, vehicleId, dealId, deliveryId, deliveredAt), and A-ServiceAssign stamps assets.serviceAdvisorUserId via deterministic round-robin over dealer_users filtered by role=Service Advisor + matching divisionId (CAM/GT). Every write dealer-scoped to activeDealerId(res).
  • Task materialization: a tasks row "Intro call — {customer}" is created with dueAt = deliveredAt + 5 business days (America/Guyana GMT-4, dealer working-days calendar), assigneeUserId = serviceAdvisorUserId. A timeline_events entry (per-asset, isAgent=false) records the handoff.
  • Advisor opens the Asset/Case console: GET /customers/:id/assets then GET /customers/:id/overview to see lifetimeValue, loyaltyTier, primary contact, prior notes. Concierge grounding here is dealer-scoped.
  • Coverage confirmation: advisor pulls GET /coverage?vehicleId=… to read the warranty / AMC coverage_plans attached to the delivered vehicle (start = deliveredAt, term, mileage cap). If none attached, advisor creates one via POST /coverage (no auto-create — capital/commitment stays human).
  • Intro call (SLA ≤ 5 business days): advisor places call through POST /leads/:id/calls-equivalent on the customer/asset (Twilio browser or Stub adapter). Call note logged to call_logs (provider, sentiment null until scored). Inbound/typed notes wrapped with guardUntrusted before any AI touches them.
  • Sentiment (fire-and-forget): A6 Call-sentiment auto-runs on the saved call note; advisor may re-run manually via POST …/calls/:cid/suggest-sentiment. If A6 confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE the label is withheld and routed to the human (shown as "needs review", never written as fact).
  • Set service cadence: advisor confirms/edits the AI-suggested next-service interval, then persists it — creating the first future service_orders (type=scheduled, status=open, scheduledDate) + a reminder cadence. This is the first-class Case object, not a lead field.
  • Unreachable path: if no answer, advisor marks call outcome=unreachable → task auto-reschedules with backoff (attempt 1 → +1d, 2 → +2d, 3 → +3d), max 3 attempts inside the 5-day window; on exhaustion a WhatsApp/email intro goes out via the email_logs outbox and the task escalates to the reporting manager (dealer_users.reportingManagerUserId).
  • Close handoff: once contacted + cadence set + coverage confirmed, advisor marks the intro task done; timeline_events logs completion and the asset enters normal service lifecycle.
