⚙️ Agents in play
  • A-ServiceAssign (deterministic, not LLM): trigger = delivery final-step Asset creation → inputs: dealer_users (role=Service Advisor, matching assets.vehicleId→division), least-recently-assigned clock → output: sets assets.serviceAdvisorUserId + creates intro tasks row. HITL: no (deterministic, audited to timeline_events). Kill-switch via agents row not required (no LLM) but reversible by manager reassignment.
  • A6 Call-sentiment (claude-sonnet-4-6, autonomous-write, declared + kill-switchable): trigger = save of intro call note (fire-and-forget) + manual /suggest-sentiment → inputs: single call_logs.note only, PII-minimized, guardUntrusted-wrapped → output {sentiment,confidence} written to call_logs.sentiment ONLY if confidence ≥ MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE; else routed to human as "needs review". Every run rows into agent_runs (runType,inputSummary,outputSummary,confidence,latency); paused via agents.status=paused. HITL: soft — advisor can override the label.
  • A15 Pipeline / cadence suggestion (assistive): trigger = advisor opens cadence panel → inputs: coverage_plans term, vehicle make/model service schedule, assets.deliveredAt, odometer → output: proposed next scheduledDate + interval (draft only, never writes service_orders itself). HITL: yes — advisor confirms before POST /service-orders.
  • A7 Outreach composer (draft-only, never auto-send): trigger = unreachable path → GET /leads/:id/agent-brief-style brief returns {headline,riskLevel,actions[],draftMessage} for the intro WhatsApp/email → advisor "Approve & Send" via /whatsapp or /emails/send. HITL: yes — hard gate, no autonomous send.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • "Suggested first-service cadence: 6 months / 5,000 km for this Toyota trim — confirm to schedule." (A15 draft; advisor edits date, then persists as a Case).
  • Call-outcome sentiment chip on the intro call note: Positive / Neutral / Negative — shown only above confidence floor; otherwise "AI unsure — review".
  • Drafted intro message for the unreachable customer (A7): editable text, WhatsApp-first if opted-in, with coverage highlights — advisor approves before send.
  • Coverage expiry watch: "Warranty ends {endsAt}; AMC renewal opportunity" surfaced from coverage_plans, dealer-scoped.
  • Concierge (SSE /anthropic, nav-only) can answer "what's covered on this asset?" using dealer-scoped grounding — read-only, no mutations.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Assignment: assets.serviceAdvisorUserId must resolve to an active dealer_users with role Service Advisor + same divisionId as the vehicle; else fallback to manager queue.
  • Intro SLA: intro contact due deliveredAt + 5 business days (America/Guyana GMT-4, dealer calendar excludes weekends/holidays). Breach = task turns red + escalation to reportingManagerUserId.
  • Retry policy (unreachable): max 3 call attempts, backoff +1d/+2d/+3d, all inside the 5-day window; on exhaustion → outbox message + escalation.
  • Coverage: coverage_plans.startsAt = deliveredAt; endsAt > startsAt; no overlapping active plan per vehicleId; type ∈ {warranty, AMC}; money USD-scale, GYD display = ×dealer.usdExchangeRate.
  • Service order: scheduledDate > now; odometer integer ≥ 0; type=scheduled for cadence; VIN/registration on vehicle already locked from delivery.
  • Confidence: A6/A15 outputs gated by MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE — below floor never written, routed to human.
  • Guarding: every call note, inbound WhatsApp/email reply, and customer-typed text passes guardUntrusted before AI; PII-minimized before any model call.
  • Dealer-scope: all reads/writes filtered by activeDealerId(res) incl. concierge grounding, object-storage ACL, and audit-logs.
  • Config touched: dealer working-days calendar; dealer_taxes only for coverage pricing tax lines; outbox retry/backoff/dedupeKey for reminders.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Coverage Plan Certificate (pdfkit): generated on POST /coverage. Header — dealer logo, dealer name + division (CAM/GT), "Coverage Certificate", plan number. Vehicle block — make/model/trim/year, VIN, engineNumber, registration. Coverage block — type (Warranty/AMC), startsAt/endsAt, mileage cap, inclusions/exclusions. Totals — plan price USD + GYD (×usdExchangeRate), tax lines from dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14% by model, EV exclusions). Footer — GRA-compliant note (GYD), terms, advisor name, issue date (GMT-4). Delivery: email + WhatsApp via outbox.
  • Service Reminder / Intro message (template, not PDF): HTML email template + WhatsApp text via email_logs outbox, dedupeKey per send. Fields: customer name, vehicle, advisor name + direct line, next scheduled service date, coverage highlights. A7-drafted, human-approved.
  • Service Order confirmation (pdfkit, optional): on POST /service-orders — header (dealer/division, order number), customer + vehicle block, scheduled date/time, complaint/type, odometer, assigned advisor, jobs[] placeholder. Delivered on request via /documents/:id/download (presigned GCS, per-object ACL, dealer-scoped).
  • Handoff timeline (system, no PDF): timeline_events entries — asset created, advisor assigned, intro attempts, cadence set — viewable in console, exportable via /reports.

Layer 11 — Service: Scheduled Service Advisor · Manager · Technician

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Cadence reminder fires. POST /api/service-orders/remind (cron-triggered lazy-eval) scans coverage_plans (AMC/warranty due dates) + assets.deliveredAt + last service_orders.scheduledDate per asset, all filtered by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id. Emits an OUTBOX row per due asset via email_logs (email + WhatsApp), dedupeKey = svc-remind:{assetId}:{dueMonth} so a reminder never double-sends. Agent brief (A7 Outreach) may draft copy — never auto-sends.
  • 2. Validate contact + vehicle identity. On POST /api/service-orders the server re-validates customers.email (RFC + MX-soft), vehicles.vin = 17 chars, vehicles.registration matches ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$, and that the vehicle is an assets row owned by this dealer/customer. Mismatch → 422 before any Case is opened.
  • 3. Open the Case (first-class service_orders). Creates a service_orders row (type maintenance|repair|recall|comeback, status=open, scheduledDate, complaint [wrapped in guardUntrusted], odometer). Emits timeline_events on the asset.
  • 4. Manager review gate. Cases flagged capital/recall/goodwill raise a gates row (type=stage_advance for readiness, manager-owned). Advance is the ONE primary CTA "Advance to Scheduled"; POST /api/service-orders/:id/advance returns 422 {unmet:[]} against versioned stage_checklists if gates unmet.
  • 5. Open the Work Order (job_cards). POST /api/job-cards — enforce exactly one active (open|in_progress) job_card per asset; second attempt → 409. Seeds checklist[] from service_orders.jobs[], sets laborHours=0, status=open.
  • 6. Appointment. Assign technician (/service-technicians availability), bay, duration; write to bookings-style slot + tasks/notifications. 24h-before reminder queued to outbox (dedupeKey appt-remind:{jobCardId}).
  • 7. Intake checklist. Technician records odometer-in, fuel, loaner issued, damage walk-around (photos → customer_documents/documents), customer signature via signatureData-style capture. PDI-style items stored in job_cards.checklist[].
  • 8. Parts. POST /api/job-cards/:id/parts issues job_card_parts against parts.stock; short stock → backorder (create part_purchases to suppliers) or supersession (linked SKU); Case moves to on-hold if awaiting parts.
  • 9. Quote → approve. POST /api/job-cards/:id computes labor+parts+tax (GRA VAT via dealer_taxes, server-side deterministic). Customer approval (WhatsApp/email link) or gates. Pay type set: warranty | customer | goodwill | rectify.
  • 10. Repair → outtake. status=in_progress→completed, actual laborHours logged, QC outtake checklist, unused parts returned to stock (job_card_parts return).
  • 11. Service invoice → pickup. POST /api/job-cards/:id/invoice → service_invoices; /payments (idempotent) recompute + receipts. Pickup signature, loaner return, sublet reconciliation, then Case status=closed and appended to Service History (assets timeline).

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A7 Outreach composer → trigger: service reminder due / on-hold parts arrival → grounded inputs: service_orders, coverage_plans, assets.deliveredAt, last service_invoices (dealer-scoped) → output {headline,riskLevel,actions[],draftMessage} via GET /leads/:id/agent-brief equivalent → HITL: YES, human "Approve & Send" through /outreach or /whatsapp; never auto-sends. Respects MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE (below floor → route to advisor, no draft surfaced as ready).
  • A6 Call sentiment → trigger: advisor logs a call_logs/comm note on the Case (fire-and-forget) + manual POST /leads/:id/calls/:cid/suggest-sentiment → input: guarded call transcript/note → output {sentiment,confidence} on call_logs → autonomous-write, therefore declared in agents, audited in agent_runs, kill-switchable (isAgentEnabled). HITL: advisor can override.
  • A2 Round-robin (deterministic, not LLM) → trigger: Case needs an advisor → picks by dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt within divisionId → output: serviceAdvisorUserId. HITL: manager reassign.
  • A3 Quote/Code (deterministic tax engine) → trigger: job_card quote build → inputs: job_card_parts, laborHours, dealer_taxes (GRA VAT 0%/14%, duty, EV exclusions) → output: pdfkit service quote PDF + taxLines[]. No LLM; server computes tax deterministically.
  • A5 Document pre-fill (vision, HITL) → trigger: uploaded warranty claim / recall notice → allowlist PREFILL_FIELDS, extractionStatus:'proposed' → confirm via /documents/extraction/review. Never invents VIN/coverage.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Service Advisor: A7 draft reminder + "next-service" upsell suggestion grounded in real coverage/history; sentiment flag on inbound WhatsApp complaint (guarded) to prioritize the queue.
  • Manager: pipeline/readiness surface for capital-repair & recall Cases with confidence and unmet-gate list before approving "Advance"; disambiguated as Readiness check (stage), Approvals (manager gate), Approve/Override run (AI).
  • Technician: A5 pre-fill of warranty claim fields (proposed, human-confirmed); supersession suggestion when SKU out of stock (validated against parts, no fabricated SKUs).
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Identity validations: vin/engineNumber = 17 chars; registration ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$; email RFC; odometer must be ≥ last recorded odometer for the asset (non-decreasing) else 422.
  • Case fields: service_orders.{type,status open→in_progress→completed/closed/on-hold,scheduledDate,complaint(guarded),odometer,jobs[],payType warranty|customer|goodwill|rectify,serviceAdvisorUserId}.
  • Work order: job_cards.{checklist[],laborHours,status} — invariant: one active job_card per asset.
  • Money: stored USD-scale; GYD display = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate; tax from dealer_taxes (VAT/duty/excise, percent|fixed, threshold, excludeEv); rectify/goodwill lines = 0 to customer.
  • SLA timers: cadence reminder T-14/T-7 days pre-scheduledDate; appointment reminder 24h before; on-hold parts escalation to manager if backorder ETA > 5 business days; quote-approval SLA 48h then Case auto-parks (not cancels).
  • Config touched: versioned stage_checklists (service variant gates), dealer_taxes, coverage_plans. Dates in America/Guyana GMT-4.
  • Guardrails: every query activeDealerId-scoped incl. audit-logs & object-storage per-object ACL; all untrusted text (complaint, inbound WhatsApp/email, uploaded docs) via guardUntrusted; money mutations use idempotency_keys.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Service Repair Order / Estimate PDF (generator: pdfkit, deterministic A3 engine; channel: WhatsApp + email outbox for approval). Layout — Header: dealer logo, division (CAM/GT), Case/RO number, date (GMT-4), advisor. Vehicle block: make/model/year, VIN, registration, odometer-in. Customer block: name, contact. Line items table columns: Op/Description · Type(labor|part|sublet) · Qty · Unit(GYD) · Amount(GYD) · PayType(warranty/customer/goodwill/rectify). Totals: Labor subtotal · Parts subtotal · Sublet · taxLines[] (GRA VAT 0%/14%, duty) · Grand total GYD (= USD × usdExchangeRate). Footer: validUntil, customer approval signature line, disclaimer.
  • Intake / walk-around sheet (checklist + signatureData capture; stored to documents/object storage with per-object ACL). Fields: fuel level, damage map, loaner issued Y/N, customer authorization signature.
  • Service Invoice PDF (generator: pdfkit; entity service_invoices; channel: email outbox + pickup print). Layout mirrors estimate with invoiceNumber, status(issued|partially_paid|paid), payments applied, receipt reference, outstanding balance. Only customer-payable lines charged; warranty/goodwill/rectify shown at 0.
  • Payment receipt (receipts): receipt no, method (cash|card|bank_transfer|cheque|mobile_money|financing), amount GYD, invoice ref, idempotency-safe.
  • Service History record (appended to assets timeline, no PDF): Case number, date, odometer, jobs, parts SKUs, payType, total — feeds next cadence reminder and resale value.

Layer 12 — Service: Unscheduled Service Advisor

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)
  • 1. Inbound contact — customer calls/walks-in/WhatsApps a breakdown or accident. Advisor identifies the customers record (or creates person account) and the owned vehicles row via assets (accountId+vehicleId+deliveredAt) — all lookups scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id and division (CAM/GT). If the caller's asset belongs to another dealer → cross-tenant block (see edges).
  • 2. Open the Case — POST /api/service-orders with type:'unscheduled', complaint, odometer, vehicleId, customerId. Case = first-class service_orders row, status:'open', scheduledDate nullable (unscheduled/immediate). complaint is customer-supplied untrusted text → wrapped with guardUntrusted before any AI touches it.
  • 3. Triage on call — advisor triages severity + off-road urgency. AI Triage assist (A-service triage, sonnet-4-6) reads guarded complaint + vehicle model/mileage history and returns a {severity, likelyJobs[], safetyFlag, draftDisposition} suggestion — advisory only, human decides. Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → no suggestion shown, pure manual triage.
  • 4a. Resolve → close — trivial (reset/advice/no-fault). Advisor logs resolution in customer_notes/comm_notes, sets service_orders.status:'completed' (resolution='advised', no work order, no invoice). Timeline event emitted.
  • 4b. Escalate → Work Order — needs shop work. Advisor creates a job_cards row (WORK ORDER) via POST /api/job-cards linked to the service_order, status:'open', seeds checklist[] from likelyJobs, assigns technician (GET /api/service-technicians). Advances open→in_progress→completed on the WORK ORDER layer.
  • 5. Accident branch → Claim — if accident, advisor records incident, uploads police report / photos (customer_documents, extraction 'proposed' via A5 vision, HITL confirm). Coverage checked against coverage_plans (warranty/AMC/insurance). A gate type:'refund_release'… no — the money-affecting decision is the insurance claim: advisor files claim, and a gates row is raised for the estimate approval before capital work begins.
  • 6. Claim approved — insurer/coverage covers work → job_card labor+parts billed to insurer via service_invoices (payer=insurer); customer deductible only.
  • 7. Claim rejected → customer-pay — advisor flips payer to customer, re-quotes OTD service estimate, requires customer authorization before parts issue. service_invoices payer=customer, standard payments ledger (idempotent).
  • 8. Off-road urgency — safetyFlag:true (unsafe to drive) escalates SLA to same-day, flags towing/loaner task (tasks), notifies reportingManager, and locks vehicle status to service.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A-Service Triage assist (claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger: Case opened / advisor clicks "Suggest triage" → grounded inputs: guarded service_orders.complaint, odometer, vehicles make/model/year, prior service_orders/job_cards for this vehicleId (all dealer-scoped) → output: {severity:'low|med|high',safetyFlag,likelyJobs[],draftDisposition,confidence} → HITL: yes — advisory chip only, never mutates the Case; confidence<MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE ⇒ suppressed, routed to manual. Kill-switch via agents.status; every call recordAgentRun into agent_runs (runType='service_triage').
  • A5 Document pre-fill (vision) → trigger: police report / accident photo / insurance form uploaded → inputs: customer_documents object bytes (per-object ACL, dealer-scoped) → output: allowlisted PREFILL_FIELDS only (claimRef, incidentDate, plate matched vs vehicles.registration), extractionStatus:'proposed' → HITL: yes — advisor confirms via /documents/extraction/review; model NEVER invents VIN/TIN/policy numbers.
  • A6 Call sentiment → trigger: advisor logs a call note on the Case (fire-and-forget) or manual /suggest-sentiment → inputs: guarded call_logs transcript/note → output: {sentiment,confidence} written to call_logs.sentiment → HITL: no autonomous-write, but declared + audited + kill-switchable (agent row, agent_runs).
  • A7 Outreach composer → trigger: advisor wants to notify customer (claim status / off-road loaner) → GET /leads|customers agent-brief-style draft → output: {draftMessage} → HITL: yes — human "Approve & Send" through email/WhatsApp outbox; never auto-sends.
  • Note: technician routing is deterministic (open-card load + skill match), not LLM.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Triage chip on the Case console: "Likely brake caliper + rotor — HIGH, unsafe to drive" with confidence bar; advisor accepts to pre-seed checklist[] or dismisses.
  • Coverage hint: "Under AMC until 2027-03 — labor covered, parts 80%" derived deterministically from coverage_plans (not model-invented).
  • Draft customer message ("Your vehicle is unsafe to drive; we've arranged a loaner") pre-composed for one-tap Approve & Send.
  • Sentiment flag on the call so a frustrated accident customer is surfaced to the reporting manager.
  • Extraction review panel: proposed claim fields highlighted for confirm/edit; nothing auto-committed.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • service_orders: type='unscheduled', complaint (required, guardUntrusted), odometer (int ≥0, must ≥ last recorded odometer for that vehicle else 422), status open→in_progress→completed, stageEnteredAt.
  • vehicles.status flips to service when a WORK ORDER opens; back to available/owner-asset state on close.
  • Money: service estimate/invoice stored USD-scale, displayed GYD = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate; taxes computed SERVER-side from dealer_taxes (VAT 0%/14% by model, EV excluded) — never AI-derived. GRA filing (if applicable) uses gra_filing gate before any submission.
  • Gate type: estimate/capital work > threshold or insurer-billed ⇒ approval gate with evidence[]; below-floor pricing on customer-pay re-quote reuses same discount discipline.
  • Idempotency: payments require Idempotency-Key; recompute invoice + emit receipt.
  • SLA timers: off-road/safetyFlag = same-day response; standard unscheduled triage acknowledge ≤2h; parts backorder auto-task + customer notice; claim decision follow-up reminder via /service-orders/:id/remind (outbox retry/backoff/dedupeKey). Dates America/Guyana GMT-4.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Service Estimate / Work Order sheet (pdfkit) — delivered via email + WhatsApp outbox. Layout: Header dealer logo, division (CAM/GT), Case# (service_orders.id), date (GMT-4), customer + vehicle block (make/model/year, VIN, registration, odometer). Complaint section (guarded text). Line-item table columns: Item | Type (labor/part) | Qty | Unit (GYD) | Amount (GYD). Totals: Subtotal, VAT (0%/14% server-computed from dealer_taxes), Total OTD in GYD (USD×usdExchangeRate). Footer: authorization signature line, validUntil, advisor name, "estimate — approval required before work".
  • Service Invoice (pdfkit / service_invoices) — same skeleton, adds invoiceNumber, payer (insurer/customer/split-deductible), payment status, receipt reference. Split-payer invoices show two totals blocks (insurer-covered vs customer-due).
  • Insurance Claim packet — consumed docs: police report + accident photos (customer_documents, A5-extracted proposed fields, HITL confirmed). No AI-fabricated claim/policy/VIN numbers; only legible extracted + manually entered fields submitted with gate evidence[].
  • Customer notice (off-road/loaner/backorder/claim-decision) — A7-drafted, human-approved, sent via WhatsApp (Meta/Twilio auto-select) or Gmail SMTP template through the retry/backoff/dedupe outbox.

Layer 13 — Other Case types Service Advisor / Manager

🚦 Flow (step-by-step, lowest level)

Every case here is a first-class service_orders row (the corrected Case entity), dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id, division-tagged CAM/GT. Three case types share one console; the CTA is always the single 'Advance to {stage}' — no duplicate "Run Review".

  1. Case intake & classify
    • Inbound arrives (post-delivery CSAT prompt reply, customer WhatsApp/email, phone note, or advisor manual open). Untrusted body is wrapped in guardUntrusted() before any AI or persistence.
    • A16 Case Classifier (claude-sonnet-4-6) fires: kill-switch isAgentEnabled('case_classifier') → PII-minimize → classify into type ∈ {feedback, profile_update, escalation} + sub-reason + suggested priority. Result stored on agent_runs with confidence.
    • Gate: if confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → no auto-route; case lands in an "Unclassified — needs triage" queue for the advisor. Above floor → pre-fills service_orders.type + a timeline_events entry (isAgent=true).
  2. Feedback / CSAT (first-class Review entity — corrected; not just delivery rating)
    • Post-delivery/post-service worker sends CSAT ask via outbox (email_logs). Reply captured → creates/updates a Review record with feedbackRating (1–5), verbatim comment (guarded), source ∈ {delivery, service, standalone}, linked dealId/serviceOrderId.
    • A6-style sentiment scoring runs fire-and-forget on the comment. Advance stages: received → acknowledged → resolved. Rating ≤ 2 (detractor) auto-raises priority and can spawn a linked escalation case.
  3. Profile Update / Info Change
    • Advisor opens case with reason ∈ {relocated, correction, other}, target customers/contacts row, and proposed field deltas (address, phone, email, TIN, loyaltyTier note).
    • Field-level RBAC enforced via role_field_permissions (hidden|view|edit). If any changed field is edit-gated for the actor → 403. Supporting doc (proof of address) uploaded to customer_documents via presigned URL (per-object ACL, dealer-scoped).
    • Advance: draft → applied. On apply, a diff snapshot goes to audit-logs (dealer-scoped) and timeline_events; customer 360 (lifetimeValue, relations) reflects change.
  4. Escalation with SLA
    • Case opened (or auto-spawned from detractor CSAT) with severity → SLA timer slaDueAt computed from dealer config. Assigned via manager or round-robin among service advisors.
    • Advance: open → in_progress → on_hold → resolved → closed. Breach of slaDueAt notifies reportingManagerUserId and flips a breached flag. Closing requires resolution text + (for compensation/refund) a manager Approvals gate.
  5. Advance guard (all types): POST /service-orders/:id/advance checks config-driven versioned stage_checklists; unmet gates return 422 {unmet:[]}.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A16 Case Classifier (claude-sonnet-4-6, autonomous-write → declared + audited + kill-switchable) → trigger: new inbound case body / manual open → inputs: guarded body text + customers context (PII-minimized), NOT raw PII → output: {type, subReason, suggestedPriority, confidence} → HITL: yes — below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE routes to triage queue; above floor pre-fills but advisor confirms on advance; every run in agent_runs + timeline_events(isAgent=true).
  • A6 Call/Comment Sentiment → trigger: fire-and-forget on CSAT comment or comm_notes, plus manual /suggest-sentiment → inputs: guarded comment text → output: {sentiment, score} stored on review/note → HITL: no auto-action; advisor sees the label only.
  • A7 Outreach Composer → trigger: GET /leads/:id/agent-brief-style brief adapted for a detractor follow-up → inputs: review rating + history → output: {headline, riskLevel, actions[], draftMessage} → HITL: yes — never auto-sends; human 'Approve & Send' via /outreach or /whatsapp (outbox).
  • Sentiment digest → GET /dashboard/sentiment — deterministic aggregation over ≤40 real dealer-scoped notes/reviews; no fabrication.

No agent invents customer field values on a profile_update; A16 only classifies/routes. Any field write is human-applied under field-perm RBAC.

🧠 AI for the persona
  • Suggested case type + reason chip with a visible confidence badge; below-floor cases explicitly say "needs your triage" rather than guessing.
  • Sentiment label on each CSAT comment; detractor (rating ≤ 2) surfaced with a "one-click open escalation" action.
  • Draft follow-up message (A7) for detractors — advisor edits then approves; nothing sends silently.
  • On profile_update, AI vision on proof-of-address extracts only legible fields (extractionStatus:'proposed', allowlist PREFILL_FIELDS); advisor confirms via /documents/extraction/review. Never auto-writes address/TIN.
  • Manager sees a sentiment digest and SLA-breach risk list, dealer-scoped only.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • service_orders: type(feedback|profile_update|escalation), reason(relocated|correction|other for profile; severity for escalation), status, priority, assignedTo, slaDueAt, breached, stageEnteredAt, complaint, divisionId.
  • Review (first-class): feedbackRating 1–5 integer (reject out-of-range), comment (guarded, length-capped), sentiment, source, one active review per delivery/service order.
  • Profile deltas: phone/email format-validated; TIN edit gated by role_field_permissions; registration ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$ if a linked vehicle reg is corrected; address change with reason:relocated requires proof doc.
  • SLA timers (dealer config): P1 escalation 4h, P2 24h, P3 72h; CSAT ack ≤ 24h; timers in America/Guyana GMT-4. Breach → notify reportingManagerUserId, set breached=true, push notifications.
  • Config touched: versioned stage_checklists per case type (e.g. escalation close needs resolution + closed manager gate); MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor; kill-switch via agents.status.
  • Money on compensation/refund is USD-scale, GYD display = amount × dealer.usdExchangeRate; refund release routes through gates(type:refund_release) + idempotency_keys.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • CSAT / Feedback request — generator: HTML email template via nodemailer + WhatsApp template; delivery: email_logs outbox (retry/backoff/dedupeKey). Content: dealer header, greeting, 1–5 rating scale link, single free-text field, opt-out line.
  • Case summary PDF — generator: pdfkit; delivery: download / email attachment. Layout: Header dealer name+division+logo, case #, type, opened date (GMT-4). Section 1 Customer name, account type, contact. Section 2 Case detail reason, complaint (guarded), assigned advisor, SLA due/breached. Section 3 Timeline table columns: Timestamp | Actor | Agent? | Event. Section 4 Resolution outcome text + any gate reference. Footer generated-at, dealer TIN, page n/of, "Amounts in GYD (rate ×usdExchangeRate)".
  • Profile change record — generator: pdfkit; layout: Header customer + reason. Diff table columns: Field | Before | After | Authorized by. Footer audit-log id, applied timestamp. Attached proof-of-address doc referenced by customer_documents id.
  • Compensation/refund voucher (escalation only) — generator: pdfkit; produced only after gates(refund_release) resolved. Layout: header, amount USD + GYD display, method, gate approver, idempotency_keys ref, footer disclaimer (internal ledger only — no payment gateway).

Cross-cutting Modules & Reference

Module A — Inventory Management Inventory / Sales Manager

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • Every read/write is dealer-scoped. Manager authenticates (Clerk) → auth/me resolves users.clerkId → dealer_users(activeDealerId, divisionId, isGeneralManager). Middleware sets activeDealerId(res) and requires header x-dealer-id; the two MUST match or 403. Every Drizzle query on vehicles, dealer_taxes, part_purchases, storage objects and audit-logs carries where dealerId = activeDealerId — no cross-dealer bleed (CAM vs GT).
  • Browse/filter stock: GET /api/vehicles?status=&make=&model=&year=&powertrain=&division=&q= → paged list scoped to dealer. Non-GM managers additionally filtered/soft-flagged by their divisionId (CAM/GT) unless role grants cross-division view. Field visibility applies role_field_permissions (e.g. cost, documents[] may be hidden|view|edit).
  • Create vehicle: Manager opens New Vehicle → enters make, model, trim, year, vin, engineNumber, registration, price(USD-scale), powertrain, division. Client shows GYD preview = price × dealer.usdExchangeRate(~209) (display only; stored USD-scale). On blur of VIN/engine → decode+validate (17-char, checksum region). POST /api/vehicles → 201 with status='available' default. Duplicate (dealerId, vin) → 409.
  • Photo/360 video upload: Client calls POST /api/storage/uploads/request-url {contentType, ext, purpose:'vehicle-image'} → server returns short-TTL presigned PUT URL (GCS via Replit sidecar) with object path namespaced dealers/{dealerId}/vehicles/{vehicleId}/…. Browser PUTs bytes direct to GCS. Server writes ACL row (dealer-scoped, module='vehicles') and appends the object key to vehicles.images[] (or a 360 documents[] entry) via PATCH /api/vehicles/:id. Reads go through GET /api/storage/objects/* which re-checks dealer ACL before signing a GET URL.
  • Bulk import: Manager downloads GET /api/vehicles/import/template (ExcelJS .xlsx with typed columns + data-validation dropdowns for powertrain/status/division). Fills, uploads via POST /api/vehicles/import (multipart). Server parses row-by-row, validates, upserts by (dealerId, vin), returns per-row result report {inserted, updated, skipped, errors[{row, field, msg}]}. All-or-nothing per row; malformed rows reported, never silently dropped.
  • Pricing master: Manager maintains dealer_taxes via admin/taxes (VAT 0%/14%, import duty, excise/levy, EV battery duty, threshold, excludeEv). Price shown on a vehicle detail composes server-computed line items — client never computes tax. Same deterministic engine feeds quotes/deals (Agent A3).
  • Status machine + soft-lock: Manager changes status through validated transitions only (VEHICLE_STATUS_TRANSITIONS). Placing a hold sets holdUntil+holdReason (soft-lock: still visible, not bookable). Deal commit hard-locks via VIN allocation (Agent A11) → reserved/booked. Delivery advance → delivered→sold.
  • Book-from-stock: From an available vehicle → Book → creates bookings row (vehicleId, bookingAmount, expiresAt), flips vehicle available→reserved atomically. Expiry worker reverts reserved→available when bookings.status='expired'.
  • Stock-mix / reorder / remarketing: Manager opens Inventory dashboard tiles fed by GET /api/dashboard/inventory-breakdown and GET /api/pipeline/suggestions (Agent A15) → surfaces slow-movers (aged stock), reorder gaps, disposal candidates. Disposal = status transition to a terminal/remarket state + audit; capital re-order raises a gates(type=capital_order) approval.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A15 Pipeline / Stock-mix suggestions → trigger: dashboard load or scheduled → grounded inputs: dealer-scoped vehicles (age = now − createdAt, status, powertrain, price), historical sales velocity, reorderLevel patterns → output: ranked slow-mover/reorder/disposal suggestions written to agent_runs (confidence, latency) → HITL: advisory only; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE suppressed to human; capital re-order acted on only via gates(capital_order) approval. Kill-switch isAgentEnabled + recordAgentRun.
  • A11 VIN allocation → trigger: deal commit (Module: Deals) → deterministic pick of a specific vehicles.vin from available stock of selectedModel, hard-locks available→reserved/booked → output: VIN-locked deal + timeline_events → HITL: none (deterministic), but writes audited; conflict → gate. Shown here because it consumes/mutates inventory status.
  • A3 Quote/Code (deterministic tax) → not inventory-owned but reads vehicles.price + dealer_taxes to compose server-side pricing; listed so managers know the price shown on a vehicle uses the SAME deterministic engine (no AI invents tax).
  • GRA vision extract (/api/gra/extract) → if a manager uploads an import/customs doc against a vehicle, AI extracts legible fields only (year/make/model/mileage) → SERVER computes duty from dealer_taxes → human gates(gra_filing) confirms. AI NEVER invents TIN/VIN/CIF/duty; untrusted OCR text wrapped with guardUntrusted; below confidence → human. All under kill-switch + recordAgentRun.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Stock health at a glance: A15 flags aged units, over/under-stocked segments (e.g. EV vs ICE mix), and reorder gaps vs parts.reorderLevel-style thresholds — always dealer- and division-scoped, always with a confidence badge; low-confidence items are hidden, not guessed.
  • Disposal/remarketing hints: Suggested remarket candidates ranked by days-in-stock and margin pressure; the manager decides — action requires a status transition + (for capital) an approval gate.
  • VIN/engine decode assist: Decode is deterministic validation (17-char, region/checksum), not generative — surfaces make/model/year hints for confirmation; the human accepts. AI never overwrites a manually entered VIN.
  • Guardrails: Every AI touch obeys isAgentEnabled kill-switch, is audited via recordAgentRun, PII-minimized, and any external/OCR text passes guardUntrusted. Outputs below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE route to a human.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • VIN & engineNumber: exactly 17 chars, uppercase alphanumeric, no I/O/Q; VIN unique per (dealerId, vin). 422 on format, 409 on duplicate.
  • registration: ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$ (Guyana plates), unique per dealer where set.
  • price: stored USD-scale (integer minor units); GYD display = price × dealer.usdExchangeRate(~209), computed at render, never persisted in GYD. Dates America/Guyana (GMT-4) incl. holdUntil, bookings.expiresAt.
  • powertrain: enum (ICE/hybrid/EV…); drives EV duty exclusion (dealer_taxes.excludeEv / battery duty).
  • status: available|reserved|booked|delivered|in_transit|sold|service. Transitions enforced by VEHICLE_STATUS_TRANSITIONS; illegal jumps → 422. holdUntil/holdReason = soft-lock (visible, unbookable); deal-commit VIN-lock = hard-lock.
  • divisionId: CAM/GT tag; non-GM managers scoped to their division unless role permits cross-division.
  • images[] / documents[] / accessories[]: JSON arrays of GCS object keys / structured items; keys namespaced dealers/{dealerId}/…; served only via ACL-checked signed URLs.
  • Tax composition: deterministic, server-side from dealer_taxes (VAT 0/14%, import duty, excise/levy, EV exclusion, threshold). Client sends none; GRA = Guyana Revenue Authority (GYD).
  • SLA: presigned URL TTL short (~minutes); import parse target < a few seconds for typical dealer sheet; A15 suggestions refreshed on dashboard load / scheduled; booking expiry worker reverts stock promptly after expiresAt.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Import template (.xlsx, ExcelJS): single sheet, header row with typed columns (make, model, trim, year, vin, engineNumber, registration, price(USD), powertrain, divisionId, accessories) + data-validation dropdowns for powertrain/division/status; example row + notes row; returned as attachment from /vehicles/import/template.
  • Import result report: JSON {inserted, updated, skipped, errors[{row,field,msg}]} rendered as a table in UI; downloadable.
  • Vehicle media: images[] (JPEG/PNG) + optional 360 walkaround video in documents[], all stored in GCS under dealers/{dealerId}/vehicles/{vehicleId}/…, served via ACL-checked signed URLs only.
  • Customs/import docs: polymorphic documents rows (target=vehicle) with extraction jsonb + extractionStatus; GRA vision extract populates legible fields for human confirmation.
  • No inventory-specific PDF is generated here — pricing feeds pdfkit quote/delivery PDFs downstream (Modules Deals/Delivery); tax lines on those PDFs come from the same deterministic dealer_taxes engine, in GYD with USD-scale source.

Module B — Import & Logistics + GRA Duty Finance / Customs

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • 1. Capital / factory order raised — Finance opens a gates row type=capital_order (evidence: proforma invoice, distributor PO). Blocks any USD-scale commitment above dealer floor until resolved. All rows scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; division = CAM/GT on the order.
  • 2. Vehicle records created / imported — POST /api/vehicles/import (xlsx via /import/template) or per-unit POST /api/vehicles with status=in_transit, make/model/trim/year/powertrain, empty vin/engineNumber until manifest confirmed. price stored USD-scale.
  • 3. Freight & landed-cost accrual — Finance appends CIF components (FOB + freight + insurance) as vehicle documents[] / accessory-cost lines. Server never invents CIF; all figures keyed from uploaded shipping docs.
  • 4. Dual-currency landed cost — every stored amount is USD-scale; GYD display = USD × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209). Rate snapshotted onto the clearance pack at compute-time so later rate drift does not retro-change a filed duty.
  • 5. GRA duty extraction (AI vision, legible-only) — POST /api/gra/extract reads customs/manifest/proforma images and returns only fields it can legibly read (make/model/year/CIF-as-printed/engine cc/fuel type). It NEVER computes duty and NEVER fabricates TIN/VIN/CIF. Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → field dropped, flagged for human key-in. All extracted text wrapped in guardUntrusted.
  • 6. Server-side deterministic tax compute — server computes VAT (0% or 14%), import duty, excise/levy strictly from dealer_taxes (kind VAT|duty|excise|levy, percent|fixed, threshold, excludeEv) against the human-confirmed CIF/GYD base. EV powertrain → excludeEv duty lines skipped. No AI arithmetic anywhere in this step.
  • 7. Human gate — GRA filing confirm — a gates row type=gra_filing holds the computed duty sheet + source docs in evidence[]. Finance/Customs reviews line-by-line, corrects any mis-read field, then POST /api/gates/:id/resolve. Only a resolved gate unlocks the filing.
  • 8. File to GRA — POST /api/gra/filings persists the confirmed, server-computed filing (immutable snapshot: CIF, exchangeRate, per-tax lines, EV flag, filedBy, filedAt). timeline_events (domain=finance) + audit-logs written, dealer-scoped.
  • 9. Clearance pack assembled — duty sheet + proforma + manifest + bill of lading + GRA filing reference bundled as polymorphic documents against the vehicle; generates a printable clearance/landed-cost PDF.
  • 10. Registration & plates (3rd-party status) — registration handled by external agency; tracked only as a status field on the delivery step (registration) + vehicles.registration validated ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$. No live GRA/agency integration — manual status transitions.
  • 11. Delivery-readiness handoff — once duty filed + registration status=complete, vehicle status flips in_transit → available; the linked deliveries 11-step (sales_order→pdi_checklist→registration→insurance→invoice→…) can advance its registration/insurance steps. VIN/engineNumber must be present (17 chars) before available.

⚙️ Agents in play
  • GRA vision extract (claude-sonnet-4-6) → trigger: POST /api/gra/extract on customs/manifest/proforma image → grounded inputs: only the uploaded document pixels; dealer context for scoping → output: fields{} of legibly-read values + per-field confidence; explicitly forbidden from computing duty or inventing TIN/VIN/CIF — missing/illegible ⇒ field omitted, never guessed → HITL: mandatory gra_filing gate; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE field routed to human key-in. Kill-switch isAgentEnabled; every call recordAgentRun (agent_runs: runType, input/outputSummary, confidence, latency); PII-minimized; output wrapped in guardUntrusted before any downstream use.
  • A5 Doc pre-fill (vision, allowlist PREFILL_FIELDS) — may pre-populate benign clearance-doc metadata only; HITL via /documents/extraction/review. No tax fields.
  • Deterministic tax engine (NOT an LLM) — server module computes all duty/VAT/excise from dealer_taxes; declared as computation, not an agent, precisely so no AI touches money math. Idempotent compute; result diffed against any client-supplied taxLines[] → 422 on mismatch.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Read, don't reckon: vision extracts what the Customs officer can already read — CIF as printed, engine cc, fuel type, model year — to save keystrokes, never to derive taxable value.
  • Confidence-gated: any field under MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE shows blank + "confirm" prompt to Finance, never a low-trust auto-fill.
  • Guyana-correct: this module fixes the prior Ghana defect — GRA = Guyana Revenue Authority, currency GYD, VAT bands 0%/14%, import duty + EV duty exclusions, all sourced from dealer_taxes.
  • No fabrication guarantee: TIN, VIN, CIF, and duty are never model-generated; TIN/VIN come from human-keyed source docs, CIF is human-confirmed, duty is server-computed.
  • Untrusted-text hygiene: extracted OCR strings pass through guardUntrusted so a malicious document cannot inject instructions into the filing or concierge context.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Money: all amounts USD-scale; GYD = USD × dealer.usdExchangeRate; the rate is snapshotted onto each gra_filing so later rate changes never mutate a filed duty.
  • VIN / engineNumber: exactly 17 chars; required before vehicle leaves in_transit; enforced 422 on write.
  • registration: ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$; 3rd-party status field only (no live agency link).
  • dealer_taxes: kind VAT|duty|excise|levy, percent|fixed, threshold (duty applies above CIF threshold), excludeEv (skipped when powertrain=EV).
  • gates: type=gra_filing and type=capital_order both block progression until resolved; evidence[] holds source docs + computed sheet; resolution immutable after resolve.
  • Dates: filedAt in America/Guyana (GMT-4).
  • Scoping: extract, filings, gates, audit-logs, and clearance-doc storage ACL are ALL dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id.
  • SLA: extract fire p95 < 8s; duty recompute synchronous < 300ms; gra_filing gate target resolution ≤ 1 business day before vehicle delivery-readiness.
  • Idempotency: POST /api/gra/filings keyed via idempotency_keys to prevent duplicate filings on retry.

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • GRA Duty Sheet PDF (pdfkit) — header: dealer + division (CAM/GT), filing reference, filedAt (America/Guyana), usdExchangeRate snapshot. Body: per-unit make/model/year/VIN/engine, CIF (USD + GYD), per-tax lines (VAT 0/14%, import duty, excise/levy) each with basis + rate + amount, EV exclusion note where applicable, computed total duty (USD + GYD). Footer: "Server-computed from dealer_taxes; AI-extracted fields human-confirmed" provenance line + gate resolver + filedBy.
  • Clearance Pack — polymorphic documents bundle against the vehicle: proforma/distributor PO, bill of lading, customs manifest, GRA filing reference, duty sheet PDF, landed-cost summary. Served via presigned GCS URL, dealer-scoped ACL.
  • Landed-cost summary — FOB + freight + insurance = CIF, + duty/VAT/excise = total landed (USD-scale, GYD display), feeding vehicle cost basis.
  • Registration/plate record — stored as status field + vehicles.registration (regex-validated); no generated cert (3rd-party issues actual plate).
  • All PDFs list source-document IDs so every figure is traceable to a human-verified input — no fabricated values.

Module C — Parts & Supply Chain Parts Advisor

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • Catalog / stock view — Advisor opens Parts. GET /api/parts lists parts rows scoped by activeDealerId(res) + header x-dealer-id (dealer-scope EVERY query — CORRECTION). Columns: sku, name, stock, reorderLevel, cost (USD-scale), on-order qty, division. GYD cost shown = cost × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209), rounded at display only.
  • Issue to a job card — Technician/Advisor on an open job_cards row requests a part → POST /api/job-cards/:id/parts body {partId,qty,move:"issue"}. Server: (1) re-reads current parts.stock inside a txn, (2) if stock < qty → 409 backorder path, (3) decrements parts.stock, (4) inserts a job_card_parts row (move:issue, unitCost snapshot = parts.cost at issue time so historical costing is stable), (5) recomputes job-card parts subtotal, (6) emits timeline_events (domain=service, entity=job_card) + activity.
  • Return from a job card — reversal (customer declined, wrong part, over-issued): POST /api/job-cards/:id/parts body {jobCardPartId,qty,move:"return"}. Server increments parts.stock back, inserts a paired job_card_parts row (move:return, negative-effect), never mutates the original issue row (append-only audit). Return qty is clamped ≤ net-issued for that job card.
  • Reorder trigger — any stock-decrementing move (issue, or obsolescence write-off) evaluates stock ≤ reorderLevel. When crossed downward, the agent A-Replenish proposes an RFQ/PO draft (see Agents); the crossing itself writes a notifications row + a tasks row for the Parts Advisor. Purely deterministic threshold — no AI needed to detect.
  • RFQ → PO to supplier — Advisor selects supplier and lines → POST /api/part-purchases creates a part_purchases header (status: draft→ordered) with lines {partId,qtyOrdered,unitCost,expectedDate}, supplierId. Money-affecting → wrapped with idempotency_keys (Idempotency-Key header). PO PDF generated via pdfkit (see Documents). Outbound RFQ/PO email queued to email_logs OUTBOX (retry/backoff/dedupe worker) addressed to suppliers.email.
  • Partial / full receipt — goods arrive → POST /api/part-purchases/:id/receive body {lines:[{lineId,qtyReceived}]}. Server per-line: qtyReceivedCum += qtyReceived (never exceed qtyOrdered), increments parts.stock by received qty, updates weighted-average parts.cost = ((oldStock×oldCost)+(recv×poUnitCost)) / (oldStock+recv). Header status → partially_received while any line short, received when all lines fully in. Each receipt is its own idempotent event (partial receipts are additive, replay-safe).
  • Backorder resolution — a receipt that lifts stock above 0 auto-scans job_card_parts rows flagged backordered for that partId (FIFO by createdAt), allocates, un-holds the linked job_cards (status back to in_progress), notifies the service advisor + customer via email_logs.
  • Supersession — if a part is superseded (supersededByPartId set on the old parts row, status:superseded), any issue/PO for the old SKU transparently redirects to the successor SKU with an advisory banner; old SKU hidden from catalog picker but retained for history.
  • Obsolescence sweep — scheduled agent flags no-movement parts (write-off proposal); human confirms → PATCH /api/parts/:id sets status:obsolete and, if writing off, a stock-adjust job_card_parts-style adjustment row records the loss (never silent).

⚙️ Agents in play
  • A-Replenish (Demand-forecast & auto-replenish) — trigger: any move crosses stock ≤ reorderLevel, plus nightly cron. Grounded inputs (dealer-scoped): trailing issue velocity from job_card_parts (issue moves), suppliers.leadTimeDays, current onOrderQty, reorderLevel, cost. Output: a draft part_purchases (status=draft) with suggested qty = max(reorderLevel−stock+leadTimeDemand, 0). Autonomous-write agent → declared + every run in agent_runs (runType=replenish, confidence, latency). HITL: Parts Advisor Approve&Send converts draft→ordered; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → no draft, only a tasks row for human to size manually. Kill-switch isAgentEnabled honored.
  • Obsolescence-sweep agent — trigger: nightly cron. Inputs: no-movement window (no issue in N days) + stock value. Output: write-off proposal list surfaced as notifications/tasks; never auto-writes stock. Human confirms via PATCH /api/parts/:id. Records agent_runs (runType=obsolescence).
  • Supersession advisory — deterministic (no LLM): when picker resolves a superseded SKU it substitutes supersededByPartId and annotates timeline. No autonomous money move.
  • All agent free-text (supplier names, part descriptions from inbound supplier emails) passed through guardUntrusted before any prompt; PII-minimized; outputs id-validated against dealer inventory before display.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Replenishment sizing suggestion with rationale ("velocity 4/wk × 10-day lead = order 12; below-reorder by 5") — advisory only; deterministic threshold detection stays server-side, AI only sizes/explains.
  • Obsolescence candidate ranking (slow-mover risk score) surfaced in /dashboard/inventory-breakdown — human approves each write-off.
  • Concierge (/api/anthropic SSE, nav-only tools) answers "what's on backorder / below reorder" grounded strictly on dealer-scoped parts/part_purchases reads (grounding is dealer-scoped — CORRECTION).
  • Never invents SKUs, supplier prices, or CIF/duty figures; unit costs come only from PO lines or existing parts.cost. Confidence below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE routes to human, no draft PO.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • parts: sku unique per dealer (409 on dup); stock integer ≥ 0 (never allowed negative — backorder path instead); reorderLevel ≥ 0; cost USD-scale, GYD = cost × dealer.usdExchangeRate at display; status ∈ active|superseded|obsolete.
  • part_purchases line invariant: qtyReceivedCum ≤ qtyOrdered; over-receipt → 422. Header status: draft→ordered→partially_received→received (cancelled allowed pre-receipt).
  • job_card_parts append-only; return qty ≤ net-issued; issue snapshots unitCost so later cost changes don't retro-alter closed job cards.
  • Weighted-average cost recompute on every receipt; write-offs record loss value, never silently zeroed.
  • All money mutations (PO create, receive) idempotent via idempotency_keys + Idempotency-Key header; replays return the original result.
  • SLA: below-reorder notification within same request as the crossing move; receipt reflected in stock atomically in-txn; RFQ/PO email delivery per OUTBOX worker retry/backoff; forecast cron nightly (America/Guyana GMT-4).
  • Every row dealer-scoped by activeDealerId+x-dealer-id; audit-logs and object-storage (PO PDF) ACL also dealer-scoped (CORRECTION).

📄 Documents & PDF format
  • Purchase Order PDF (pdfkit, GET /api/part-purchases/:id/pdf): dealer header (division CAM/GT, address), PO number, supplier block, date (America/Guyana GMT-4), line table SKU · description · qtyOrdered · unitCost(USD) · unitCost(GYD @rate) · line total, subtotal/total in USD with GYD equivalent at dealer.usdExchangeRate, expected date, terms. Stored in GCS via presigned URL, ACL dealer-scoped.
  • RFQ email: HTML template via nodemailer, queued to email_logs OUTBOX with dedupeKey, retry/backoff worker; PO PDF attached.
  • Goods-receipt note: per-receipt record derivable from part_purchases receipt events (received qty, date, receiver, resulting weighted-avg cost) — printable audit trail.
  • No tax on internal POs unless supplier is GYD-registered; any GRA/GYD tax figures come only from server-computed dealer_taxes, never AI-invented (CORRECTION — GRA = Guyana Revenue Authority).

Reference R1 — Data Model (all ~57 tables)

Authoritative schema reference for the AURA automotive DMS (Guyana; dealers CAM Motors, GT Automotive). Storage is Drizzle ORM over PostgreSQL, contract-first from OpenAPI. Conventions applied throughout:

  • Tenancy: dealers is the tenant root. Every other business table is dealer-scoped and every query filters on the resolved activeDealerId(res) reconciled against the x-dealer-id header — including concierge grounding reads, audit_logs, and object-storage ACL checks. A table is marked Scope = dealerId when it carries a direct dealerId FK, or Scope = via FK when scoped transitively through its parent (join always re-asserts dealerId).
  • Money: stored at USD scale (integer/decimal USD); GYD display = USD × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209). Never persist GYD.
  • Dates: timestamptz; business rendering in America/Guyana (GMT-4).
  • Identifiers: vin & engineNumber are 17 chars; registration matches ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$.
  • Standard columns on most tables (omitted from field lists below unless load-bearing): id (uuid PK), dealerId (FK→dealers.id), createdAt, updatedAt, createdByUserId.

Cross-cutting integrity rules

  • Every table above except users (global identity) carries or transitively enforces dealerId; joins re-assert it to prevent cross-tenant leakage.
  • All monetary columns (price, vehiclePrice, otdPrice, total, amount, bookingAmount, lifetimeValue, cost, etc.) are stored at USD scale; GYD is a display projection via dealers.usdExchangeRate.
  • Vehicle lifecycle status is single-sourced on vehicles.status; deals (commit/VIN-lock), bookings, and deliveries transition it — no duplicate status columns.
  • Every AI-touched table (customer_personas, documents, call_logs, quotes tax, GRA extraction) records provenance in agent_runs and defers below-confidence output to a human via gates or a HITL review endpoint.

Reference R2 — State Machines

Every state machine below is dealer-scoped: transitions run only against rows whose dealerId matches activeDealerId(res) derived from the x-dealer-id header, and every state change appends a timeline_events row (per-entity, domain, isAgent) plus, when an agent performs the write, a recordAgentRun audit row gated by isAgentEnabled. Money-moving side-effects are wrapped by idempotency_keys. Autonomous-write agents are declared and audited; any agent-proposed transition below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE is downgraded to a human decision and never auto-committed.

1. lead.phase — pipeline stage (versioned checklists)

Phase is the ordered sales-pipeline position; advance is gated by the versioned stage_checklists for the phase being entered. There is exactly ONE Advance CTA — the Workflow panel merges former "Run Review" into a single readiness-gated advance.

Endpoint: POST /api/leads/:id/advance. Readiness is inspected via /leads/:id/review (Readiness Review) against the pinned checklist version; A15 pipeline suggestions and A10 availability-check surface as advisory only.

Reference R3 — Agent Catalog (consolidated)

Every AI-touching capability in AURA is listed here exactly once. The pipeline distinguishes LLM agents (call claude-sonnet-4-6 via the Anthropic SDK) from deterministic engines (no model call — pure code/DB logic) that are colloquially numbered as "agents" but must never be gated by model confidence. Every LLM row obeys the mandatory pipeline in order: kill-switch isAgentEnabled(key) → PII-minimize input → guardUntrusted() wrapping of any customer/third-party text → model call → MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor → recordAgentRun() audit into agent_runs. Autonomous-write agents (create/merge/move records with no human in loop) are explicitly flagged. All grounding queries are dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id — including concierge grounding, GRA extraction lookups, and object-storage ACL — with no cross-dealer read.

Legend

  • Kind: LLM = calls model; DET = deterministic, no model, confidence-floor N/A.
  • HITL: Human-in-the-loop gate before the effect lands. "Autonomous" = writes without a gate (must be declared + audited).
  • Floor: behavior when confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE.

Consolidated catalog

Agent Kind Domain Trigger Grounded inputs (tables / rows) Output shape HITL (yes/no + how) Kill-switch key guardUntrusted? Confidence-floor behavior

Cross-cutting invariants (apply to every LLM row)

  • Kill-switch first: if isAgentEnabled(key) is false the agent short-circuits — no model call, no write — and the caller degrades to the manual path.
  • Audit always: every invocation writes an agent_runs row (runType,input/outputSummary,confidence,status,latency) plus timeline_events(isAgent=true); reviewable via /api/agents/:id/runs and /agents/runs/:id/review (Approve-run review — distinct from Readiness and Approvals review).
  • PII-minimize: only the fields required for the task enter the prompt; identifiers are redacted where not needed.
  • guardUntrusted everywhere: all customer, email, WhatsApp, transcript, ad-payload, and document text is wrapped before reaching the model — no exceptions.
  • Confidence floor: MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE is enforced server-side; below-floor outputs are downgraded to human-review, never auto-committed.
  • Deterministic engines (A2, A3, A11): excluded from confidence-floor and guardUntrusted logic; they still respect kill-switches and audit, and are the sole authority for round-robin assignment, tax math, and VIN allocation respectively.
  • Autonomous-write declaration: only A1 writes business entities without a human gate; it is explicitly declared, fully audited, and its merges are reversible.

Reference R4 — RBAC & API/Error Conventions

AURA authorization is contract-first and multi-tenant. Every request is resolved through three independent gates that must all pass: (1) authentication (Clerk session → users.clerkId → auth/me), (2) dealer-tenancy scoping (activeDealerId(res) derived from the x-dealer-id header cross-checked against dealer_users), and (3) role-based module×category authorization read from roles / role_permissions, refined by role_field_permissions at the serializer/validator boundary. Tenancy is a hard corrective invariant: EVERY query — including concierge grounding retrieval (/anthropic, /copilotkit), audit-logs, and object-storage ACL checks (/storage/objects/*) — is filtered by the resolved dealer id. There is no unscoped read path.

Authorization pipeline (order of evaluation)

#StageSourceFailure status
1Session valid (Clerk)users.clerkId lookup401
2Dealer membership resolvedx-dealer-id header ↔ dealer_users row for user403 cross_tenant (header present but not a member) / 401 (no header on tenant route)
3Module×category permittedrole_permissions(roleId, module, category)403
4Row belongs to activeDealerIdWHERE dealerId = activeDealerId on the target table404 (never 403, to avoid cross-tenant existence disclosure)
5Field-level projection/mutationrole_field_permissions (hidden|view|edit)silent redaction on read / 422 on write of a non-edit field

Modules (permission subjects)

A module is the coarse-grained subject stored in role_permissions.module. Path segments map deterministically to modules (see mapping table); several path families collapse onto one module.

Categories (permission verbs)

The second axis of role_permissions is category. A grant is a (module, category) pair; absence = deny.

CategoryGrantsTypical HTTP
viewList and read one; access read-models/exports for the moduleGET
createCreate new rows; trigger creating sub-actions (e.g. POST /leads/:id/quote)POST (collection / factory)
editMutate existing rows; workflow transitions (advance, assign, decision, deliveries /advance, finance /submit,/sync)PATCH, PUT, action POST /:id/*
deleteSoft-delete / void / cancel (invoices void, bookings cancel)DELETE
adminConfiguration & privileged sub-actions within the module: resolve a gate, review an agent run, approve field-permission changes, retry an email, edit tax configPOST /gates/:id/resolve, /agents/runs/:id/review, /admin/*, /emails/logs/:id/retry

Cross-cutting rules: the entire admin module requires category admin (admin = settings:admin — a dealer-scoped super-user within one tenant, not cross-tenant). The entire platform module requires platform super-admin, which is a distinct flag on users evaluated before dealer scoping and is the only role permitted to operate cross-tenant (manage dealers, dealer members, and global users). All AI-privileged actions (resolving gra_filing gates, approving A5 doc extractions at /documents/extraction/review, approving A7 outreach send) require the target module's admin or edit category as noted, never a bare login.

Path-segment → module mapping

Resolution walks the first meaningful path segment (after /api) and normalizes aliases and action sub-paths onto the owning module. Nested action segments do NOT change the module.

First segment(s)ModuleNotes
enquiries, leadsleads/enquiries GET vehicles is public-facing but still dealer-scoped by the enquiry context; all /leads/:id/* actions resolve to leads
customerscustomerspersona recommend, relations, assets all under customers
dealsdealsquote sub-paths under a lead map to deals (quote authoring) even though the URL prefix is /leads

finance-applications, finance-connector, banksfinance/banks GET is view; LOS submit/sync require edit

Method → category mapping

HTTP method / shapeDefault categoryOverrides
GETview—
POST to collection (/leads, /deals)create—
POST to action sub-path (/:id/advance, /:id/assign, /:id/decision, /submit, /sync, /remind)editstate-mutating workflow actions
POST to privileged sub-path (/gates/:id/resolve, /agents/runs/:id/review, /documents/extraction/review, /emails/logs/:id/retry)adminexplicit route-level override
PATCH / PUTedit—
DELETEdeletevoid/cancel semantics
POST that only reads (/leads/:id/availability-check, /persona/recommend, /calls/:id/suggest-sentiment)viewread-model POST (body-carried query, no persistence beyond an agent_runs audit row)

AUTH_ONLY segments

These require a valid session and dealer scope but perform no module×category check because they either target the caller's own identity or are navigation/search surfaces gated entirely by downstream row scoping:

  • GET /auth/me — returns the caller's users + resolved dealer_users membership and effective permission set.
  • GET /search — federated search; results are post-filtered by activeDealerId AND by the caller's per-module view grants (a hit in a module the user cannot view is dropped, never returned as 403).
  • /anthropic/conversations (SSE concierge) and /copilotkit — the assistant exposes nav-only tools; every grounding read it performs is dealer-scoped and re-checks the caller's view grant server-side, so no data the user could not otherwise see is surfaced.
  • GET /notifications, GET /tasks (own) — scoped to the caller's user id within the dealer.

Not AUTH_ONLY (public / signed): webhook endpoints (/webhooks/meta, /webhooks/whatsapp, /webhooks/twilio/*, /webhooks/gmail) bypass RBAC entirely and are authenticated by HMAC signature + idempotency on webhook_events; the tokenized /test-drive/:token pages are authenticated by the opaque testDriveToken, not a session.

Field-level permissions (role_field_permissions)

After module×category passes, field visibility/mutability is enforced at two boundaries so it cannot be bypassed by a raw API call:

LevelBoundaryBehavior
hiddenSerializer (response projection)Field is stripped from the response payload entirely — key absent, not null. Also excluded from search, exports, and PDF renders (quote-pdf.ts, delivery invoice).
viewSerializer + validatorField is returned read-only. If present in a write body it is silently ignored (dropped by the Zod pre-parse) — or rejected with 422 when strict mode is on for that route.
editValidator (request body)Field may be written. Absence of an edit grant for a field present in the body → 422 {unmet:[{field, reason:"field_not_editable"}]}.

Field permissions are the mechanism for role sensitivity such as hiding deals.floorPrice / cost basis from a junior advisor, hiding customers.lifetimeValue, hiding vehicles cost while showing price, or making finance_applications.apr view-only for non-finance roles. Because enforcement is at the serializer, the same rules apply to nested reads (e.g. a vehicle embedded in a deal).

HTTP status conventions

StatusMeaning in AURABody shape
200Successful read or successful mutation returning current stateresource / list envelope
201Resource created (POST to collection, quote version created, booking created)created resource incl. server-assigned id / number (quoteNumber, invoiceNumber, bookingId)
400Malformed request — JSON parse failure, wrong types, failed Zod primitive validation (bad vin/engineNumber not 17 chars, registration not ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$), missing required header shape{error, issues:[Zod]}
401No/invalid Clerk session, or a tenant route called without a resolvable x-dealer-id{error:"unauthenticated"}
403Authenticated but not permitted: (a) module×category denied, (b) cross_tenant — x-dealer-id names a dealer the user is not a member of, (c) platform route without super-admin{error:"forbidden", code:"cross_tenant" when applicable}
404Resource not found within activeDealerId. Deliberately returned instead of 403 for a row that exists in another tenant, to prevent cross-tenant existence disclosure{error:"not_found"}
409Conflict: an idempotency key is still in_progress; or optimistic-concurrency clash (stale version on quote/deal update); or business-state conflict such as committing a deal whose vehicle is no longer available (VIN-lock race), or double-converting a booking{error:"conflict", reason}
422Semantic/precondition failure — request is well-formed but a business rule/checklist is unmet. Canonical shape {unmet:[…]}{unmet:[{code, label, …}]}
500Unhandled server error / upstream failure (LOS live connector down before falling back to sandbox, GCS sidecar unreachable, Anthropic call failure not caught by kill-switch){error:"internal"} (no stack leak)

The 422 {unmet:[]} contract

AURA standardizes precondition failures on a single machine-readable envelope so the UI can render a blocking checklist and surface one Advance CTA. It is emitted by:

EndpointChecked againstExample unmet entries
POST /leads/:id/advanceversioned stage_checklists for the target phase/statusreservation_fee_unpaid, test_drive_incomplete, financing_unqualified
POST /deliveries/:id/advance11-step sequence; current step's required fields (PDI items, signature, insurance/registration)pdi_checklist_incomplete, signature_missing, insurance_not_recorded
POST /deals/:id commitbelow-floor / VIN availability gatesbelow_floor_price_gate_open, vehicle_not_available
PATCH with non-editable fieldrole_field_permissions{field, reason:"field_not_editable"}
AI-gated confirmationsMIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE, open gatesagent_confidence_below_threshold, gra_filing_gate_unresolved

Each entry carries a stable code and human label; the client never advances until unmet is empty. This is the corrective single-CTA model — Run Review is merged into the Workflow advance, and the three senses of "Review" are disambiguated as: Readiness (the unmet checklist), Approvals (open gates), and Approve-run (/agents/runs/:id/review HITL).

Idempotency-Key semantics

All money-moving and other non-idempotent create actions require an Idempotency-Key header, recorded in the idempotency_keys table (dealer-scoped). Applies to: POST /payments, POST /receipts, POST /invoices, deal commit, finance /submit & /sync (los_submissions), and outbound send actions.

State of stored keyServer behaviorStatus
Unseen keyReserve row (in_progress), execute, persist request-hash + response200/201
Seen key, completed, same request hashReplay the stored response — no re-execution, no duplicate ledger entryoriginal 200/201
Seen key, completed, different request hashReject — key reuse with mismatched payload422 {unmet:[{code:"idempotency_key_payload_mismatch"}]}
Seen key, still in_progressConcurrent duplicate in flight — do not double-execute409 {error:"conflict", reason:"in_progress"}

Keys are unique per (dealerId, endpoint, key). Webhook idempotency is handled separately via webhook_events (provider event id / dedupeKey), and the email/WhatsApp outbox dedupes on email_logs.dedupeKey — both share the same "at-most-once effect" intent as Idempotency-Key but are keyed by inbound event rather than a client-supplied header.

Agent-invoked authorization & audit

Autonomous-write agents (A1 Intake/Dedup lead create-merge, A2 round-robin assignment, A11 VIN allocation) execute under a declared service principal that still passes dealer scoping and records to audit-logs + agent_runs. Every AI call additionally enforces the kill-switch isAgentEnabled, PII-minimization, guardUntrusted wrapping of all untrusted text, and MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE — a run below threshold does not write; it opens a gate/HITL review instead (returned to the caller as 422 agent_confidence_below_threshold where the action was user-initiated). Human-approval agents (A5 doc pre-fill, A7 outreach) never mutate the target entity until the corresponding admin/edit review action is invoked.

Reference R5 — Reports, Dashboards & BI

All reporting, dashboard, and BI surfaces in AURA are computed server-side (corrected from client-side aggregation). Every query is dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) resolved from x-dealer-id — no report, chart, prediction, or export may read cross-dealer rows. Money is stored USD-scale and rendered GYD via dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209) at serialization; dates are bucketed in America/Guyana (GMT-4). Division splits are always CAM vs GT. Reports respect role_permissions (module×category) and role_field_permissions (hidden|view|edit): a field the caller cannot view is elided from report rows and from exports before the response leaves the server.

R5.1 — The 10 Report Types (GET/POST /api/reports, per-type RBAC)

/api/reports exposes ten typed reports. Each takes a type discriminator plus a shared filter envelope; each has its own RBAC gate keyed on role_permissions for the reports module. Aggregation runs in Postgres/Drizzle (SUM/COUNT/window functions), never in the browser.

#Report typePurposePersona visibility (RBAC)Primary source tablesKey computed fields

Shared filter envelope

  • Date range — from/to bucketed in America/Guyana; presets today/WTD/MTD/QTD/YTD.
  • Division — divisionId (CAM/GT/both); Advisors/Managers are pre-scoped to their dealer_users.divisionId.
  • Owner/advisor — ownerUserId, assignedTo, salesAdvisorUserId, technician; Advisors forced to self.
  • Dimensional — make/model/year/powertrain, channel/source (via lead_sources), bankId, status/phase/stage enums, loyaltyTier, accountType person|business.
  • All filters translate to WHERE/GROUP BY in Drizzle; the response carries only aggregates and, where requested, a paginated drill-down of visible rows.

R5.3 — Persona Dashboards

The same endpoints power three role-scoped landing dashboards; RBAC and role_field_permissions decide which tiles/fields render, and division scope is applied server-side.

PersonaScopeComposition (tiles & sources)

R5.4 — Review Disambiguation on Dashboards

Corrected: dashboard tiles labeled "Review" are explicitly disambiguated so counts never conflate distinct queues.

  • Readiness Review — POST /leads/:id/review / /advance 422 {unmet:[]} against versioned stage_checklists; the single Advance CTA. Not an approval.
  • Approvals Review — the gates queue (below_floor_price|credit_decline|capital_order|gra_filing|refund_release|stage_advance) with evidence[]/resolution.
  • Approve-run Review — HITL over AI output: /documents/extraction/review (A5), /agents/runs/:id/review, A7 Approve&Send, /gra/extract confirmation. Only sub-threshold or autonomous-write runs land here.

R5.5 — Export (CSV / Excel / PDF)

  • Every report and dashboard drill-down exports via a format=csv|xlsx|pdf parameter; the file is built server-side from the same dealer-scoped, RBAC-filtered aggregate — fields hidden by role_field_permissions never appear in the file.
  • CSV/XLSX — flat aggregate + optional drill-down rows; money exported as GYD with the applied usdExchangeRate noted; dates in America/Guyana. Mirrors the /vehicles/import xlsx conventions.
  • PDF — rendered with pdfkit (same engine as quote-pdf.ts and the delivery invoice), branded per dealer/division, with filter header and generation timestamp.
  • Large exports are queued and delivered through the storage layer (/storage/uploads/request-url, presigned GCS URL via the Replit sidecar) with dealer-scoped object-storage ACL — no cross-dealer object is ever addressable.
  • Every export is written to audit-logs (dealer-scoped) and activity.

R5.6 — Natural-Language "Ask Your Data"

  • The Concierge (/api/anthropic/conversations SSE + /api/copilotkit) answers NL analytics questions, but its grounding is dealer-scoped — it may only read the current dealer's aggregates (corrected).
  • It does not free-form query the database: it maps intent to the existing /dashboard/* and /reports endpoints, so numbers stay server-computed and RBAC/field-permission filtered; CopilotKit tools remain nav-only.
  • Powered by claude-sonnet-4-6 under the standard guardrails: isAgentEnabled kill-switch, recordAgentRun audit, PII-minimization, guardUntrusted on any user/inbound text, and MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE (below-threshold answers defer rather than assert). It never fabricates figures — it cites the report/endpoint that produced them.

Reference R6 — Notifications & Comms Matrix

Every notification in AURA is dealer-scoped (persisted with dealerId, filtered by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id). In-App is always the primary channel — a row is written to notifications for the target dealer_users record and rendered via GET /api/notifications. Email and WhatsApp are supplementary, opt-in, best-effort channels that flow through the unified OUTBOX (email_logs, one table for both email and WhatsApp with channel discriminator) and the retry/backoff worker. Recipient resolution never trusts request-body role claims; it derives role from role_permissions / dealer_users (reportingManagerUserId, divisionId, isGeneralManager). All customer-facing message bodies that echo user/AI text are wrapped with guardUntrusted before templating. Any notification originating from an autonomous agent carries isAgent = true on its timeline_events row and a linked agent_runs record.

R6.1 Channel & delivery model

ChannelStore / mechanismDelivery guaranteeDedupe / idempotencyFailure behavior
In-App (primary)Row in notifications (per dealer_users), surfaced by GET /api/notifications; unread badgeSynchronous DB write inside the triggering transaction — never lost, never retriedNatural key (dealerId, recipientUserId, type, entityType, entityId); upsert bumps updatedAt instead of duplicatingN/A (transactional). If parent txn rolls back, no notification.
Emailemail_logs OUTBOX (channel='email'), rendered from emails/templates, sent by nodemailer Gmail SMTP workerAt-least-once via DB outbox; emails/settings per dealer can disablededupeKey unique per logical event; worker skips already-sent keysExponential backoff, capped attempts; POST /api/emails/logs/:id/retry for manual replay; terminal → status='failed'
WhatsAppemail_logs OUTBOX (channel='whatsapp') → Meta Cloud API v21 / Twilio adapter; conversation state in whatsapp_conversations/whatsapp_messagesAt-least-once; requires opt-in + open 24h session or approved templatededupeKey as below; outbound linked to whatsapp_conversations.idBackoff retry; on hard failure or opt-out → fallback to Email, then In-App only (see R6.4)

R6.3 dedupeKey construction rules

  • Every OUTBOX (email_logs) row carries a dedupeKey; the worker treats an already-sent key as a no-op, making all senders idempotent under retries and duplicate triggers (e.g. webhook redelivery, sweep overlap).
  • Keys are event-scoped, not time-scoped, except recurring nudges which append a bounded index/window suffix (:remind:{n}, :{dueWindow}) so each legitimate reminder is distinct but the count is capped.
  • Advisor and manager copies of the same underlying event use distinct keys (suffix :advisor/:manager) so both are delivered rather than one deduping the other.
  • In-App notifications dedupe on the natural key (dealerId, recipientUserId, type, entityType, entityId) — a repeat trigger bumps updatedAt/unread rather than stacking rows.
  • All keys are implicitly dealer-partitioned; dealerId is part of the stored row scope so identical entity ids across dealers never collide.

R6.4 WhatsApp opt-out & delivery-failure fallback

ConditionDetectionActionPersistence
Explicit opt-out Inbound STOP/unsubscribe keyword on webhooks/whatsapp or webhooks/twilio/whatsapp, or preference cleared on contacts/customers Suppress all future WhatsApp sends to that number; downgrade every WhatsApp row for that recipient to Email → In-App Opt-out flag on contacts/whatsapp_conversations; audited
No prior opt-in No consent record / no session in whatsapp_conversations Do not initiate WhatsApp; use Email (if valid) else In-App only. Customer-facing content still delivered Consent state on contact record
Outside 24h session window Last inbound > 24h ago in whatsapp_conversations Only pre-approved Meta templates may send; free-form composed messages (A7 outreach) queue until session reopens or route to Email whatsapp_messages window state
Delivery failure (transient) Provider error / no delivery receipt; email_logs attempt fails Exponential backoff retry up to cap; POST /api/emails/logs/:id/retry for manual replay Attempt count + status on email_logs
Delivery failure (terminal) Retry cap reached or hard bounce/invalid number Cascade: WhatsApp → Email → In-App. Re-enqueue the same logical event on the next channel with a new channel-suffixed dedupeKey (...:fallbackEmail) so it is not deduped against the failed WhatsApp send email_logs.status='failed'; fallback row linked; advisor In-App notice on customer-facing failure

In-App is never subject to opt-out or delivery failure — it is the guaranteed floor for every trigger, so a customer opting out of WhatsApp and lacking a valid email still leaves the internal recipient (advisor/manager) fully notified, and vice-versa. Fallback cascades are dealer-scoped and audited via activity/timeline_events.

Reference R7 — Integration Contracts

This reference gives the concrete wire contract for every external integration in AURA. Each entry states the exact request shape, the response/verification path, and the AURA-side handling. Every integration surface that touches data is dealer-scoped: inbound webhooks resolve x-dealer-id / division from the routing metadata before any write, presigned-storage ACLs carry the dealerId, and AI grounding (concierge, extraction, classification) is filtered to activeDealerId(res). All untrusted inbound text (webhook bodies, email bodies, WhatsApp message text, transcript fragments) is wrapped with guardUntrusted() before it is ever placed in an AI prompt. Autonomous-write agents (A1 Intake/Dedup) run only when isAgentEnabled(key) is true, log via recordAgentRun, PII-minimize their input, and drop below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE to a human queue.

Integration surface map

IntegrationDirectionAURA endpoint(s)VerificationPersistence
Meta Lead AdsInboundGET/POST /api/webhooks/metahub.verify_token / X-Hub-Signature-256 HMAC-SHA256webhook_events (type meta_leadgen)
WhatsApp Meta Cloud API v21In + OutGET/POST /api/webhooks/whatsapp; outbound Graph sendhub.verify_token / X-Hub-Signature-256webhook_events, whatsapp_conversations/_messages, email_logs (outbox)
Twilio WhatsAppIn + OutPOST /api/webhooks/twilio/whatsappX-Twilio-Signature HMAC-SHA1webhook_events (type twilio_whatsapp), whatsapp_messages
Twilio VoiceIn + OutPOST /api/webhooks/twilio/voice, /voice/complete; GET /api/telephony/tokenX-Twilio-Signature; JWT AccessTokencall_logs
Gmail inbound (IMAP)Inbound pollworker (2-min cadence) → A1OAuth/app-password IMAP authwebhook_events (type gmail_email), leads, email_logs
Gmail outbound (SMTP)OutboundPOST /api/emails/send; outbox workernodemailer SMTP authemail_logs (retry/backoff/dedupe)
