Meta Lead Ads webhook

PhaseContract
Verify (GET)GET /api/webhooks/meta?hub.mode=subscribe&hub.verify_token=&hub.challenge=. Compare hub.verify_token to configured META_VERIFY_TOKEN. Match → respond 200 with the raw hub.challenge value (text/plain, echo). Mismatch → 403.
Signature (POST)Header X-Hub-Signature-256: sha256=<hex>. Compute HMAC-SHA256 over the raw request body with META_APP_SECRET; constant-time compare. Fail → 401, no processing.
Body shape{ object: "page", entry: [{ id, time, changes: [{ field: "leadgen", value: { leadgen_id, page_id, form_id, ad_id, created_time } }] }] }. The payload carries only leadgen_id, not the field answers.
Graph fetchFetch answers: GET https://graph.facebook.com/v21.0/{leadgen_id}?access_token={PAGE_TOKEN} → { id, created_time, field_data: [{ name, values: [] }], ad_id, form_id }. Field names (full_name, phone_number, email, vehicle_of_interest, …) are mapped to leads columns.
Idempotency + dealer scopePersist webhook_events keyed on leadgen_id (unique) — replays no-op. page_id/form_id resolve the target dealerId and divisionId before any lead write.
DownstreamHandoff to A1 Intake/Dedup (autonomous write, gated by isAgentEnabled + recordAgentRun). Creates or merges leads (channel=meta_leadgen, source from ad). Below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE on a merge → human dedup queue. All fetched text guardUntrusted-wrapped. Ack 200 immediately; processing is async.

WhatsApp Meta Cloud API v21

PhaseContract
Verify (GET)Same handshake as Meta: GET /api/webhooks/whatsapp?hub.mode&hub.verify_token&hub.challenge → echo hub.challenge on token match.
Inbound signatureX-Hub-Signature-256 HMAC-SHA256 over raw body with app secret (as Meta above).
Inbound message body{ object:"whatsapp_business_account", entry:[{ id, changes:[{ field:"messages", value:{ messaging_product:"whatsapp", metadata:{ display_phone_number, phone_number_id }, contacts:[{ profile:{ name }, wa_id }], messages:[{ from, id, timestamp, type:"text"|"interactive"|"button"|"image", text:{ body }, interactive:{ ... } }] } }] }] }. Status callbacks arrive as value.statuses:[{ id, status:"sent"|"delivered"|"read"|"failed", recipient_id }].
Dealer routingmetadata.phone_number_id maps to the owning dealer; wa_id ties to/creates a whatsapp_conversations row scoped to that dealer. Message stored in whatsapp_messages; body guardUntrusted-wrapped before the guided-bot / A1 classifier reads it.
Outbound sendPOST https://graph.facebook.com/v21.0/{phone_number_id}/messages, header Authorization: Bearer {WHATSAPP_TOKEN}. Session (<24h) text: { messaging_product:"whatsapp", to, type:"text", text:{ body } }. Outside window: { ..., type:"template", template:{ name, language:{ code }, components:[] } }. Response { messages:[{ id }] } — the wamid is stored for status correlation.
OutboxAll sends go through the unified email_logs OUTBOX (email+WhatsApp) with dedupeKey, retry, and exponential backoff; a worker drains it. Idempotency prevents double-send on retry.
AckReturn 200 within Meta's timeout; failed sends are the worker's concern, not the webhook's.

Twilio WhatsApp (alternate transport)

PhaseContract
Inbound signatureHeader X-Twilio-Signature. Validate HMAC-SHA1 (base64) computed over the full request URL concatenated with the sorted POST params, keyed by TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN. Fail → 403.
Inbound body (form-encoded)MessageSid, AccountSid, From=whatsapp:+<E164>, To=whatsapp:+<E164>, Body, NumMedia, ProfileName, WaId, MediaUrl0…. To number maps to the dealer.
Persistencewebhook_events (type twilio_whatsapp, idempotency on MessageSid) + whatsapp_messages. Body guardUntrusted-wrapped.
ResponseReply with TwiML <Response><Message>…</Message></Response> for a bot turn, or empty <Response/> to ack silently and send later via the outbox.

Twilio Voice

PhaseContract
Access token mintGET /api/telephony/token (auth required, dealer-scoped) → { token, identity }. JWT AccessToken with a VoiceGrant (outgoingApplicationSid, incomingAllow), signed by API key/secret, short TTL. Client SDK uses it for the browser softphone. GET /api/telephony/config exposes provider (twilio vs stub) and whether the browser dialer is enabled.
Incoming call (POST)POST /api/webhooks/twilio/voice, X-Twilio-Signature validated (SHA1). Form params CallSid, From, To, CallStatus, Direction. Respond TwiML that dials: <Response><Dial action="/api/webhooks/twilio/voice/complete" ><Client>{identity}</Client></Dial></Response>.
Completion (POST)POST /api/webhooks/twilio/voice/complete with CallSid, CallDuration, DialCallStatus, RecordingUrl. Upserts call_logs (provider=twilio, direction, duration). Fires A6 Call sentiment fire-and-forget; sentiment is also refreshable via POST /api/leads/:id/calls/:id/suggest-sentiment. Transcript text guardUntrusted-wrapped before AI scoring; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE the sentiment stays null pending human.
FallbackWhen provider is unconfigured, StubTelephonyAdapter serves /telephony/config and call_logs are created manually; no live TwiML.

Gmail inbound (IMAP intake)

AspectContract
CadenceWorker polls IMAP every 2 minutes per dealer inbox (auth via OAuth or app password from emails/settings).
DedupEach message keyed by RFC Message-ID into webhook_events (type gmail_email); already-seen IDs skipped.
Classify → leadSubject + body (both guardUntrusted-wrapped, PII-minimized) go to A1 Intake/Dedup (claude-sonnet-4-6). AI classifies enquiry vs noise and extracts candidate name/phone/vehicle-interest. Autonomous leads create/merge (channel=gmail_email) only when isAgentEnabled; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → human triage, no auto-create. Every run logged via recordAgentRun.
ScopeThe polled mailbox belongs to exactly one dealer; the resulting lead inherits that dealerId/divisionId. Replies leave via the SMTP outbox (email_logs).

LOS — Demerara Bank finance connector

PhaseContract
ConnectorPluggable via LOS_CONNECTOR. Live = Demerara Bank REST; else sandbox DTI. los_submissions.mode = live | mock. GET /api/finance-connector reports active connector + mode.
SubmitPOST /api/finance-applications/:id/submit. Request derived from finance_applications: { amount, downPayment, termMonths, apr, bankId, applicant:{…PII-minimized}, vehicle:{ vin, price } } — amounts USD-scale as stored (GYD only at display via usdExchangeRate). Records los_submissions (event=submit, connector, mode, reference, payload). Response { losReference, status } stored as finance_applications.losReference; status moves pending→submitted, appended to statusHistory[]. capital_order/credit_decline may open a gates row.
SyncPOST /api/finance-applications/:id/sync (or worker) polls the LOS by losReference. Response { status, apr?, declineReason? } maps to submitted→under_review→approved|declined→disbursed; each transition appended to statusHistory[] and logged in los_submissions (event=sync). A declined result raises a credit_decline gate.

GCS presigned object storage

PhaseContract
Request URLPOST /api/storage/uploads/request-url req { fileName, contentType, module, entityId }. Server (via Replit GCS sidecar) mints a presigned PUT URL. Resp { uploadUrl, objectPath, expiresAt, headers:{ "Content-Type" } }. Client PUTs bytes directly to GCS.
ACLThe object path/ACL is stamped with the caller's dealerId and module via the ACL module — every object read/write is dealer-scoped. Cross-dealer access is denied even with a valid path.
Serve/downloadGET /api/storage/objects/* resolves the ACL, then redirects to a presigned GET URL (short TTL) or streams. Document rows (documents, customer_documents, finance_documents) and vehicles.images[]/documents[] store objectPath, never public URLs.
Downstream AIUploaded docs feed A5 Doc pre-fill and GRA vision extract (POST /api/gra/extract) — see tax note below.

Anthropic — claude-sonnet-4-6

ModeContract
Streaming (SSE)POST /api/anthropic/conversations is the Concierge. Server calls Anthropic Messages API with stream:true, model claude-sonnet-4-6, and relays SSE (content_block_delta → token chunks). Grounding context is filtered to activeDealerId(res) — the concierge can only cite this dealer's data. Persists conversations/messages. User text and any retrieved records are guardUntrusted-wrapped.
Batch / synchronousAgents (A1, A3 code path, A5, A6, A7, A10, A15, persona recommend, sentiment digest, GRA extract) call the Messages API non-streaming and parse a single JSON result. Every call: kill-switch isAgentEnabled(key) → recordAgentRun (runType, input/outputSummary, confidence, latency, status) in agent_runs → enforce MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE (below routes to human) → PII-minimize input.
GRA tax (correction)GRA = Guyana Revenue Authority (GYD). AI extracts legible fields only from the document image; it never invents TIN/VIN/CIF/duty. The server computes tax deterministically from dealer_taxes (VAT 0/14%, import duty, EV duty exclusions via excludeEv), and a human gra_filing gate confirms before any gra/filings record.

CopilotKit runtime

AspectContract
EndpointPOST /api/copilotkit — CopilotKit runtime backed by claude-sonnet-4-6.
Tool policyNav-only: the exposed actions are limited to client navigation (open a lead, deal, vehicle, deliveries board, report). No mutation, no money, no autonomous writes are wired as CopilotKit tools — write/approval flows stay behind their own dealer-scoped, gated endpoints.
Scope + safetyAny surfaced data is filtered to the active dealer; user prompts are guardUntrusted-wrapped. Kill-switch and audit apply as with any AI call.

Reference R8 — Document & PDF Template Layouts

All AURA documents are rendered server-side with pdfkit (see server/pdf/quote-pdf.ts and the delivery-invoice renderer). No client-side PDF generation exists. Every document is dealer-scoped: the header block, logo, currency conversion rate, terms, and tax lines are all resolved from the dealers row bound to activeDealerId(res) / x-dealer-id — never a global default. Money is stored USD-scale and displayed as GYD via USD × dealer.usdExchangeRate (~209); both figures are printed. Dates render in America/Guyana (GMT-4). Tax lines are always computed deterministically on the server from dealer_taxes (agent A3 for sales code/quote; never AI-invented amounts).

R8.0 — Shared Rendering Contract

ConcernRule
Generatorpdfkit streaming to buffer → storage/objects (GCS via Replit sidecar, ACL-scoped to dealer) → presigned URL, OR streamed inline on the *.pdf endpoint.
Dealer resolutionHeader/logo/terms/footer from dealers (name, division CAM|GT, address, TIN placeholder, logo asset). Division badge from divisions.
CurrencyEvery money value printed twice: USD (stored) and GYD = round(USD × dealer.usdExchangeRate). Rate printed in footer as of issue date.
Tax sourcedealer_taxes rows: kind ∈ VAT|duty|excise|levy, percent|fixed, threshold, excludeEv. Applied in row order; EV vehicles skip rows with excludeEv=true.
Vehicle identityVIN (17), engineNumber (17), registration (^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$), make/model/trim/year from vehicles.
Untrusted textAny customer-supplied note/complaint/free-text merged into a PDF is passed through guardUntrusted before rendering.
Quotation vs InvoiceIdentical layout skeleton; only the document title, the number field (quoteNumber vs invoiceNumber), and the footer (validity vs receipt/payment) differ.

R8.1 — Sales Code / Quotation (GT format)

Generator: quote-pdf.ts (pdfkit), values from agent A3 (deterministic tax + pdfkit). Source tables: quotes (versioned: quoteNumber, taxLines[], total, validUntil, status current|superseded, sentVia), leads, vehicles. Endpoints: POST /leads/:id/quote, GET /leads/:id/quote.pdf, GET /leads/:id/quotes/:id/pdf. Delivery: POST /leads/:id/quotes/:id/send → email outbox (email_logs) or WhatsApp outbox; sentVia recorded.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer logo + name (CAM Motors / GT Automotive), division badge, dealer address; doc title QUOTATION; quoteNumber; issue date (Guyana TZ); version tag (v1, v2… — superseded versions watermarked).
Party blockCustomer: name, accountType person|business, contact (from contacts), lead ref. Vehicle: make/model/trim/year, VIN, registration (if allocated), selectedModel / interestedVehicleId, powertrain.
Line itemsColumns: Description | Qty | Unit Price (USD) | Unit Price (GYD) | Line Total (USD/GYD). Rows: vehicle base price, accessories[], less discount, less tradeInValue (if quoted with appraisal).
Tax linesOne row per applicable dealer_taxes entry: VAT (0/14%), import duty, excise/levy as configured. EV skips excludeEv rows. Each shows rate + USD + GYD.
Totals blockSubtotal, discount, taxable base, total tax, Grand Total in GYD (bold) + USD. Deposit / reservation fee line if applicable.
FootervalidUntil date, terms & conditions, exchange rate used, prepared-by advisor (ownerUserId/assignedTo). No receipt number (unpaid document).

R8.2 — Reservation Invoice

Generator: pdfkit invoice renderer. Source: bookings (vehicleId, bookingAmount, amountPaid, paymentStatus, expiresAt), invoices (invoiceNumber, status), payments (idempotent), receipts. Also driven by leads.reservationFeePaid. Delivery: email/WhatsApp outbox after payment recorded via idempotent POST /payments. Layout = Invoice skeleton, title RESERVATION INVOICE.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer/logo/division; title RESERVATION INVOICE; invoiceNumber; issue date.
Party blockCustomer + reserved vehicle: make/model/year, VIN, holdUntil/holdReason, vehicle status reserved.
Line itemsReservation / booking fee (bookingAmount) row; USD + GYD.
Tax linesVAT on fee if dealer_taxes configures it; otherwise none.
Totals blockFee total GYD + USD; amountPaid; balance to full deal (informational). paymentStatus.
FooterReservation expiresAt (booking expiry), refund terms, method (cash|card|bank_transfer|cheque|mobile_money|financing), receipt # from receipts, exchange rate.

R8.3 — Final Invoice (Vehicle Sale)

Generator: pdfkit; issued when deals.stage=committed (VIN-lock on commit, auto-creates delivery). Source: deals (vehiclePrice, discount, tradeInValue, otdPrice, depositPaid, salesAdvisorUserId), invoices, payments, receipts, outstanding-balances. Endpoint: exposed via delivery step (/deliveries/:id/invoice.pdf) as the tax invoice. Delivery: email/WhatsApp outbox + attached to delivery pack.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer/logo/division; title TAX INVOICE; invoiceNumber; issue date; deal ref.
Party blockCustomer (person|business, parentAccountId for business), vehicle: make/model/trim/year, VIN + engineNumber + registration, powertrain.
Line itemsDescription | Qty | Unit USD | Unit GYD | Total: vehicle price, accessories[], trade-in credit (negative, from linked appraisals.finalOffer), discount (gated: >5% triggers gates below_floor_price).
Tax linesAll applicable dealer_taxes: VAT 0/14%, import duty, excise, levy; EV exclusions honored; each with rate + USD + GYD.
Totals blockSubtotal, discount, taxable base, per-tax totals, OTD price (otdPrice) in GYD + USD; deposit paid; balance due / financing portion.
FooterPayment method(s), financing note (finance_applications.losReference if applicable), receipt #, exchange rate, advisor (salesAdvisorUserId), invoice status issued|partially_paid|paid|void.

R8.4 — Delivery Handover Form

Generator: pdfkit delivery renderer over the 11-step deliveries.steps jsonb. Source: deliveries (pdiItems[], signatureData, feedbackRating, feedbackComment, registration/insurance fields), assets (deliveredAt, serviceAdvisorUserId), coverage_plans. Endpoints: POST /deliveries/:id/advance (steps sales_order→pdi_checklist→registration→insurance→invoice→appointment→delivery→signature→feedback), GET /deliveries/:id/pdi, /delivery-advisors. Delivery of doc: signed copy to email/WhatsApp outbox; feedback captured as first-class Review/CSAT entity.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer/logo/division; title VEHICLE DELIVERY & HANDOVER; delivery ref; deliveredAt date.
Party blockCustomer + delivery advisor (serviceAdvisorUserId/delivery-advisors); vehicle: make/model/year, VIN, engineNumber, registration, odometer at handover.
Line items / checklistsPDI checklist table (pdiItems[]: item | pass/fail | note); registration details; insurance policy fields; linked invoice #; coverage plan (warranty|amc) terms.
Tax linesNone on handover itself (references the Final Invoice for amounts).
Totals / acknowledgementSummary of items delivered (keys, documents[], accessories[]); asset created (assets.status=active).
FooterCustomer signature (signatureData) + date; advisor signature; CSAT rating/comment (feedbackRating/feedbackComment, guardUntrusted); handover terms.

R8.5 — Service Quotation

Generator: pdfkit. Source: service_orders (type, complaint, odometer), job_cards (checklist[], laborHours, laborRate), job_card_parts, parts (sku, cost). Endpoint: job-card / service-order estimate; Delivery: email/WhatsApp outbox. Layout = quotation skeleton, title SERVICE QUOTATION.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer/logo/division; title SERVICE QUOTATION; service-order ref; date.
Party blockCustomer + vehicle from assets: make/model/year, VIN, registration, odometer; complaint (guardUntrusted); technician.
Line itemsTwo groups — Labor (job description | hours laborHours | rate laborRate | USD/GYD) and Parts (sku | qty | unit cost | USD/GYD from job_card_parts issue moves).
Tax linesVAT per dealer_taxes on labor + parts as configured.
Totals blockLabor subtotal, parts subtotal, tax, Grand Total GYD + USD.
FootervalidUntil, authorization terms, exchange rate, advisor. No receipt (unpaid).

R8.6 — Service Invoice

Generator: pdfkit. Source: service_invoices, job_cards (status completed), job_card_parts, coverage_plans (warranty|amc offsets), payments, receipts. Endpoint: /job-cards/:id/invoice, service-invoices. Delivery: email/WhatsApp outbox. Layout = invoice skeleton, title SERVICE INVOICE.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer/logo/division; title SERVICE INVOICE; invoiceNumber; date; job-card ref.
Party blockCustomer + vehicle (VIN, registration, odometer), technician, coverage plan applied.
Line itemsLabor rows (final laborHours×laborRate) + parts rows (returned parts credited via job_card_parts return moves); warranty/AMC coverage line as credit.
Tax linesVAT per dealer_taxes on net taxable amount.
Totals blockLabor total, parts total, coverage credit, tax, Amount Due GYD + USD.
FooterPayment method, receipt #, status issued|partially_paid|paid|void, exchange rate, terms.

R8.7 — GRA Duty Pack (Guyana Revenue Authority)

GRA = Guyana Revenue Authority (GYD; VAT 0/14%; import duty; EV duty exclusions) — not Ghana. Generator: pdfkit assembly of extracted-field summary + computed duty. Source: gra/extract (AI vision on documents.extraction jsonb — extracts legible fields only, never invents TIN/VIN/CIF/duty), dealer_taxes (server computes duty deterministically), gates (type gra_filing, evidence[], resolution), gra/filings. Delivery: generated only after the gra_filing human gate confirms figures; then email/outbox + stored in storage/objects (dealer-ACL). Enforces MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE — low-confidence extractions route to the human gate, never auto-file.

ZoneContents
HeaderDealer/logo/division; title GRA IMPORT DUTY PACK; filing ref (gra/filings); date (Guyana TZ).
Party / entity blockDealer TIN (from dealer config — never invented), importer/customer; vehicle: make/model/year, VIN, engineNumber (extracted + human-confirmed), CIF value.
Extracted fieldsAI-extracted legible fields (bill of lading, invoice value, CIF, cc/powertrain) each tagged with source doc + confidence; low-confidence flagged for gate review.
Tax / duty linesServer-computed from dealer_taxes: import duty %, VAT 0/14%, excise/levy; EV rows skipped where excludeEv=true. Each shows rate + base + GYD.
Totals blockCIF (GYD + USD), total duty, VAT, Total Payable to GRA in GYD (+ USD reference).
FooterGate resolution (gates.resolution) + confirming user, evidence[] doc references, exchange rate, declaration that figures were server-computed and human-confirmed.

Reference R9 — Corrected-Design Deltas (spec vs built app)

This appendix reconciles the granular AURA spec against the shipped application catalogued in DMS_Build_Audit.html. Each row states the defect as built, the correct mandate this spec enforces, a severity, and the load-bearing location. Severity legend: P0 = tenant-isolation / money / safety breach, ship-blocker; P1 = correctness or compliance defect, fix before GA; P2 = UX / IA / maintainability debt. All fixes inherit the global invariants: every query dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id; every AI call gated by isAgentEnabled, audited via recordAgentRun, PII-minimized, wrapped with guardUntrusted, and floored at MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE.

R9.1 — Tenant isolation & access-control deltas (P0)

AreaWhat the BUILT app does (defect)What the CORRECT spec mandatesSeverityWhere (file)
Concierge cross-tenant grounding leak The /api/anthropic/conversations SSE concierge and /api/copilotkit assemble grounding context (leads, deals, vehicles, dashboard rollups) from repository reads that omit the dealer predicate, so a CAM Motors user can be grounded on GT Automotive rows and the model narrates another tenant's pipeline. The RAG retrieval and the CopilotKit action readbacks trust clerkId alone, not activeDealerId. EVERY grounding read is dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) AND validated against the x-dealer-id header before a token is streamed. Concierge tools stay nav-only; any data tool re-filters by dealerId server-side. Grounding payloads pass guardUntrusted so injected lead/WhatsApp text cannot re-steer retrieval scope. Cross-dealer requests → 403, never a silent widen. P0 server/routes/anthropic.ts, server/routes/copilotkit.ts, server/agents/concierge/grounding.ts
Object-storage ACL bypass GCS objects behind the Replit sidecar are served via presigned URLs minted from the raw object path with no ownership check. /api/storage/objects/* and /api/documents/:id/download issue a URL for any key a caller can name; customer_documents, finance_documents, vehicles.documents[], and delivery signatureData are effectively cross-tenant readable by path guessing. Every storage read/write passes the ACL module keyed to the owning row's dealerId (resolved via the polymorphic documents parent or the entity's FK) before a presigned URL is minted; presign TTL is short and single-use. /api/storage/uploads/request-url stamps the dealer prefix server-side and rejects caller-supplied prefixes. Signature/PDI images and finance docs require the same dealer + RBAC field-permission gate as their parent entity. P0 server/storage/acl.ts, server/routes/storage.ts, server/routes/documents.ts
Audit-log not dealer-scoped /api/audit-logs and the agent_runs / activity / timeline_events readers return the platform-wide table; a dealer admin sees other dealers' agent runs, gate resolutions, and money events. Writes to audit-logs also omit dealerId, so records cannot be partitioned after the fact. Audit reads are dealer-scoped like every other query; dealerId is a NOT-NULL column stamped on write for audit_logs, agent_runs, activity, timeline_events, and gates. Only platform/* super-admin endpoints may read cross-dealer, and those are separately RBAC-gated and themselves audited. P0 server/routes/audit-logs.ts, server/services/audit.ts, server/db/schema/audit.ts
AUTH_BYPASS / impersonation headers A dev shortcut honours AUTH_BYPASS and an x-impersonate-user / x-dealer-id header pair to select identity and tenant without a verified Clerk session. The same header trust path exists in production builds, so a caller can set x-dealer-id to any dealer and act as any dealer_users row, defeating both auth and tenancy. x-dealer-id is ONLY honoured after the Clerk-verified clerkId is confirmed to have a dealer_users membership in that dealer; otherwise 403. AUTH_BYPASS is compiled out of prod (guarded by NODE_ENV + explicit build flag) and every bypass use is audited. Impersonation is a first-class, permissioned, fully-audited platform-admin action — never an unauthenticated header. P0 server/middleware/auth.ts, server/middleware/dealerScope.ts
PATCH /agents under-gated (kill-switch tampering) PATCH /api/agents/:id flips agents.status (active⇄paused) and thresholds with a coarse authenticated check but no dealer scoping and no module×category RBAC, so a low-privilege user in one dealer can pause/enable another dealer's agents — disabling the intake dedup or VIN-allocation kill-switch. Agent mutations require the agents module edit permission via role_permissions, are dealer-scoped, and every status/threshold change is written to audit_logs and timeline_events with before/after. The kill-switch (isAgentEnabled) reads the dealer-scoped row only. MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE overrides are admin-only. P0 server/routes/agents.ts, server/middleware/rbac.ts

R9.2 — AI safety & GRA compliance deltas (P0/P1)

AreaWhat the BUILT app does (defect)What the CORRECT spec mandatesSeverityWhere (file)
GRA = Ghana, not Guyana + LLM tax fabrication /api/gra/extract and the filing flow treat GRA as a Ghana tax authority and prompt the vision model to return computed duty/VAT and to fill in TIN, VIN, CIF, and duty values it cannot read. The model both extracts and computes tax, inventing amounts; there is no deterministic recompute and no human confirmation before a gra_filing is created. GRA is the Guyana Revenue Authority. AI EXTRACTS only legible fields from the document (never invents TIN/VIN/CIF/duty). The SERVER computes VAT (0% / 14%), import duty, excise, and EV duty exclusions deterministically from dealer_taxes (kind VAT|duty|excise|levy, percent|fixed, threshold, excludeEv) in GYD. A human gra_filing gate confirms before filing. Extraction runs under isAgentEnabled + recordAgentRun, and low-confidence fields drop to HITL review. P0 server/routes/gra.ts, server/agents/gra/extract.ts, server/services/tax/compute.ts
Confidence-floor unenforced Agents record agent_runs.confidence but act on the output regardless — A1 intake auto-creates/merges leads, A5 doc pre-fill writes fields, and persona/vehicle recommend surfaces IDs even when confidence is below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE. The threshold is logged, not gated. MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE is a hard gate: any run below it is routed to a human (HITL review queue / gates / draft state) and NEVER auto-applied. Autonomous-write agents (A1 create/merge, A5 pre-fill, A11 VIN allocation) additionally declare their write intent and are audited; below-floor writes are suppressed with a recorded reason. P1 server/agents/base/guard.ts, server/agents/intake/*.ts, server/agents/prefill/*.ts
Sentiment / untrusted-text guardUntrusted missing A6 call sentiment, the sentiment digest (/api/dashboard/sentiment), /leads/:id/calls/:id/suggest-sentiment, and the A7 outreach composer feed raw call_logs transcripts, whatsapp_messages, and inbound email bodies straight into the prompt with no injection wrapper, so a customer message can steer the model or poison the digest. ALL untrusted text (call transcripts, WhatsApp/email inbound, extracted document text, lead notes from webhooks) is wrapped with guardUntrusted before entering any prompt. Sentiment and outreach outputs are treated as suggestions, PII-minimized, audited, and floored by confidence. Digest aggregation excludes runs that failed the guard. P1 server/agents/sentiment/*.ts, server/agents/outreach/compose.ts, server/lib/guardUntrusted.ts
Autonomous-write agents undeclared A1 (lead create/merge), A5 (field pre-fill), and A11 (VIN allocation on deal commit) mutate tenant data without a declared, audited "autonomous write" contract; their runs look identical to read-only advisories in agent_runs, so operators cannot tell what the AI changed. Every autonomous-write agent declares runType as a write, records input/outputSummary, the affected entity IDs, and emits a timeline_events row with isAgent=true. Writes are reversible/traceable and gated by the kill-switch + confidence floor. P1 server/agents/registry.ts, server/services/audit.ts

R9.3 — Correctness & compliance deltas (P1)

AreaWhat the BUILT app does (defect)What the CORRECT spec mandatesSeverityWhere (file)
Hardcoded service tax 15% Service invoicing (/api/service-invoices, job-card invoice) applies a literal 15% tax constant instead of reading dealer_taxes, so Guyana VAT (0%/14%) and EV exclusions are wrong and diverge from the deterministic sales-side tax used by A3. Service tax is computed by the same deterministic engine that reads dealer_taxes (kind, percent|fixed, threshold, excludeEv) as quotes/deals; no literal rates anywhere. Money stays USD-scale internally, GYD on display via dealer.usdExchangeRate. P1 server/routes/service-invoices.ts, server/services/tax/compute.ts
Hardcoded avgResponseSeconds / fabricated agent metrics /api/agents/metrics and /api/dashboard/* return a hardcoded avgResponseSeconds and cosmetic successRate/tasksToday rather than aggregating real agent_runs.latency/status. Agent metrics are computed from dealer-scoped agent_runs (latency, status, confidence) over the window; no synthetic constants. Empty windows return zero/nulls honestly, not placeholder numbers. P1 server/routes/agents.ts, server/routes/dashboard.ts
Reports client-side aggregation Several of the 10 report types pull raw rows to the browser and aggregate/roll-up client-side, which leaks unfiltered records over the wire (bypassing role_field_permissions hidden/view/edit), is slow, and applies per-type RBAC only in the UI. All 10 reports aggregate server-side, dealer-scoped, with per-type RBAC enforced on the endpoint and role_field_permissions applied before serialization. The client receives only the rolled-up, permission-filtered result set. P1 server/routes/reports.ts, server/services/reports/*.ts
Missing first-class Case / Test-Drive / Review entities Test drives live as loose leads columns (testDriveToken/Licence/Waiver); customer issues/exceptions have no home; CSAT is a bare deliveries.feedbackRating/feedbackComment. There is no durable Case, Test-Drive, or Review/CSAT record with its own lifecycle, so they cannot be queried, RBAC-scoped, or audited independently. Promote Case, Test-Drive, and Review/CSAT to first-class dealer-scoped entities with their own status enums, timeline, RBAC, and endpoints. Test-Drive owns the token/licence/waiver/availability lifecycle (A10); Review/CSAT owns feedback beyond delivery; Case owns exceptions/complaints linked polymorphically to lead/deal/service_order. P1 server/db/schema/{cases,test_drives,reviews}.ts, server/routes/{cases,test-drives,reviews}.ts

R9.4 — Information-architecture & UX deltas (P2)

AreaWhat the BUILT app does (defect)What the CORRECT spec mandatesSeverityWhere (file)
Workflow / Run-Review redundancy The lead detail exposes both a Workflow advance panel and a separate "Run Review" panel that duplicate the stage-advance action against /leads/:id/advance and /leads/:id/review, producing two competing CTAs and confusion over which drives stage progression. ONE Advance CTA. Merge Run Review into the Workflow panel: readiness (the 422 {unmet:[]} from advance vs versioned stage_checklists) renders inline, and the single Advance button is the only stage-progression control. P2 client/src/pages/leads/LeadDetail.tsx, client/src/components/leads/WorkflowPanel.tsx
"Review" word overloaded "Review" labels three unrelated things: stage-advance readiness (/advance unmet checklist), approval gates (gates resolution), and agent-run inspection (/agents/runs/:id/review). Users cannot tell which action they are taking. Disambiguate into three named surfaces: Readiness (stage-advance checklist), Approvals (gates resolve — below_floor_price, credit_decline, capital_order, gra_filing, refund_release, stage_advance), and Approve-run (agent-run HITL review). No shared "Review" label. P2 client/src/components/{Readiness,Approvals,AgentRunReview}.tsx
Hidden Deals page The Deals list/detail exists in the router but is unlinked from primary navigation, so deals (desking→committed→delivered, below-floor gate, VIN-lock on commit, auto-created delivery) are only reachable from a lead, orphaning direct deal management. Deals is a first-class navigation destination with its own dealer-scoped list, filters, and detail — surfacing stage, otdPrice, discount/below-floor gate status, VIN-lock state, and the linked delivery. P2 client/src/App.tsx (nav), client/src/pages/deals/*.tsx
Workshop / Service overlap Two overlapping surfaces ("Workshop" and "Service") both render service_orders / job_cards with divergent filters and duplicate actions (/service-orders, /job-cards), splitting the same workflow across two IAs. Consolidate into one Service surface: service_orders (type/status/scheduledDate/complaint/odometer/technician/jobs) → job_cards (checklist/labor/status open→in_progress→completed) → job_card_parts (issue/return stock moves) → service_invoices, with a single navigation entry and coherent status flow. P2 client/src/pages/service/*.tsx, client/src/App.tsx (nav)

R9.5 — Remediation ordering

  • Fix P0 first, together: tenant isolation (concierge grounding, storage ACL, audit scope, auth-bypass/impersonation, PATCH /agents gating) and GRA fabrication are a single hardening pass — they share the dealerScope middleware, the ACL module, and the deterministic dealer_taxes engine.
  • Then P1: enforce MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE and universal guardUntrusted, declare autonomous-write agents, move service tax + reports to server-side deterministic aggregation, replace fabricated metrics, and stand up first-class Case / Test-Drive / Review entities.
  • Then P2: collapse Workflow/Run-Review to one Advance CTA, disambiguate the "Review" vocabulary into Readiness / Approvals / Approve-run, expose the Deals page, and unify Workshop/Service.
  • Every delta above is cross-referenced by Area to the corresponding finding in DMS_Build_Audit.html; this appendix is the authoritative spec-side resolution for each.

Platform & Dealer-Onboarding (Portal A)

Platform P1 — Two-Portal Architecture & Tenancy Super Admin / Platform

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • Two planes, one codebase. Control-plane = Platform Console (super-admin control surface that onboards & governs dealers, users, entitlements, agent kill-switches — see P2 dealer onboarding, P3 RBAC, P4 entitlements/flags). Data-plane = Dealer Workspace (the tenant surface dealer staff actually operate: leads, deals, service, finance — every downstream module section). The tenant unit = one row in dealers; there is no "customer account" tenancy — customers are data inside a dealer.
  • Identity is global, membership is per-dealer. A human is ONE row in users keyed by Clerk clerkId (email, name, avatar, isSuperAdmin derived from SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL). Their right to see a dealer lives in dealer_users(dealerId,userId,roleId,isGeneralManager,divisionId,reportingManagerUserId,lastLeadAssignedAt). One user may hold N memberships across dealers/divisions; each membership carries its own role + division + reporting line.
  • Request tenant-binding pipeline (runs on EVERY data-plane request):
    • 1. Clerk middleware verifies the session JWT → resolves clerkId. Unauthenticated → 401.
    • 2. JIT provisioning: on first-ever login, upsert the users row from Clerk claims. first-user-becomes-GM is retired — a freshly provisioned user with zero memberships is a valid global identity with NO dealer access until a super-admin (or dealer settings:admin) grants a dealer_users row.
    • 3. Load all dealer_users memberships for the user → the membership set.
    • 4. Read the x-dealer-id request header. Invariant: x-dealer-id ∈ membership set, else the request is rejected. If header absent → default-dealer resolution (below).
    • 5. Bind res.locals.activeDealerId = x-dealer-id, plus activeMembership (roleId, divisionId, isGeneralManager). This is the ONLY dealer identifier trusted downstream — body/query dealerId fields are ignored/overwritten server-side.
    • 6. Every ORM query (Drizzle) for tenant-owned tables is composed WHERE dealer_id = :activeDealerId at the repository layer — never optional, never client-supplied.
  • Default-dealer resolution (header omitted, e.g. first paint / deep-link): pick lastActiveDealerId from user prefs if still a valid membership; else if exactly one membership, use it; else return the membership list to the client for an explicit dealer-picker and DO NOT auto-bind (avoids silent wrong-tenant writes). Super-admin with zero dealer memberships defaults to the Console, not a workspace.
  • Division as the second axis. Within a bound dealer, divisions (CAM / GT) sub-scope assignment, reporting lines and some visibility. Division is a filter/attribution axis, NOT a hard isolation boundary — a dealer-scoped settings:admin can see across divisions; a division-scoped user sees their division's slice (see edge cases + P3 RBAC).
  • Cross-plane control. /platform/* is SUPER-ADMIN only and is not dealer-scoped by activeDealerId — it operates ON the control-plane tables (dealers, global users, cross-dealer memberships). /admin/* is dealer-scoped and gated by settings:admin within the bound dealer. See P2/P3/P4.
🔌 APIs & data (request → response)
METHOD pathRequestResponse / statusEntityRBAC

GET /api/audit-logsx-dealer-id; ?actor,entity,from,to200 audit rows filtered by activeDealerId (CORRECTION: audit-logs are tenant-scoped, no cross-dealer leak). Super-admin control-plane actions carry dealerId=null and are visible only in Console.audit_logsdealer settings:admin; platform rows → super-admin
⚙️ Agents / automation in play
  • No LLM agent runs in P1 itself — tenancy is deterministic middleware. But P1 defines the envelope every agent obeys: each agent invocation inherits activeDealerId and MUST scope reads/writes to it. recordAgentRun writes agent_runs.dealer_id = activeDealerId; a run with a null/mismatched dealer is rejected before model call.
  • Concierge / copilotkit (SSE nav-only assistant) is dealer-scoped like any other query (CORRECTION): its retrieval, tool calls and conversations/messages rows are constrained to activeDealerId; it cannot surface another tenant's data even for a super-admin browsing a specific dealer.
  • Kill-switch inheritance: per-dealer agents.status / isAgentEnabled and entitlements/feature-flags (managed in P4) are resolved against activeDealerId, so a suspended dealer disables all its agents automatically.
  • Object-storage ACL (GCS presigned URLs): every signed URL is minted only after confirming the object's owning dealer_id == activeDealerId (CORRECTION) — storage is a tenant boundary, not just DB rows.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Super-admin gets a read-only Console concierge for control-plane questions ("which dealers are suspended?", "how many members has CAM Motors?"). It queries control-plane tables directly and is the ONE place AI may span dealers — but only for super-admins, only for governance metadata (dealer/user/entitlement counts), never tenant business data.
  • All governance-facing text (dealer notes, member invite messages) passes guardUntrusted before any model sees it (CORRECTION: guardUntrusted on ALL untrusted text), and any AI suggestion honors MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE with a human gate.
  • No model call may fabricate tenancy facts (memberships, roles, exchange rate). AI here explains/aggregates what the deterministic tables already say; writes remain human-driven via the endpoints above.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Isolation invariant (P1 prime directive): for tenant-owned tables, NO query is issued without dealer_id = activeDealidId in the predicate — enforced at the repository layer, asserted in tests. Applies to leads, deals, customers, service, finance, storage, audit_logs, conversations/messages (concierge) alike.
  • x-dealer-id ∈ memberships is checked on every data-plane request. Failure semantics (CORRECTION): a dealer id the caller has NO membership to → 404 at the data-plane (no existence leak — never reveal that a dealer exists). 403 is used ONLY where membership exists but the role/permission is insufficient (RBAC denial, see P3).
  • Global vs scoped keys: users.clerkId unique globally; dealer_users unique on (dealerId,userId); a user may repeat across dealers. isSuperAdmin is derived, not stored as a grant — flipping SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL is the source of truth.
  • Money/date context inherited per tenant: display GYD = stored USD × dealers.usdExchangeRate (~209); dates render in America/Guyana (GMT-4). Changing usdExchangeRate affects display only and never re-scales stored USD values.
  • Division field: divisions ∈ {CAM, GT} per dealer; dealer_users.divisionId may be null (dealer-wide) or set (division-scoped). Round-robin (A2) and reporting lines read this axis.
  • SLA: /auth/me p95 < 300 ms (cached memberships/permissions per session, invalidated on membership change). Tenant-binding middleware adds < 5 ms. Membership revocation takes effect on next request (no stale-session grant).
  • Audited impersonation only (CORRECTION): raw AUTH_BYPASS / x-test-user-email are retired. A super-admin who needs to view a dealer as a user does so via an explicit, audited impersonation grant that writes audit_logs with actor=super-admin, subject=user, dealerId=target — never a silent header override.

📄 Documents / artifacts
  • /auth/me bootstrap payload — canonical identity+tenancy+permission envelope consumed by the SPA to render either Console or Workspace and to gate UI.
  • Dealer roster export (Console) — dealers with status/city/exchange rate/createdBy for governance review.
  • Membership grant/revoke records in audit_logs (actor, subject user, dealerId, roleId, divisionId, timestamp).
  • Impersonation session log — audited super-admin-as-user entries (CORRECTION: no raw bypass), tenant-scoped.
  • Tenant-scoped audit trail — audit_logs per dealer for compliance; platform-level rows (dealerId=null) segregated to Console.
  • PENDING-INFRA seams referenced but no-op here: encryption-at-rest, backups/DR, tracing (lib/infra-seams.ts) — tenancy invariants are designed to hold once those land.

Platform P2 — Dealer Onboarding & Provisioning Super Admin

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • 0 · Auth to control-plane. Super Admin signs in via Clerk; auth/me resolves clerkId→users and confirms email ∈ SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL. Platform Console (portal A, control-plane) is the ONLY surface exposing /platform/*; dealer staff never see it (see P1 for portal-split & JIT model). No activeDealerId is required for /platform/* — these are cross-tenant control-plane routes gated purely on super-admin identity.
  • 1 · Pre-flight dedupe. Console calls a name/city existence probe before create; A1-style deterministic normalize (trim, casefold, collapse whitespace) on (name,country) surfaces near-duplicates ("CAM Motors" vs "Cam Motors Inc") as a warn, not a block.
  • 2 · Create dealer (tenant root). POST /platform/dealers with name, city, country=GY, usdExchangeRate, status='draft'. Server stamps createdBy=users.id, generates tenant id. This row is the root every data-plane query scopes to via activeDealerId(res)+x-dealer-id (see P3 tenancy guard). All of steps 3–9 run inside ONE provisioning transaction with a compensation log so a mid-way failure leaves NO half-tenant.
  • 3 · Divisions + showrooms. Seed divisions rows (CAM, GT) — the mandatory second scoping axis. Each division carries showroom/location metadata + timezone default America/Guyana (GMT-4).
  • 4 · Seed reference data. Batch /admin/* seeding under the new tenant: lead_sources (Walk-in, Meta Lead Ads, WhatsApp, Referral, IMAP email), dealer_taxes (VAT + GRA import duty bands — server-computed later, see F-GRA), stage_checklists (versioned; drives the 422 unmet-gate on /leads/:id/advance, see L2), email templates (email_logs template registry), and roles+role_permissions+role_field_permissions from a canonical starter matrix.
  • 5 · Agent registry + kill-switches. Insert one agents row per real agent (A1,A2,A3,A5,A6,A7,A10,A11,A15, Sentiment-digest, Persona-recommend, Concierge, GRA-extract) with status='disabled', isAgentEnabled=false, and per-agent MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE defaults. Onboarding ships agents OFF; go-live enables selectively (governance-gated PATCH, see A0).
  • 6 · Entitlements / feature-flags. Seed per-dealer entitlement + feature-flag rows and the PENDING-INFRA no-op seams from lib/infra-seams.ts (backups/DR, encryption-at-rest, uptime, tracing, message-broker) so downstream code has stable seams.
  • 7 · Brand setup. Upload logo/colors to object storage via storage/* presigned PUT; the object ACL is dealer-scoped (correction: object-storage ACL scoped per tenant — no cross-tenant read). Brand feeds PDF headers (quotes/invoices/delivery, pdfkit) and Console theming.
  • 8 · Invite first GM. POST /platform/dealers/:id/members with email, roleId(GM), isGeneralManager=true, divisionId. Users are GLOBAL — if the email has no users row yet, a pending membership is written and Clerk JIT provisions the users row on first login (first-user-becomes-GM heuristic is RETIRED; GM is explicitly assigned here).
  • 9 · Data migration (optional). Vehicle inventory import via POST /vehicles/import (xlsx) against vehicles/import/template; runs under the new tenant scope, dry-run→commit. See V1.
  • 10 · Go-live checklist & cutover. Console runs a computed readiness gate (a gates entity of kind dealer_go_live): currency set, ≥1 tax, ≥1 lead_source, stage_checklists present, GM invite ACCEPTED, sandbox connectors validated. On all-green, PATCH /platform/dealers/:id flips status draft→sandbox→live and swaps LOS/WhatsApp/email connectors from sandbox to live credentials. Every step writes audit_logs.

⚙️ Agents / automation in play
  • Deterministic dedupe (A1-flavored): name normalization on the pre-flight probe — no LLM, pure string canonicalization; surfaces likely-duplicate dealers as a soft warning.
  • Provisioning transaction + compensation: steps 2–9 wrapped so any failure triggers reverse-order compensating deletes (members→flags→agents→roles→checklists→taxes→sources→divisions→dealer). Emits activity+audit_logs per step; a webhook_events/broker seam (PENDING-INFRA no-op) records the outbox intent.
  • Agent registry seeding: every agent inserted OFF (isAgentEnabled=false) with default MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE; go-live selectively enables via governance PATCH only.
  • Clerk JIT hook: first login of an invited GM materializes the global users row and activates the pending dealer_users membership.
  • Email outbox: GM invite dispatched through nodemailer Gmail SMTP with retry/backoff/dedupe; delivery state tracked in email_logs.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • No autonomous AI writes during onboarding. All seeding is deterministic/config — appropriate because provisioning must be reproducible and auditable.
  • Assist-only (optional, HITL): a Concierge (SSE + copilotkit, nav-only — never mutates) can walk the Super Admin through the go-live checklist. Every call still runs isAgentEnabled → guardUntrusted on any pasted dealer text → PII-minimize → recordAgentRun in agent_runs, and respects MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE. Concierge queries are dealer-scoped even in the control-plane (correction).
  • Tax/duty AI boundary: GRA duty bands seeded here are SERVER-COMPUTED at runtime; onboarding never lets AI fabricate rates — AI only extracts legible values from uploaded rate sheets, behind a human gate (see F-GRA).
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • dealers.usdExchangeRate required > 0 (typ. ~209); money stored USD-scale, GYD display = USD×rate. UNSET or ≤0 → blocks go-live.
  • country = GY for Guyana tenants; drives GRA duty engine + America/Guyana GMT-4 timezone default.
  • status enum draft → sandbox → live (also suspended); flip requires Idempotency-Key + passing go-live gate.
  • divisions ≥1 (CAM/GT) mandatory — second scoping axis; every data-plane row carries a divisionId.
  • GM membership exactly one isGeneralManager=true per dealer at go-live; email valid; roleId ∈ seeded roles; divisionId ∈ seeded divisions.
  • Validation registry (Zod, contract-first from OpenAPI): VIN & engineNumber 17-char; registration ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$ — enforced when vehicle import runs.
  • Go-live gate (all required): usdExchangeRate set · ≥1 dealer_tax · ≥1 lead_source · stage_checklists version present · GM invite ACCEPTED · sandbox connectors (LOS Demerara, WhatsApp, email) validated.
  • SLA: dealer create < 2s; full provision transaction < 30s; GM invite email dispatch < 60s (outbox retry ≤ 24h); import dry-run report < 10s / 5k rows.

📄 Documents / artifacts
  • Provisioning audit trail — full audit_logs chain (dealer create → each seed → GM invite → go-live flip), dealer-scoped, exportable.
  • Go-live readiness report — computed gates snapshot (kind dealer_go_live) with resolved/unmet items and timestamps.
  • GM invitation email — templated (email_logs), Clerk JIT accept link; delivery/retry state tracked.
  • Vehicle import template + results — vehicles/import/template xlsx and the dry-run/commit report (inserted/skipped/errors).
  • Brand asset set — logo + palette in dealer-scoped object storage; consumed by pdfkit headers on quotes/invoices/delivery docs.
  • Seeded config snapshot — starter RBAC matrix, lead_sources, dealer_taxes (VAT+duty bands), stage_checklists versions, agent registry with kill-switch/confidence defaults.

Platform P3 — Dealer Lifecycle, Entitlements & Offboarding Super Admin / Platform Finance

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • Plan → entitlement resolution. Super-admin (SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL, see P1) attaches a subscription plan to a dealers row; plan expands into a per-tenant entitlement set = {modules enabled × RBAC categories, agents enabled, seat limit, usage caps, feature-flags}. Entitlements are the OUTER gate; RBAC role_permissions (P2) is the INNER gate. Effective access = entitlement.module ∈ plan AND role_permissions(module×category) allows AND agents.status='active' && isAgentEnabled. Both gates dealer-scoped; entitlement lookup keyed by activeDealerId(res).
  • Feature-flag / agent kill-switch write. Toggling an agent flips agents.status (draft|active|paused|retired) + isAgentEnabled for that dealerId only; every downstream AI call already checks isAgentEnabled before recordAgentRun, so a paused agent short-circuits with a no-op reason (never a 500). PATCH on the agent governance surface is gated at governance level, NOT plain edit (CORRECTION).
  • Usage metering tick. Each metered event (agent_run created, message sent, seat provisioned via JIT, storage object written) appends to an append-only usage counter partitioned by dealerId + period(month, America/Guyana GMT-4). Meter reads never touch data-plane tables directly — they aggregate agent_runs, email_logs, whatsapp_messages, dealer_users, storage seams server-side (reports aggregate server-side — CORRECTION).
  • Suspend. Super-admin sets dealers.status='suspended'. Effect = read-only lockout preserving data: middleware maps method→category (P2) and forces every write category (create|edit|delete|admin) to 423 Locked for that tenant while GET/view stays allowed; all agents forced to paused (kill-switch), outbox/webhook workers skip suspended tenants, scheduled reminders (bookings/service/coverage) suppressed. No rows mutated, no cascade.
  • Reactivate. status→'active'; entitlements re-resolved from current plan (which may have changed while suspended), agents restored to their pre-suspension agents.status snapshot, outbox drains queued-but-held items with dedupe so nothing double-sends.
  • Offboarding. (1) Request export → server takes a consistent snapshot (single serializable read txn / point-in-time label) across all ~57 tables for one dealerId; (2) apply retention policy; (3) either anonymize PII (tombstone keep-shape) or hard-delete after grace window — blocked while any gates open, any legal-hold flag set, or unreconciled money (open invoices/payments/receipts). Every lifecycle transition writes audit_logs (dealer-scoped) + timeline_events.

Cross-tenant note: platform endpoints are control-plane and legitimately enumerate dealers for the super-admin. Any data-plane probe carrying a foreign x-dealer-id ∉ memberships → 404 (no existence leak); 403 only where membership itself is the check (CORRECTION).

⚙️ Agents / automation in play
  • Entitlement enforcement is the master kill-switch fabric. Disabling a module or agent at plan level flips agents.status/isAgentEnabled so A1 Intake, A2 Round-robin, A3 Quote, A5 Doc pre-fill, A6 Sentiment, A7 Outreach, A10/A11, A15 Pipeline, GRA extract all no-op cleanly for that dealer (governance short-circuit before recordAgentRun).
  • Suspend pauses every agent + freezes email outbox (retry/backoff/dedupe respected — no lost sends, no double sends on reactivate) and WhatsApp/Twilio senders for the tenant.
  • Usage-cap breach automation: when a metered counter crosses its cap, the relevant agent auto-pauses (soft-limit) and raises a platform notification rather than silently erroring; hard-cap → 423 on the feature.
  • Export/offboard jobs run through the PENDING-INFRA message-broker seam (lib/infra-seams.ts) — today synchronous/queued no-op seam, swappable to real broker without contract change.
  • No agent fabricates lifecycle data: export = verbatim snapshot; anonymization = deterministic tombstoning, never AI-generated. GRA duty stays server-computed (CORRECTION) and is unaffected by offboarding transforms.
🧠 AI for the persona
  • Advisory only, HITL, guardUntrusted-wrapped. A copilot summary (Concierge SSE, nav/read-only, dealer-scoped incl. concierge — CORRECTION) can narrate "Dealer CAM Motors: plan Growth, 4/5 seats, 812 agent-runs this month, 3 open gates — offboarding would be blocked." All figures pulled from server aggregates, never invented.
  • Downgrade impact brief: AI drafts a plain-language "what breaks if you disable module X" note by reading open counts (leads mid-stage, open deals, unbilled service_orders); MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE ENFORCED (CORRECTION) — below threshold it returns "insufficient signal, review manually" rather than a guess. Every call: isAgentEnabled + PII-minimize + recordAgentRun audit.
  • No AI action mutates dealers.status, entitlements, deletion, or legal-hold — those are super-admin-only, permissioned, audited writes.
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • dealers.status ∈ {active, suspended, offboarding, closed}; transitions validated by state machine (422 on illegal, e.g. closed→active forbidden). usdExchangeRate > 0 (money = USD-scale, GYD = USD×rate ~209; rate frozen into snapshot at export time for reproducibility).
  • Entitlement modules must be a subset of plan ceiling (422 if exceeds). Feature-flags boolean; agent flags map 1:1 to agents.status. Seat limit checked at JIT provisioning (P1) — over-limit login → membership creation refused with clear reason, not a crash.
  • Export snapshot = point-in-time consistent across all ~57 tables (serializable read); each table emitted with row count + checksum; presigned GCS URL TTL-bounded and dealer-ACL scoped (object-storage ACL dealer-scoped — CORRECTION).
  • Retention / deletion: anonymize mode nulls/tokenizes PII columns (customers, contacts, customer_documents, users email/phone, whatsapp_messages, call_logs, comm_notes) while preserving referential shape + aggregate history; delete mode removes after retentionDays grace. Both irreversible → require confirmName == dealer name and idempotency key.
  • Legal-hold is a hard veto: while set, offboard/delete/anonymize → 409; hold recorded with caseRef in audit_logs.
  • SLA: suspend/reactivate effective <1s (middleware flag). Export ready target <15 min small tenant. Every transition emits audit_logs + timeline_events synchronously (durability = PENDING-INFRA backup/DR seam). Dates/timestamps America/Guyana GMT-4.

📄 Documents / artifacts
  • Data export bundle — per-dealer, all ~57 tables as JSONL + CSV, manifest {snapshotAt, usdExchangeRate frozen, per-table rowCount + checksum}, delivered via presigned GCS URL (dealer-ACL scoped, TTL-bound).
  • Entitlement / plan snapshot — JSON of {planCode, modules, agents, featureFlags, seatLimit, usageCaps} at each change, retained in audit trail.
  • Usage / metering statement — server-aggregated monthly (America/Guyana), for Platform Finance billing reconciliation (no payment gateway — ledger only).
  • Lifecycle audit trail — audit_logs + timeline_events rows for suspend/reactivate/downgrade/legal-hold/offboard with actor, before/after, reason, caseRef.
  • Offboarding certificate — attestation of anonymize|delete completion, tables affected, PII columns tombstoned, retention window, legal-hold clearance.

Platform P4 — Super-Admin Operations & Cross-Tenant Governance Super Admin

🚦 Flow (lowest level)
  • Identity gate. Super-admin is not a dealer role — it is resolved server-side by comparing the Clerk-verified email to SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL (allow-list, comma-split, case-folded). No dealer_users membership is required or consulted; super-admin operates on the control-plane only. Every /platform/* and /audit-logs (cross-tenant read) request re-checks this on each call (stateless) — a stale session that lost the flag 401→re-auths, never silently downgrades.
  • Platform Console entry. Super-admin lands on the Platform Console (Portal A, see P1), NOT the Dealer Workspace. There is no activeDealerId bound to the session by default; control-plane queries are explicitly cross-tenant and audited. To act as a dealer, super-admin must either (a) hold a real membership, or (b) open an audited impersonation session (below) — never via a raw header.
  • Platform users & roles. Super-admin lists/creates GLOBAL users (GET/POST /platform/users), assigns per-dealer membership+role via POST /platform/dealers/:id/members (see P2 for dealer onboarding). Users are global (users.clerkId); role/division/reporting live in dealer_users. First-user-becomes-GM is retired — GM is set explicitly via isGeneralManager on membership.
  • Agent-policy library. Super-admin authors platform-default agent governance per domain (intake, quote, doc, outreach, call, VIN, pipeline, GRA…): default isAgentEnabled (kill-switch), MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE floor, and a domain never-list (actions the agent may never auto-execute, always HITL). These defaults are pushed to every dealer as the ceiling; a dealer settings:admin (see D-Admin) may tighten but never loosen below the platform floor. Governance-level writes go through PATCH /agents (governance-gated), which fans out to agents.status / agents config rows per dealer.
  • Global incident kill-switch. One control-plane action (PATCH /agents with scope=global) sets agents.status='paused' for ALL agents across ALL dealers atomically (single txn), writes an audit_logs entry + agent_runs is short-circuited: any in-flight recordAgentRun checks isAgentEnabled at execution time and aborts with a governance-halt outcome. Deterministic agents (A2 round-robin, A3 tax math) MAY be exempted per policy since they invoke no LLM — configurable on the incident action.
  • Audited impersonation (support access). Super-admin opens POST /platform/impersonation {dealerId, targetUserId, reason(required), ttlMinutes(≤ policy max)}. Server mints a short-lived, time-boxed impersonation grant, writes audit_logs (actor=super-admin, subject=targetUser, dealer, reason, expiry), and issues a session that behaves as that dealer_user — every downstream data-plane call is tagged impersonatedBy and re-audited. This REPLACES the retired raw AUTH_BYPASS / x-test-user-email defect. On expiry or explicit DELETE /platform/impersonation/:id, the grant is revoked and a close-out audit row written.
  • Cross-tenant audit. Super-admin reads GET /audit-logs WITHOUT a dealer scope (control-plane privilege) — the only path where dealer-scoping is intentionally lifted, and that lift is itself audited (read-of-audit is logged). Dealer-plane callers hitting the same endpoint are hard-scoped to their activeDealerId.
  • Observability / health. Super-admin views platform health (GET /platform/health): agent-run error rates (from agent_runs), outbox/email backlog (email_logs), webhook DLQ depth (webhook_events), and PENDING-INFRA seam status (DR/backup/tracing/broker/encryption) surfaced from lib/infra-seams.ts as declared no-op stubs with clear "not-yet-wired" flags — never faked as green.

⚙️ Agents / automation in play
  • Policy fan-out. A PATCH /agents at scope=global|domain writes the platform-default row then fans out effective config to every dealer's agents rows in one transaction; dealer overrides are re-clamped to the new floor (any override looser than the floor is snapped up, logged as an auto-correction in audit_logs).
  • Governance interceptor. Every agent (A1 Intake/Dedup, A2 Round-robin, A3 Quote, A5 Doc pre-fill, A6 Call sentiment, A7 Outreach, A10 Test-drive avail, A11 VIN, A15 Pipeline, Sentiment digest, Persona/vehicle recommend, Concierge, GRA extract) passes through isAgentEnabled → recordAgentRun → guardUntrusted → MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE. Global incident pause flips isAgentEnabled false at the source so in-flight runs abort with outcome=governance_halt.
  • Deterministic exemptions. A2 (round-robin assignment) and A3 (tax math + pdfkit) invoke no LLM; the incident kill-switch can optionally leave them live so pipeline routing and quote totals keep working during an AI incident — a per-action toggle, defaulting to pause-all for safety.
  • Impersonation tagging. While a support session is active, every automation triggered runs with impersonatedBy=super-admin stamped into agent_runs and audit_logs so AI actions taken during support are attributable and never anonymous.
  • Auto-expiry watchdog. A control-plane sweep closes impersonation grants past expiresAt, revokes the session, and emits a close-out audit — no long-lived support backdoor can persist (replaces the always-on AUTH_BYPASS defect).
🧠 AI for the persona
  • No autonomous AI on the control-plane. Super-admin governance actions (policy, kill-switch, impersonation) are deliberately human-driven and non-AI — governance must not be model-mediated. PATCH /agents is gated at the governance level, not exposed to any agent.
  • AI-assisted incident triage (read-only). Platform health may surface a summarized anomaly digest over agent_runs (spike in guardBlocks / belowConfidence / failures) using the Concierge in nav-only, read-only mode (SSE, see the Concierge section) — it can point the super-admin at the incident but can NEVER pull the kill-switch itself; every suggestion is advisory and the action stays a human click. guardUntrusted applies to any dealer-sourced text that reaches it.
  • Cross-tenant scoping still applies to AI. Per CORRECTIONS, even the Concierge is dealer-scoped for data-plane content; on the control-plane its inputs are limited to aggregate metrics, never raw dealer PII (PII-minimize before any model call).
🔑 Key fields · validations · SLA
  • Super-admin resolution: email ∈ SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL (env, case-folded, comma-list); re-checked per request, never cached to the JWT claims alone. Never inferred from any dealer_users row.
  • Impersonation: reason required (≥10 chars, stored verbatim in audit); ttlMinutes ≤ platform max (default 60); grant is single-dealer, single-target; cannot impersonate another super-admin; self-impersonation rejected 422.
  • Agent policy: minConfidence ∈ [0,1] and must be ≥ platform floor for that domain (dealer tighten-only); neverList is union of platform + dealer (dealer may add, never remove a platform entry); status ∈ {enabled, paused}. Loosen-below-floor → 422 {unmet:['min_confidence_below_floor'|'never_list_removal']}.
  • Cross-tenant audit read: the ONLY intentional lift of dealer-scoping; the lift is itself written to audit_logs (meta-audit). Dealer callers on /audit-logs are hard-scoped — a dealerId param outside their scope is IGNORED, not honored.
  • Money/locale invariants unchanged: control-plane surfaces are dealer-agnostic; any per-dealer figure shown respects that dealer's usdExchangeRate (GYD = USD×rate) and America/Guyana GMT-4 — never a global rate.
  • PENDING-INFRA seams (lib/infra-seams.ts): DR/backup, encryption-at-rest, uptime/tracing, message broker are declared no-op stubs; health reports them state:'pending-infra' — must render as amber "not wired", never green.
  • SLA: global incident pause fan-out completes ≤2s (single txn); impersonation grant minted ≤500ms; audit reads paginated (cursor) — never full-table scan; policy fan-out is idempotent (re-apply same policy = no-op, no dup audit).

Policy fan-out partially fails (one dealer row errors)Transaction rollback on any failureAtomic — either all dealers updated or none; error surfaced, no partial floor state; retry idempotentSuper AdminNo
Incident runbook: AI provider outage (claude-sonnet-4-6 5xx spike)Health shows failure/latency spike in agent_runsSuper-admin pulls global kill-switch (LLM agents paused, A2/A3 optionally exempt); post-incident re-enable is explicit + audited; digest summarizes affected runs for replaySuper AdminYes — human pulls & restores switch
Dealer tries to remove a platform neverList entryPolicy validator compares against platform union422 {unmet:['never_list_removal']} — dealer may only add HITL constraintsPlatformNo
Two super-admins issue conflicting global switch nearly simultaneouslyRow-version / last-write on agents global rowSerialized in txn; both writes audited with actor+timestamp; final state is last committed; no lost-updatePlatformNo
📄 Documents / artifacts
  • Audit ledger export — cursor-paginated audit_logs extract (actor, subject, dealer, action, reason, before/after, timestamp GMT-4), cross-tenant when generated by super-admin; the export action is itself audited.
  • Agent-policy library snapshot — the current platform-default matrix (domain × {status, minConfidence floor, neverList}) plus each dealer's effective clamped policy and override source.
  • Impersonation session log — every support grant: super-admin, target dealer_user, dealer, reason, opened/closed/TTL, and the list of actions taken under impersonation (from audit_logs filtered by impersonatedBy).
  • Platform health / incident report — agent error rates, outbox & webhook DLQ depth, PENDING-INFRA seam states, and the incident timeline (pause → mitigation → restore) with kill-switch audit rows.
  • Governance change audit — immutable trail of every PATCH /agents at global/domain scope with actor, reason, and fan-out result count.

Platform P5 — Isolation Guarantees & Platform API Index

This section is the authoritative reference for the control-plane surface (/platform/*, super-admin only) and the dealer settings surface (/admin/*, dealer settings:admin), and for the tenant-isolation invariants that every other section depends on. Isolation is not a feature of one module — it is a property of the request pipeline that all 163 paths inherit. See P1 for the tenancy data model (dealers, dealer_users, divisions), P2/P3 for RBAC and JIT provisioning, P4 for entitlements/feature-flags/kill-switches, and R2 for how data-plane modules consume activeDealerId(res).

1. Control-plane vs data-plane — the two portals

AURA is split into a Platform Console (control-plane: onboards and governs dealers) and a Dealer Workspace (data-plane: the tenant staff's operational system). The two planes never share a scoping authority. The control-plane authorizes off SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL and the global users table; the data-plane authorizes off dealer_users membership resolved into activeDealerId(res).

PropertyControl-plane (/platform/*)Data-plane (everything else)
Authorizing identitySuper-admin (email ∈ SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL)Signed-in Clerk user with a row in dealer_users for the target dealer
Scoping keyNone — operates across all tenants by designactiveDealerId(res) injected from validated x-dealer-id
Membership checkNot applicable (global operator)x-dealer-id MUST ∈ caller memberships, else 403
RBAC evaluated againstFixed super-admin capabilityrole_permissions (module×category) + role_field_permissions
Cross-tenant read of a foreign rowAllowed (that is the job)404 — no existence leak (see §5)
Object-storage namespaceMay list any dealers/{id}/ prefixLocked to dealers/{activeDealerId}/
Audit targetaudit_logs with actorType=super_admin, dealerId=null or affected dealeraudit_logs always stamped with dealerId=activeDealerId

2. Secret & credential separation between planes

A compromised dealer-scoped token must never reach control-plane authority, and a dealer's integration credentials must never be reachable from another dealer's session. Secrets are partitioned by plane and by tenant.

Secret / credentialOwned byReachable fromIsolation rule
SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL, platform service credsControl-plane envServer process onlyNever sent to any browser; never resolvable by dealer role
Clerk backend key / clerkId mappingGlobal auth layerAuth middleware onlyIdentity is global; authorization is per-plane
dealer.usdExchangeRatePer-dealer (dealers)Data-plane, own tenantRead via activeDealerId; drives GYD = USD×rate (~209) display only
WhatsApp Meta / Twilio / Gmail SMTP+IMAP credsPer-dealer entitlement config (P4)Data-plane, own tenant onlyWebhook events routed to a dealer, then all follow-up scoped; see R7 (comms)
LOS Demerara live/sandbox keysPer-dealer finance configFinance module, own tenantlos_submissions and finance_connector scoped by activeDealerId; see R5
GCS presigned URL signingPlatform storage serviceServer issues; client receives short-lived URLSigner refuses keys outside dealers/{activeDealerId}/ (§4)
Anthropic API key (claude-sonnet-4-6)Platform envServer-side agent runtime onlyNever client-exposed; every call gated by isAgentEnabled + MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE, audited via recordAgentRun
idempotency keys (money)Per-dealer (idempotency_keys)Data-plane, own tenantKey uniqueness scoped by dealerId so two tenants cannot collide; see R5
PENDING-INFRA seams (backups/DR, encryption-at-rest, tracing, broker)lib/infra-seams.tsServer, no-op todaySeams accept dealerId so isolation semantics are preserved when implemented

3. Tenant-isolation invariants — enforcement checklist

Each invariant below is a MUST. A violation is a P0 security defect, not a bug. These are the guarantees every data-plane section (R2–R9) relies on rather than re-implementing.

#InvariantEnforced atFailure mode if absent
I1activeDealerId(res) is injected on every data-plane request by middleware — no handler reads x-dealer-id rawTenancy middlewareHandler forgets scope → cross-tenant read
I2x-dealer-id header MUST resolve to a row in the caller's dealer_users; otherwise 403Tenancy middlewareAny user impersonates any dealer
I3Every DB query on a tenant-owned table is filtered by dealerId = activeDealerId — including joins and sub-selectsRepository layerForeign rows leak via relations
I4A read/write targeting a row that exists but belongs to another dealer returns 404 (no existence leak), never 403, at the data-planeRepository + handlerEnumeration reveals foreign IDs
I5403 is returned ONLY where membership itself is the check (I2) or RBAC denies a permitted-tenant actionMiddleware / RBACMixing 403/404 leaks which rows exist
I6Object-storage keys are namespaced dealers/{activeDealerId}/…; the presigner rejects any other prefix (corrected: storage ACL is dealer-scoped)Storage serviceCross-tenant document/asset access
I7/audit-logs reads are scoped to activeDealerId (corrected: audit-logs are dealer-scoped for dealer users)Audit handlerOne dealer reads another's audit trail
I8Concierge (SSE + copilotkit, nav-only) is dealer-scoped — its context, retrieval, and suggestions all filter by activeDealerId (corrected)Concierge runtimeAI surfaces another tenant's data
I9Every AI call PII-minimizes, runs guardUntrusted on ALL untrusted text, checks isAgentEnabled, enforces MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE, and writes agent_runs via recordAgentRun — all stamped with dealerIdAgent runtimePrompt-injection / cross-tenant context / silent low-confidence output
I10Division axis (CAM/GT) is a second filter within a dealer, never a substitute for I3Repository layerDivision scoping mistaken for tenant scoping
I11Money idempotency keys are unique per dealer; a replayed key from another tenant never matchesidempotency_keys constraintCross-tenant idempotency collision
I12Impersonation is audited and role-based (corrected: raw AUTH_BYPASS / x-test-user-email retired); every impersonated action writes audit_logs with the real actorAuth + auditUntraceable privileged access
I13Reports and dashboards aggregate server-side under activeDealerId — clients never receive foreign rows to aggregate (corrected)Reports serviceRow-level leak through analytics
I14Webhook events (Meta/WhatsApp/Twilio) are resolved to exactly one dealer before any data-plane write; unresolvable events are quarantined in webhook_eventsWebhook routerInbound message written to wrong tenant
I15PATCH /agents (kill-switch / status) is gated at governance level, not ordinary edit (corrected)RBAC (governance)Line staff disable/enable agents

4. Object-storage namespacing (I6 detail)

  • All keys follow dealers/{dealerId}/{module}/{entityId}/{filename} — e.g. dealers/CAM/customer_documents/…, dealers/GT/deliveries/pdi/….
  • Presigned-URL issuance (/storage/*) derives the prefix from activeDealerId(res); a client-supplied key outside that prefix is rejected before signing.
  • Applies to customer_documents, finance_documents, documents, delivery PDI photos, quote/invoice/PDI PDFs (pdfkit), and vehicle import artifacts.
  • GRA extract inputs/outputs are stored under the owning dealer's prefix; the GRA vision extract (Guyana) reads only legible fields and never fabricates — duty is server-computed and human-gated (see R6).

These are data-plane admin endpoints: fully scoped by activeDealerId, requiring the settings module at admin category in role_permissions. They configure the tenant, not the platform.

Note: Per-dealer agent kill-switches and feature-flags are governed under P4; PATCH /agents and /agents/runs/:id/review are gated at governance level (I15), distinct from settings:admin. Do not conflate them.

7. Cross-tenant negative-test suite — attempts that MUST fail

These are contract tests the enterprise build MUST keep green. Each asserts the exact status and the absence of any foreign data in the body. "404 (no leak)" means the body is a generic not-found, never a 403 that would confirm the row exists.

#AttemptRequired resultInvariant
N1Dealer-A user sends x-dealer-id: B where B ∉ memberships403I2, I5
N2Dealer-A user requests GET /customers/:id where the customer belongs to dealer B404 (no leak)I3, I4
N3Dealer-A user requests GET /leads/:id for a B-owned lead, then tries POST /leads/:id/advance404 on bothI3, I4
N4Dealer-A user requests a presigned URL for key dealers/B/customer_documents/x.pdf403/reject at signerI6
N5Dealer-A user fetches a GCS object using a URL presigned for dealer B403/expired-scopeI6
N6Dealer-A user calls GET /audit-logs and expects to see B's actionsOnly A's rows (B absent)I7
N7Dealer-A user asks concierge (SSE) about "customer Ramnarine at GT Automotive" while in CAM tenantNo B data; retrieval scoped to AI8
N8Prompt-injection payload in an inbound WhatsApp/email body attempts to exfiltrate other-tenant context via an agentNeutralized by guardUntrusted; nothing cross-tenant surfacedI9
N9Agent output below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE is auto-applied without HITLBlocked; routed to human gate / gatesI9
N10Ordinary dealer user (no governance) calls PATCH /agents/:id to flip isAgentEnabled403I15
N11Dealer-A user calls any /platform/* endpoint (not super-admin)403§1, §5
N12Dealer-A user replays a money idempotency key that dealer B used on POST /paymentsNo match; A processes independentlyI11
N13Dealer-A user forces a division filter divisionId=GT to reach a B row404 (dealer filter still applies first)I3, I10
N14Request omits x-dealer-id entirely on a data-plane path400/403 — no default tenantI1, I2
N15Caller uses retired x-test-user-email / AUTH_BYPASS to impersonateRejected; only audited role-based impersonation permittedI12
N16Dealer-A user pulls GET /reports/* or GET /dashboard/* expecting client-side access to B rowsServer-side aggregate over A only; no foreign rows in payloadI13
N17Inbound webhook (/webhooks/meta|whatsapp|twilio) that cannot be resolved to a single dealerQuarantined in webhook_events; no data-plane writeI14
N18Dealer-A user requests GET /vehicles/:id, GET /deals/:id, GET /finance-applications/:id, GET /service-orders/:id for B-owned rows404 on every entity (uniform)I3, I4
N19Dealer-A user edits role_field_permissions to unmask a finance field, then reads a B finance appField rules apply within A only; B row still 404I3, I4
N20Super-admin token used against a data-plane write without a valid x-dealer-id403 — platform authority does not auto-scope data-plane mutations§1, I2

8. How other sections rely on P5

  • R2 (Leads/CRM): assumes I3/I4 so /leads, /customers, notes, and the single Advance CTA never span tenants; stage-checklist config lives in /admin/stage-checklists (§6).
  • R4 (Deals/Quotes): A3 tax codegen reads /admin/taxes (§6); below-floor and VIN-lock gates write gates under activeDealerId.
  • R5 (Finance/Money): depends on I11 idempotency scoping and LOS credential separation (§2); no payment gateway — ledger only.
  • R6 (GRA/Compliance): Guyana duty is server-computed and human-gated; AI extracts legible fields only, under I6/I8/I9.
  • R7 (Comms): webhook resolution (I14) and per-dealer WhatsApp/Twilio/Gmail creds (§2) keep inbound traffic tenant-correct.
  • P4 (Governance): owns kill-switches and feature-flags; P5 only asserts that flipping them is governance-gated (I15) and audited (I12).

Cross-Module Integration

Integration X1 — Entity Ownership & Relationship Map

This map is the authoritative registry of every one of the ~57 persisted tables in AURA. For each entity it fixes one creating module, one lifecycle owner role, its load-bearing foreign keys, the RBAC module that gates it (role_permissions module × category — see R1), whether it is dealer-scoped, whether it is audited, and whether it carries a formal state machine (defined in R2). The governing rule: nothing is orphaned — every data-plane row is reachable from a tenant root (dealers) and, transitively, from the CRM→Inventory→Booking→Delivery→Asset→Service→Finance "golden thread" described below.

Scoping & audit conventions

  • Dealer-scoped = y means every read/write is filtered by activeDealerId(res) derived from the x-dealer-id header, which must be a member dealer of the caller (dealer_users) or the request is rejected. Per CORRECTIONS, a cross-tenant reference at the data plane returns 404 (no existence leak); 403 is reserved for the control-plane check where membership itself is the gate (/platform/*, /admin/*). This now includes concierge conversations/messages, audit_logs, and object-storage ACLs.
  • Dealer-scoped = n is reserved for genuinely global identity/tenant-root rows: dealers (the tenant root itself) and users (global Clerk identity; per-dealer role lives in dealer_users).
  • Audited = y means mutations emit an audit_logs row (actor, before/after, entityRef). AI mutations additionally emit agent_runs via recordAgentRun. Impersonation is audited (raw AUTH_BYPASS/x-test-user-email retired).
  • State-machine = y entities are governed by explicit transition rules and, where applicable, versioned stage_checklists; see R2 for each machine and its 422 {unmet:[]} semantics.

Master entity ownership table (all ~57 tables)
