The golden thread — how the modules link end-to-end

A single customer opportunity flows through the system as a chain of foreign keys, so no downstream entity is ever orphaned from the opportunity that spawned it:

#LinkFK that carries the threadTrigger / gate
1lead → customer + contactleads.customerId, contacts.customerIdA1 Intake/Dedup on enquiry/webhook; A2 round-robin assignment
2lead → quotequotes.leadIdA3 deterministic tax quote (uses dealer_taxes) → pdfkit
3lead + quote + appraisal → dealdeals.leadId, deals.appraisalId, deals.vehicleIdAdvance past checklist (R2); below-floor >5% opens a gate
4deal → vehicledeals.vehicleId (A11 VIN allocation)VIN-lock: vehicle available→reserved→sold
5deal → bookingbookings.dealIdreservation confirmed
6deal → finance_application → los_submissionfinance_applications.dealId, los_submissions.financeApplicationIdA5 doc pre-fill (HITL); Demerara LOS connector
7deal/booking → deliverydeliveries.dealId, deliveries.vehicleIdauto-delivery on deal close; 11-step advance + PDI (R2)
8delivery → assetassets.deliveryId, assets.vehicleId, assets.customerIddelivery completion materializes the owned-vehicle asset — the sales→service handoff
9delivery/deal → invoice → payment → receiptinvoices.dealId, payments.invoiceId, receipts.paymentIdmoney mutations gated by idempotency_keys; ledger only (no gateway)
10asset → service_order → job_card → job_card_parts → partsservice_orders.assetId, job_cards.serviceOrderId, job_card_parts.jobCardId/partIdservice intake against an owned asset; parts drawn from stock (part_purchases replenish)
11service_order/job_card → service_invoice; asset → coverage_planservice_invoices.serviceOrderId, coverage_plans.assetIdservice billing closes the loop back into the money ledger

Cross-cutting attachments (why nothing is orphaned)

  • Every entity above carries dealerId (except the two tenant-root globals), so no row exists outside a tenant. Divisions (CAM/GT) provide the second scoping axis where present.
  • activity, timeline_events, documents, tasks, gates, notifications, email_logs, and audit_logs attach to any business entity through a polymorphic entityRef (type + id), so cross-cutting workflow, approvals, files and audit hang off the golden thread rather than floating free.
  • agent_runs reference the entity each AI action touched (lead, quote, delivery, finance app, etc.), giving every AI decision a scoped, auditable home — enforcing MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE, guardUntrusted, isAgentEnabled at the point of write (governance detail in the AI section; state transitions in R2).
  • Control-plane rows (dealers, users, dealer_users, roles, role_permissions, role_field_permissions) are the roots that authorize the data plane — the dealer_users membership is what makes a data-plane query legal (else 404 at data plane, 403 at control plane per CORRECTIONS).

Integration X2 — Cross-Module Event & State Propagation

AURA is not a set of screens; it is one connected state machine. A single user action (or inbound webhook) fans out into writes across many of the ~57 tables, appends to timeline_events + activity, may open or resolve gates, fires notifications/email_logs, and — only when governance permits — invokes an agent through recordAgentRun. This section is the canonical event graph: every row is Event → Preconditions → Cross-module Effects → Notifications → Agents → Idempotency/Compensation. Every effect below is dealer-scoped (activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id ∈ caller memberships) and money-touching effects are guarded by idempotency_keys. See R2 (tenancy & RBAC), R3 (money/GYD), and the agent-governance reference for the primitives referenced throughout.

X2.0 — Universal invariants applied to every row below

  • Tenancy: every read/write carries dealerId = activeDealerId(res); a row whose dealerId ≠ caller scope is invisible — cross-tenant reference resolves to 404 (no existence leak) at the data-plane; 403 is reserved for the membership check itself (header ∉ dealer_users). Division (CAM/GT) is the second axis and never widens tenant scope.
  • Audit + timeline: each event appends one activity row (actor, verb, target) and one or more timeline_events (entity-anchored, user-visible). Control-plane and impersonated actions additionally write audit_logs (audited impersonation replaces raw AUTH_BYPASS/x-test-user-email).
  • Idempotency: money mutations (payments, receipts, refunds, LOS submissions) require an Idempotency-Key header persisted in idempotency_keys; a replay returns the first stored response, never a second write.
  • Agents: an agent runs only if isAgentEnabled(agent) (kill-switch agents.status), passes guardUntrusted on all free text, PII-minimizes the payload, records agent_runs, and clears MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE; below-threshold output is suppressed to a HITL suggestion, never auto-committed.
  • Gates: a required approval is a first-class gates row (type, status open|resolved|rejected, target entity). Blocking effects wait on gate.resolve; see the gate matrix in X2.4.

X2.1 — Lead lifecycle: intake → assignment → stage advance

EventPreconditionsCross-module effects (tables · status)NotificationsAgentsIdempotency / Compensation
enquiry.created
POST /api/enquiries · inbound webhooks/meta, webhooks/whatsapp, Gmail IMAP intake		 Valid dealer resolvable from channel routing; payload text treated as untrusted. Anonymous/token endpoints still resolve a dealerId. leads insert (status=new, division set by source map); customers + contacts created or matched on dedupe; lead_sources attributed; comm_notes/whatsapp_conversations+whatsapp_messages or email_logs threaded; timeline_events: lead created; webhook_events logs raw envelope. notifications to inbound queue owner / GM; owner-notify optional. A1 Intake/Dedup — normalizes + matches existing customers; below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE → creates a review suggestion, does not auto-merge. guardUntrusted on message body. webhook_events deduped by provider message id → duplicate delivery is a no-op. Dedup merge is reversible via customers/relations unlink.
lead.assigned
POST /api/leads/:id/assign		 Lead ∈ scope; target user has membership in same dealerId+division; assigner has leads:edit. leads.assignedToUserId set; dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt bumped for round-robin fairness; tasks "first contact" created; timeline_events: assigned. notifications to assignee (+ reporting manager via reportingManagerUserId). A2 Round-robin — deterministic selection by division + lastLeadAssignedAt + capacity; no ML nondeterminism. Runs only when auto-assign flag on. Re-assign overwrites idempotently (last-writer). Fairness clock corrects itself on next pick; manual reassign is the compensation.
lead.advance (per stage)
POST /api/leads/:id/advance — the ONE Advance CTA		 Every item in versioned stage_checklists for the target stage satisfied. Unmet → 422 {unmet:[]}, zero writes. leads.stage transitions; stage-specific side entities created (see per-stage rows below); timeline_events per transition; activity logged. notifications to owner + manager on stage change. A15 Pipeline suggestions may recommend next best action (HITL, nav-only surfacing). 422 is the guard — an incomplete advance never partially writes. Reverse advance is a manual stage set with reason logged to timeline_events.
advance → Qualified Contact confirmed, source attributed, consent captured. leads.stage=qualified; customer_personas may be seeded; tasks for needs-analysis. Owner. Persona/vehicle recommend (HITL) via customers/:id/persona/recommend. Idempotent stage set.
advance → Test-Drive Availability confirmed; Test-Drive is a first-class entity (not a free note). Test-drive record via /leads/:id/test-drive + tokenized /api/test-drive/:token; vehicles/assets soft-held for slot; bookings not yet created. Customer (token link) + advisor. A10 Test-drive availability — proposes slots vs calendar/vehicle; deterministic conflict check. Token single-use; expiry releases the soft-hold. No money involved.
advance → Quote/Proposal Vehicle selected; tax profile resolvable from dealer_taxes. See quote.generated below. Owner. A3 (below). See quote row.
advance → Negotiation / Decision /leads/:id/decision recorded; /leads/:id/review = disambiguated Review (manager deal review, distinct from agent-run review). gates may open (below-floor); leads.stage=negotiation; timeline_events. Manager on review request. A6 Call sentiment on /leads/:id/calls + calls/suggest-sentiment. Decision reversible until deal commit.
advance → Won (→ deal) Manager review passed; below-floor gate (if any) resolved. Triggers deal.commit (next table). Owner + finance queue. — See deal.commit.

X2.2 — Sales close: quote → deal commit → reservation → payment → booking expiry

EventPreconditionsCross-module effects (tables · status)NotificationsAgentsIdempotency / Compensation
quote.generated
POST /api/leads/:id/quote · /quote/pdf · /quotes/send		 Lead ∈ scope; vehicle + dealer_taxes present; dealer.usdExchangeRate set for GYD display. quotes insert (USD-scale line items; GYD = USD×rate at render); deals draft may be created; documents/email_logs on send; timeline_events: quote sent. Customer (send) + owner. A3 Quote/Code — deterministic tax computation from dealer_taxes + pdfkit render; no AI-invented figures. A7 Outreach composer drafts the send message (HITL Approve & Send). Regenerating a quote versions it; sends deduped in email outbox (retry/backoff/dedupe). PDF render is pure/reproducible.
deal.commit
deals — VIN-lock + auto-delivery		 Won lead; below-floor gate resolved if discount > 5%; specific VIN/engineNumber (17 chars) selected. deals.status=committed; VIN-lock: vehicles/assets row status → allocated (exclusive); auto-create deliveries (11-step, step 1); invoices draft; timeline_events on lead + deal + vehicle. Finance + delivery advisor queues. A11 VIN allocation — deterministic pick of an unallocated in-scope unit; conflicts rejected, never double-allocated. Uncommit compensates: release VIN-lock (vehicle → available), void draft delivery + draft invoice, log reversal. gates below-floor must be resolved first (no bypass).
reservation.paid
booking + customer account creation		 Deal exists; reservation amount valid; Idempotency-Key present. payments insert (reservation), receipts issued; bookings created with expiry; customers account/ledger opened; invoices/outstanding-balances recomputed; vehicle hold firmed; timeline_events. Customer receipt (email_logs) + sales. — Idempotent via idempotency_keys. Compensation = refund gate (see cancellation) + booking cancel + vehicle release.
payment.recorded
POST /api/payments		 Invoice ∈ scope; amount ≤ outstanding (over-payment → credit); Idempotency-Key. payments insert; receipts issued; invoices.status → partial/paid; outstanding-balances recomputed (USD-scale, GYD display); timeline_events. No external gateway — ledger only. Customer receipt + AR. — Replay returns first receipts row. Compensation = reversing payment entry (negative) + receipt void, both audited; invoice status recomputed.
booking.expired
scheduled sweep · bookings/remind		 Booking past expiry with no qualifying payments/deal progression. bookings.status=expired; vehicle release: vehicles/assets → available (VIN-lock cleared); associated draft deliveries/invoices voided; timeline_events. Owner + customer (lapse notice) via notifications/email_logs. — Idempotent: expiring an already-expired booking is a no-op. Re-reservation re-locks the VIN only if still available (else 422 conflict).

X2.3 — Delivery & handoff: 11-step advance → invoice → complete → service

EventPreconditionsCross-module effects (tables · status)NotificationsAgentsIdempotency / Compensation
delivery.advance:invoice
POST /api/deliveries/:id/advance + invoice.pdf		 Prior steps (incl. PDI via /pdi) complete; deal committed; VIN still locked to this delivery. deliveries.step → invoice step; invoices finalized from deal/quote (USD-scale, GYD render); documents stores invoice PDF (pdfkit); outstanding-balances updated; timeline_events. Customer (invoice) + finance. — Step advance is monotonic + idempotent per step; re-advance to same step no-ops. Invoice finalize reversible only by voiding delivery back a step (audited).
delivery.advance:complete
final of the 11 steps		 Invoice paid or approved terms; delivery advisor assigned via delivery-advisors; all checklist steps satisfied. deliveries.status=completed; asset creation: assets row transferred to customer ownership (VIN/engine locked to customer); service handoff: coverage_plans activated + first service_orders schedule seeded; survey task queued; deals.status=delivered; timeline_events across deal, asset, customer. Customer (delivery confirm + survey link) + service dept + owner. A7 Outreach drafts the survey/thank-you (HITL Approve & Send). Completion is terminal for the sales flow; reversal is an exception path creating a return case, not a step-back. Asset ownership transfer is single-shot (idempotent on delivery id).

X2.4 — Gates: resolution matrix (gate.resolve per type)

POST /api/gates/:id/resolve — the resolver must hold the gate's required permission/role; approve vs reject each write distinct downstream effects. Resolution is idempotent (a resolved gate re-resolved returns its stored outcome).

X2.5 — Reversal & exceptions: cancellation

EventPreconditionsCross-module effects (tables · status)NotificationsAgentsIdempotency / Compensation
cancellation
deal/booking cancel with refund		 Deal/booking ∈ scope; if money was taken, a refund gate must exist and be resolved (see X2.4). Compensating sequence: gates refund resolved → payments reversal + receipts void (idempotent) → bookings.status=cancelled → vehicle release (vehicles/assets → available, VIN-lock cleared) → deals.status=cancelled, invoices voided → history preserved: original rows retained, reversal appended to timeline_events + audit_logs (never hard-deleted). Customer (refund/cancel) + owner + finance. — Whole sequence is a saga: each step idempotent; partial failure is retried, not double-applied (guarded by idempotency_keys). Refund cannot fire before its gate resolves — no silent money movement.

X2.6 — Aftersales: service order → job card → parts → service invoice → coverage

EventPreconditionsCross-module effects (tables · status)NotificationsAgentsIdempotency / Compensation
service_order.created (Case)
POST /api/service-orders		 Customer + assets (owned VIN) ∈ scope; Case is a first-class entity linking the visit to the asset history. service_orders insert (status open); linked to assets + coverage_plans (warranty check); technician assignable via service-technicians; timeline_events on asset + customer. Customer (booking confirm) + service advisor. — Duplicate open case for same asset+window flagged, not blocked. Cancel closes case with reason (history kept).
job_card.open
POST /api/job-cards		 Parent service_orders open; technician assigned. job_cards insert (status open); labor lines seeded; service_orders → in-progress; timeline_events. Technician + advisor. — Idempotent per service order; reopening a closed card creates a new card, preserving prior.
job_card.parts (stock move)
/job-cards/:id/parts		 Job card open; requested parts in stock (≥ qty) in scope. job_card_parts insert; stock decrement on parts; consumption may trigger part_purchases reorder against suppliers; job card cost accrues; timeline_events. Parts dept on low-stock reorder. — Stock move idempotent per line id; removing a part line restores stock (compensating increment, audited). Insufficient stock → 422, no partial decrement.
service_invoice.paid
service-invoices + POST /api/payments		 Job card(s) closed; invoice ∈ scope; coverage applied where valid; Idempotency-Key. service_invoices.status=paid; payments+receipts; warranty/coverage portion netted against coverage_plans; service_orders → completed; outstanding-balances updated; timeline_events on asset. Customer receipt + AR. A6 Call sentiment digest may sample the visit follow-up call. Idempotent payment; refund path mirrors X2.5 refund gate.
coverage.due (cadence reminder)
coverage/remind · service-orders/remind		 Active coverage_plans with next-service date within cadence window; asset still owned in scope. No state mutation to plan; creates tasks/notifications; may seed a draft service_orders suggestion; timeline_events: reminder sent. Customer (service-due) + advisor, via email_logs/WhatsApp. A7 Outreach composer drafts the reminder (HITL Approve & Send); guardUntrusted on any customer-supplied context. Reminder deduped per plan+window in the outbox — a re-run in the same window does not double-send.

X2.7 — Reading the graph end-to-end

  • Sales spine: enquiry.created → lead.assigned → lead.advance×N → quote.generated → deal.commit (VIN-lock + auto-delivery) → reservation.paid → delivery.advance:complete → asset born → aftersales spine.
  • Aftersales spine: asset → service_order.created (Case) → job_card.open → job_card.parts (stock/supplier chain) → service_invoice.paid → coverage.due loop.
  • Money spine: every value node (reservation.paid, payment.recorded, service_invoice.paid, refunds) is USD-scale, GYD-rendered at dealer.usdExchangeRate, idempotency-guarded, and reversible only through an audited compensating entry — never a hard delete.
  • Governance spine: gates block state; agents only suggest across the graph (deterministic where money/tax/VIN are involved — A2/A3/A11 — and HITL where judgment is involved — A5/A7/GRA); reports (reports, dashboard/*) aggregate this graph server-side within dealer scope. Reversal always appends history; cross-tenant traversal is impossible by construction (404 at data-plane).

Integration X3 — End-to-End Scenario Matrix (all situations)

This reference enumerates the ~24 cross-cutting journeys AURA must survive intact, tracing each from its originating trigger through the module/layer path, the gates (gates entity + stage 422 {unmet:[]} against versioned stage_checklists) and agents that fire, the mandatory HITL decision points, and the terminal outcome written to timeline_events / activity / audit_logs. Every path below is dealer-scoped — activeDealerId(res) is derived per request and every query filters by it; a cross-tenant read at the data-plane resolves to 404 (no existence leak), and 403 is reserved for the membership check itself (caller's x-dealer-id ∉ dealer_users). All money is USD-scale (GYD = USD × dealers.usdExchangeRate, see R6), all timestamps America/Guyana GMT-4, all mutating money paths carry an idempotency_keys row, and every AI hop obeys isAgentEnabled + recordAgentRun + guardUntrusted + MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE (see R9 governance, R11 agents).

Legend

  • Gate — a blocking gates row or a stage-advance 422 {unmet:[]}; resolved via POST /gates/:id/resolve or by satisfying the checklist, never by skipping the single Advance CTA (see X1 for the ONE-Advance rule).
  • HITL — human-in-the-loop: agent output is a suggestion requiring an explicit human Approve/Send/Confirm before any external effect or state change.
  • Terminal — the durable end-state; every terminal writes timeline_events + audit_logs, and money terminals reconcile invoices/payments/receipts/outstanding-balances.
  • Agent codes: A1 Intake/Dedup, A2 Round-robin assign, A3 Quote/tax, A5 Doc pre-fill (vision, HITL), A6 Call sentiment, A7 Outreach composer (HITL), A10 Test-drive availability, A11 VIN allocation, A15 Pipeline suggestions.

1. Retail & sales journeys

#ScenarioTriggerModule / layer pathGates + agents firedHITL pointsTerminal outcome
S1 Standard retail cash sale Walk-in / web POST /enquiries or Meta Lead Ads webhooks/meta enquiries → leads → leads/:id/advance → quote → deals → bookings → invoices+payments+receipts → deliveries (11-step) — see R2, R3, R4, R6, R7 A1 dedup on intake; A2 round-robin assign (deterministic, updates dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt); A3 quote (deterministic tax + pdfkit); A11 VIN allocation at deal; A15 pipeline nudges. Stage gates: each advance validates versioned stage_checklists → 422 {unmet} Sales confirms quote before quote/send; owner sign-off via notify-owner if configured; delivery PDI sign-off Full-pay receipts settle outstanding-balances to zero; deliveries reaches step 11; VIN-locked; deals=completed; timeline closed
S2 Bank-financed sale (LOS approve / decline) Customer selects finance on quote → POST /finance-applications finance-applications → finance-connector → LOS Demerara (los_submissions) → banks → deal → delivery — see R5 A5 doc pre-fill (vision) on finance-documents; los_submissions/:id/sync polls decision. Gate: deal cannot advance to delivery until finance status ∈ {approved}. Decline → gates row blocks; VIN allocation (A11) deferred until approval to avoid stranding stock Finance officer reviews A5-prefilled forms (confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE ⇒ forced manual); submit to LOS is explicit; on decline, advisor chooses re-submit / switch bank / convert to cash Approve: finance funds booked as payments, deal→delivery. Decline: deal parked, gate open, lead re-nurtured (A7 outreach); no VIN consumed
S3 Trade-in + finance + accessories Customer offers trade vehicle during quoting appraisals → appraisals value nets against quote lines; accessories added as quote/invoice lines; finance on net — see R5, R3 A3 recomputes tax on net + accessory lines (deterministic); appraisal is human-entered, AI only assists condition notes (guardUntrusted). Gate: appraisal must be finalized before it can offset a quote; below-floor check (see S17) runs on net discount Appraiser finalizes valuation; sales confirms net figure; finance re-runs if net changes principal Trade vehicle enters assets/vehicles inventory as used stock; net-financed deal proceeds; single reconciled invoices with trade credit + accessory lines
S4 Fleet / PO multi-vehicle order-split Corporate buyer PO for N units → single customers (company) enquiry lead → deal with N line items → split into N deliveries + per-VIN allocation; consolidated invoices with PO reference — see R3, R4, R7 A11 allocates N distinct VINs (VIN-lock per unit, uniqueness enforced, see S19); A3 taxes per unit. Gate: each unit is its own 11-step delivery advance; PO number captured as gate prerequisite Fleet manager confirms per-VIN allocation; staggered delivery scheduling confirmed per unit One deals parent, N delivered children; partial completion allowed (some units delivered, others pending stock); balance tracked in outstanding-balances
S5 Repeat / household buyer (account reuse) New enquiry matches existing customers/contacts A1 dedup surfaces match → reuse customers, link via customer relations; customers/:id/overview + persona — see R2 A1 dedup (confidence-gated merge suggestion); Persona/vehicle recommend agent proposes next vehicle. Gate: merge is HITL, never automatic Sales confirms the dedup match / rejects false positive; household relationship (relations) confirmed manually Single customer record with full timeline_events history; no duplicate; persona enriched; new deal opened under existing account
S6 Skip-test-drive direct buy (STP) Decisive buyer opts out of test drive lead advance path where test-drive stage is explicitly waived (recorded), not silently skipped — see R2 Stage checklist item satisfied via a recorded "test-drive waived" decision (leads/:id/decision), not by bypassing the Advance CTA; A15 confirms readiness Customer's waiver captured by staff (attributable); no agent auto-waives Lead advances straight to quote/deal with an audited waiver in timeline_events; no bookings test-drive row created

2. Test-drive, cancellation, refund & reallocation

S7 Test-drive no-show → reschedule Booked slot elapses with no arrival leads/:id/test-drive + test-drive/:token booking → bookings/:id/remind → reschedule — see R2, R7 A10 test-drive availability re-proposes slots; reminder via WhatsApp/email/telephony (see S21 opt-out fallback). No gate blocks; SLA timer on no-show restart Advisor confirms new slot; customer re-confirms via token link New bookings slot; original marked no-show in timeline_events; lead stays in stage (no penalty advance)
S8 Cancellation — pre-registration (simple) Buyer cancels before vehicle registered / delivered deal → cancel path; VIN released back to inventory — see R3, R4 A11 VIN de-allocation returns unit to available pool. Simple path: no refund gate if no money captured; if deposit taken, routes to S9 refund. No gates approval needed pre-registration Advisor confirms cancellation reason (dropdown, audited) Deal→cancelled; VIN freed; deposit (if any) flows to refund; timeline closed with reason
S9 Cancellation — post-registration (gate + refund) Buyer cancels after registration / partial delivery deal cancel → approval gate → refund via payments reversal + receipts — see R6 Gate: post-registration cancellation opens a gates row requiring manager approval (financial + regulatory exposure). Refund payments row carries idempotency_keys. No payment gateway — refund is ledger reversal only Manager approves via gates/:id/resolve; finance confirms refund amount net of registration/duty already remitted to GRA (see S-GRA in R5) Refund ledgered; outstanding-balances reconciled; VIN may be un-registerable for resale — flagged; audited
S10 Refund on bounced / reversed payment Bank notifies cheque/transfer reversal after receipt issued payments reversal → receipts void → outstanding-balances re-opened — see R6 Gate: reversal re-opens balance and can block delivery advance if goods not yet handed over; idempotency prevents double-reversal. No auto-refund — negative event, not a payout Finance confirms reversal against bank statement; manager notified via notifications/tasks Receipt voided, balance restored, delivery gate re-armed; customer contacted (A7 outreach, HITL); audited
S11 VIN recall after allocation → reallocate Manufacturer/QC recall flags an allocated VIN vehicles flag → deal VIN-lock broken → A11 reallocate — see R4 Gate: recalled VIN cannot proceed to delivery; A11 proposes replacement VIN of same spec (deterministic, uniqueness-checked). Delivery advance blocked until reallocation resolved Sales confirms substitute unit; customer notified of any spec/color delta and consents New VIN locked to deal; recalled unit quarantined in inventory; audited reallocation in timeline_events
S12 Concurrency — double VIN allocation Two advisors allocate the same VIN simultaneously A11 allocation under DB uniqueness constraint on VIN-lock — see R4 No agent overrides the constraint; second write loses at the persistence layer (unique index), returns conflict. A11 re-proposes next available VIN to the loser Losing advisor re-confirms the newly proposed VIN Exactly one deal holds the VIN; second deal auto-reoffered alternative; both paths audited; zero oversell

3. Cross-division, delivery & service

#ScenarioTriggerModule / layer pathGates + agents firedHITL pointsTerminal outcome
S13 Cross-division interest (CAM ↔ GT, no transfer) Lead in CAM division wants a GT-division vehicle (same dealer) divisions second axis on lead/deal; no cross-division record transfer — see R2, X2 Division is a scoping axis, not a tenant boundary; A2 assignment respects dealer_users.divisionId. No gate crosses divisions silently; a new division-scoped lead/deal is opened rather than moving the record Manager confirms which division owns the resulting deal (commission/inventory implications) Two clean division-scoped records linked by customer; no ownership ambiguity; audited
S14 Delivery rejected at handover Customer refuses vehicle at PDI/handover (damage, wrong spec) deliveries/:id/pdi fails → 11-step advance halts — see R7 Gate: PDI failure blocks delivery advance; opens gates row. Depending on cause routes to rework (S16), reallocation (S11), or cancellation (S9) Delivery advisor records rejection reason + PDI evidence (photos to GCS presigned); manager triages Delivery reverts to pre-handover step; VIN stays locked pending resolution; balance not settled; audited with evidence
S15 Scheduled service w/ parts backorder / supersession Booked service-orders needs a part that is out of stock or superseded service-orders → job-cards → job_card_parts → parts/suppliers/part-purchases → service-invoices — see R8 Gate: job card cannot close/invoice while a required part is unfulfilled; supersession swaps the superseded part number to its replacement in job_card_parts. coverage/service-orders/:id/remind notifies customer of delay Service advisor confirms supersession substitution; customer approves any price delta before order Backordered part raised as part-purchases; job card held open; on receipt, fitted and service-invoices raised; audited
S16 Unscheduled accident → insurance claim → rejected → customer-pay Accident-damaged vehicle arrives; insurer involved service-order (insurance type) → estimate → claim submission → claim outcome → invoice re-target — see R8, R5 Gate: work authorization gate pending insurer approval; on rejection, invoice payer switches from insurer to customer, re-opening outstanding-balances. A5 assists claim doc extraction (HITL, guardUntrusted on insurer docs) Advisor confirms estimate; insurer decision recorded; on rejection, customer explicitly accepts self-pay before work proceeds Claim rejected ⇒ customer-billed service-invoices; if declined, vehicle released un-repaired with audited authorization trail
S17 Comeback / rework (rectify) Customer returns; prior job did not resolve fault new job-cards linked to original as rework — see R8 Rework flagged; internal-cost vs chargeable determined. No customer charge if dealer-fault rectification. A6 sentiment on the comeback call flags escalation risk Service manager classifies rework as warranty/goodwill/chargeable (see S18) Rectification job closed at dealer cost when own-fault; linked to original for quality metrics; audited
S18 Warranty vs goodwill Repair claimed under coverage_plans warranty or discretionary goodwill coverage → job-card → service-invoice with payer = warranty | goodwill | customer — see R8 Gate: warranty eligibility validated against coverage_plans terms; goodwill requires manager approval gates row (discretionary spend) Warranty auto-eligible ⇒ advisor confirms coverage; goodwill ⇒ manager approves the concession amount Payer set correctly; warranty billed to plan, goodwill to dealer expense, else customer; audited concession trail

4. Approvals, platform lifecycle & governance

#ScenarioTriggerModule / layer pathGates + agents firedHITL pointsTerminal outcome
S19 Below-floor discount approval Quote/deal discount exceeds the 5% floor threshold deals below-floor gate (>5%) → gates row → gates/:id/resolve — see R3, R6 Gate: discount > 5% below floor opens a mandatory approval gate; deal cannot advance/lock VIN until resolved. A3 recomputes taxed totals at the approved price Manager (permission deals:admin/approver role) approves or rejects the discount; single Advance CTA re-enabled only on approval Approved ⇒ deal proceeds at discounted price with audited approver + amount; rejected ⇒ price reverts, customer re-quoted
S20 Dealer onboarding go-live Super-admin (SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL) provisions a new dealer Platform Console: POST /platform/dealers → divisions → platform/dealers/:id/members → entitlements/feature-flags + agent kill-switches → dealer seeds (admin/roles, role_permissions, lead-sources, stage-checklists, taxes, usdExchangeRate) → JIT provisioning on first login — see X2, R1 No sales gate; governance gates instead: entitlements toggle module availability, agents.status/isAgentEnabled default per-dealer kill-switches. PENDING-INFRA seams (lib/infra-seams.ts: backups/DR, encryption-at-rest, uptime, tracing, broker) are no-op but wired. First-user-becomes-GM is retired — GM assigned explicitly via dealer_users.isGeneralManager Super-admin sets exchange rate, seeds roles/checklists, assigns GM and initial members; dealer admin (settings:admin) tunes admin/* before go-live Dealer status=active; users JIT-provisioned globally (Clerk clerkId) with per-dealer dealer_users membership; data-plane opens under full dealer-scope; audited
S21 Dealer suspension mid-deal Super-admin sets dealers.status=suspended (non-payment / compliance) Platform Console governance → data-plane access change — see X2, R1 Gate at tenancy layer: suspended dealer's data-plane requests denied; in-flight deals frozen, not deleted. Agent kill-switches force-off. No cross-tenant leak — suspended-dealer reads by others still 404 Super-admin confirms suspension scope; on reinstatement, dealer admin resumes frozen deals from last audited state All dealer data preserved and scoped; access blocked while suspended; full continuity on reinstatement; audited status transitions
S22 WhatsApp opt-out → fallback Customer opts out via webhooks/whatsapp (STOP) or 24h window closed whatsapp_conversations/whatsapp_messages → channel fallback to email (nodemailer outbox) / telephony (Twilio) — see R10 A7 outreach composer re-targets the permitted channel; guardUntrusted on inbound message text; opt-out honored before any send. Email outbox retry/backoff/dedupe applies A7 output is HITL Approve&Send on the fallback channel; no auto-send to an opted-out number Consent state recorded; communication continues on a compliant channel or ceases; comm-notes/email_logs updated; audited
S23 Document AI mis-extract correction A5 vision pre-fill (finance/GRA docs) returns a wrong or low-confidence field finance-documents / customer_documents / gra/extract pre-fill → human correction — see R5, R9 A5 confidence < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE ⇒ field not auto-applied, forced manual entry; guardUntrusted on OCR text. GRA = Guyana: AI extracts only legible values, never fabricates; server computes duty; a human gate signs off. recordAgentRun logs the run for agents/runs/:id/review Staff corrects the field before submission; correction feeds agent review metrics Corrected data persisted; original AI suggestion + human override both audited in agent_runs; no fabricated regulatory value ever committed
S24 Agent kill-switch mid-flow Governance disables an agent (PATCH /agents, gated at governance level) while a flow relies on it agents (agents.status/isAgentEnabled) → dependent module degrades gracefully — see R9, R11 Kill-switch checked at every call site via isAgentEnabled; disabled ⇒ agent step becomes a manual task, flow does not crash. PATCH /agents requires governance permission, not mere settings:admin The formerly-automated step surfaces as a human tasks item (e.g., manual assignment replaces A2, manual quote replaces A3 UI-assist) Flow completes manually; no partial AI state; kill-switch toggle audited; metrics reflect degraded mode
S25 SLA breach cascade A stage/response SLA timer expires (unassigned lead, stale finance sync, backordered part, no-show) SLA timers across leads/finance-applications/service-orders → notifications/tasks escalation → dashboard/*/reports — see R2, R12 A15 pipeline suggestions flag at-risk items; escalation notifies reportingManagerUserId. Reports aggregate server-side (no client-side rollups). No hard gate — escalation, not blockage Manager acts on escalated tasks; can reassign (A2 re-run) or override SLA with reason Breach recorded for KPI; item re-prioritized; cascade contained (one breach escalates, does not silently fail downstream); audited

Cross-cutting invariants exercised by every row

  • Tenancy — no scenario escapes activeDealerId(res) + x-dealer-id membership scoping; cross-tenant read = 404, membership failure = 403 (see X2).
  • Money integrity — every payment/refund/reversal (S1, S9, S10, S16, S19) carries an idempotency_keys row; USD-scale storage, GYD display at dealer rate; no payment gateway, ledger-only (see R6).
  • Gate discipline — a single Advance CTA per entity; blocking is always an explicit gates row or a 422 {unmet} checklist result, never a hidden branch (see X1).
  • Agent governance — every AI hop (A1/A3/A5/A6/A7/A10/A11/A15 + GRA vision) is guarded by isAgentEnabled, MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE, guardUntrusted, and recordAgentRun; all external-effecting AI output is HITL (see R9, R11).
  • Impersonation — any staff-acting-as flow (support intervention in S21/S24) uses audited impersonation; raw AUTH_BYPASS/x-test-user-email is retired (see R1).
  • Auditability — every terminal writes timeline_events + audit_logs; reports and dashboards aggregate server-side (see R12).

Integration X4 — Failure, Concurrency & Recovery Taxonomy

This section is the authoritative reference for what breaks, how AURA detects it, who owns the recovery, and when a human must be pulled in. It is deliberately cross-cutting: every module (leads R2, deals/money R4, delivery R6, service/parts R7, finance/LOS R5, AI agents X2, platform/tenancy P1) inherits these rules rather than re-inventing them. All failure handling is dealer-scoped exactly like every other query: outbox rows, webhook_events, idempotency_keys, agent_runs, and audit_logs all carry dealerId, and a cross-tenant reference to any of them resolves to 404 at the data-plane (no existence leak), never 403 unless membership itself is the failing check.

X4.1 — Failure Domain Model

Every failure in AURA is classified along four axes before a handler is chosen. The taxonomy below is the vocabulary used by the rest of this section.

DomainDefinitionBlast radiusPrimary detectorRecorded in
External outageA third party (Meta, Twilio, Gmail, Demerara LOS, GCS, Anthropic) is down, slow, or returns 5xx/timeoutOne integration channel; graceful degradation elsewhereHTTP status / socket timeout / breakeremail_logs, agent_runs, webhook_events, los_submissions
Write conflictTwo transactions race to mutate the same row (VIN allocation, payment, stage advance)One entity; both actors see deterministic outcomeOptimistic version mismatch / row lock / unique indexrow version, audit_logs, timeline_events
Duplicate deliverySame webhook / callback / client retry arrives more than onceIdempotent no-op; single side effectwebhook_events ledger, idempotency_keys
Partial multi-stepA saga (delivery 11-step R6, dealer provisioning P1) fails between stepsWhole aggregate until compensated / resumedstep marker + transaction boundarydeliveries.currentStep, gates, activity
ConsistencyStale read, message re-ordering, read-after-write skewUI staleness / wrong orderingversion echo, monotonic timestampsreact-query cache, timeline_events
PoisonA message/job can never succeed (malformed payload, permanent 4xx)One job; quarantined so it cannot block the queueattempt-count thresholdDLQ column on webhook_events/outbox, audit_logs
AI failureLow confidence, timeout, refusal, or suspected hallucination from any agentSingle suggestion; never auto-appliedMIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE, guardUntrusted, timeoutagent_runs, gates, tasks

X4.2 — External Outage: detection, degradation & fallback

Each external dependency has an explicit degradation contract. The rule is uniform: never lose a durable intent, never block the data-plane on a third party, always leave an audit trail. Retry uses exponential backoff with jitter; a circuit breaker trips a channel to open after a rolling error threshold and half-opens on a probe. Tracing/uptime for these probes is a PENDING-INFRA seam in lib/infra-seams.ts (tracing, uptime) — the hooks exist and are no-ops until the platform wires an APM/broker.

DependencyFailure signalRetry / backoffBreaker + fallbackDegraded UXOwner
WhatsApp — Meta Cloud API v214xx template/window error vs 5xx/timeout5xx: outbox backoff 30s→8m, 6 tries; 4xx template: no retry, humanBreaker per dealer WABA; fallback to Twilio WhatsApp path where number is provisionedMessage shows queued→sent; 24h window closed → prompt template-onlyComms/integrations
Twilio (Voice + WhatsApp)REST 5xx, status callback failed/undeliveredBackoff on 429/5xx; honor Retry-AfterFallback to Twilio Voice stub in non-prod; call logged with provider:stub in call_logsCall/message flagged failed on leads/:id/calls; no silent dropComms/integrations
Gmail SMTP (nodemailer)SMTP 4xx/5xx, connection reset, auth errorOutbox: retry with backoff + dedupe key; permanent 5xx → DLQBreaker pauses sender; queued mail retained, not droppedemail_logs.status = queued/sent/failed; retry visibleComms/integrations
Gmail IMAP intakeIMAP disconnect, poll gap, dedupe collisionReconnect w/ backoff; resume from last UIDMissed poll self-heals on next cycle; dedupe by message-idInbound enquiry lands late but exactly once → leads via A1 IntakeComms/integrations
Demerara LOS (finance)Connector 5xx/timeout on submit/syncfinance-applications/:id/submit writes los_submissions first, then dispatches; sync retriedSandbox connector fallback in non-prod; live stays pending_submitApplication shows submitted/syncing; never lost — durable los_submissions rowFinance R5
GCS (object storage)Presign/upload 5xx, ACL denialPresign re-issue; client resumable retryNo local-disk fallback (compliance); upload deferred, gate heldDocument upload retriable; every object ACL dealer-scoped (correction)Storage/documents
Anthropic (claude-sonnet-4-6)429/5xx, stream abort, timeout, refusalShort bounded retry (≤2) then abandon — AI is never on the critical pathBreaker; feature degrades to manual; isAgentEnabled kill-switch honored firstSuggestion simply absent; user does the step by hand (see X4.7)AI/agents X2
  • Outbox pattern is the backbone of all outbound comms: the business transaction commits the domain row and an outbox intent atomically; a separate dispatcher drains it. This guarantees at-least-once send with idempotent dedupe, so an SMTP crash mid-send never loses or double-sends mail.
  • A message broker for the outbox/DLQ is a PENDING-INFRA seam (lib/infra-seams.ts): today the dispatcher is an in-process poller over the DB-backed queue; the seam lets it move to a real broker without changing producers.
  • Backups/DR, encryption-at-rest are also PENDING-INFRA no-op seams — recovery procedures below assume they will exist but do not depend on them for correctness of the failure logic.

X4.3 — Write Conflict & Concurrency Control

AURA uses optimistic concurrency by default and pessimistic row locks for scarce, money-or-inventory-critical resources. The canonical hazards are double VIN allocation and double payment.

HazardEndpointMechanismLoser seesInvariant protected
Double VIN allocation (A11)deals VIN-lock / deliveriesSELECT … FOR UPDATE on vehicles/assets row + unique partial index on assigned VIN; VIN-lock flag on deal409 conflict — vehicle already allocatedA VIN (17-char) is bound to at most one open deal/delivery
Double paymentpayments, receiptsRow lock on invoices balance + idempotency_keys guard on the write409 in_progress or replayed original resultOutstanding balance never goes negative; no duplicate ledger entry (no gateway — ledger only)
Concurrent stage advancePOST /leads/:id/advanceOptimistic version on lead; checklist re-evaluated inside txn409 (version) or 422 {unmet:[]} (checklist)ONE Advance CTA (correction); stage moves once, checklist honored
Below-floor deal editdeals (gate > 5%)Optimistic version + approval gates entity409 then re-fetch; gate re-opens if price re-crosses floorDiscount > 5% cannot commit without an open→resolved gate
Round-robin assignment (A2)POST /leads/:id/assignRow lock / atomic update on dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt; deterministic orderSerialized — next rep chosen without double-assignFair, deterministic distribution; no two leads to same tick
Job-card parts draw (R7)job-cards/:id/partsRow lock on parts stock qty409 / 422 insufficient stockParts stock never oversold
  • Optimistic path: client sends the version it read; server updates WHERE id=? AND version=?; zero rows affected ⇒ 409, client re-fetches and retries against fresh state. Used for leads, deals, customers, tasks, most PATCH surfaces.
  • Pessimistic path: used only where a lost update means money or a physical asset — VIN allocation, payment application, parts draw. Locks are held for the shortest possible transaction and never across an external call.
  • No external I/O inside a lock/transaction — Anthropic, GCS, Twilio and LOS calls happen outside the DB transaction; their durable intent is captured via outbox/los_submissions so a rollback never orphans a side effect.

X4.4 — Duplicate Delivery & Webhook Idempotency Ledger

Every inbound webhook (webhooks/meta, webhooks/whatsapp, webhooks/twilio/*, Meta Lead Ads, LOS callbacks, telephony status) is idempotent via the webhook_events ledger.

StepActionOutcome
1. VerifySignature/HMAC + dealer resolution; untrusted body passed through guardUntrusted before any AI touches itBad signature → 401; unknown tenant → 404
2. DedupeInsert (dealerId, provider, externalEventId) into webhook_events with unique constraintDuplicate → row exists → return 200 immediately, no re-processing
3. Persist-then-ackStore raw payload + set status=received, ack the provider fastProvider stops retrying; work continues async
4. ProcessFan out to domain handler (message → whatsapp_messages, delivery status → email_logs/call_logs, lead ad → A1 Intake)status=processed; on failure status=failed + attempt++
5. DLQAttempts exceed threshold or permanent parse error → status=deadQuarantined; alert; human replay (X4.6)
  • Providers retry aggressively; the unique externalEventId makes reprocessing a safe no-op even under a thundering-herd retry.
  • WhatsApp status transitions (sent→delivered→read) may arrive out of order; the handler is idempotent and monotonic — a lower status never overwrites a higher one (see X4.5 ordering).

X4.5 — Consistency: stale reads & message ordering

ProblemManifestationGuard
Stale read / lost updateUser edits a lead from a cached view another user already advancedOptimistic version on write → 409; react-query invalidation on mutation success
Read-after-write skewFreshly created row not visible in a listMutation returns the canonical row; client seeds cache, does not refetch-race
WhatsApp status re-orderread callback arrives before deliveredMonotonic status ladder; only forward transitions applied
Timeline orderingConcurrent events on one entitytimeline_events ordered by server timestamp (America/Guyana GMT-4) + insertion id tiebreak
SSE stream gapConcierge/Anthropic SSE drops mid-streamStream is nav/advisory-only; client re-establishes; no durable state depends on a partial stream
Exchange-rate driftGYD display uses a rate that changed mid-session (USD×usdExchangeRate)Rate snapshotted onto the money document at commit; display recomputes, ledger frozen

X4.6 — Idempotency-Key Replay, Poison & DLQ

All money-moving and side-effecting POSTs (payments, receipts, quote send, outreach send, delivery advance) accept an idempotency key tracked in idempotency_keys.

Key stateRequest behaviorResponse
noneFirst seen — claim key, begin workProceed; store result on completion
in_progressConcurrent retry while original still running409 in_progress — client backs off, does not duplicate
completedReplay after successReturn the stored original response verbatim — no re-execution
failedReplay after a retryable failureKey released for a fresh attempt
  • Poison messages: a job that fails deterministically (malformed payload, permanent 400 from provider, un-parseable AI output) is retried up to a bounded count, then moved to the DLQ (status=dead on webhook_events / outbox) so it cannot wedge the queue behind it.
  • DLQ recovery is a human, audited operation: an admin inspects the quarantined payload and triggers a replay. Replay re-enters at step 4 of X4.4 and is still idempotency-guarded, so a replay of an already-partially-applied event is safe.
  • Every replay, key claim, and DLQ action is written to audit_logs (dealer-scoped). Impersonated replays use the audited impersonation path — raw AUTH_BYPASS / x-test-user-email is retired (correction).

X4.7 — Partial Multi-Step Failure: Saga & Compensation

Two long, multi-step processes must survive a crash between steps: the delivery 11-step advance (R6) and dealer provisioning (P1). Both are modeled as resumable sagas — a durable step marker plus per-step compensation, rather than one giant transaction.

SagaDurable markerFailure mid-stepCompensation / resume
Delivery 11-step (PDI → invoice.pdf → handover)deliveries.currentStep + per-step gatesCrash after PDI gate resolved but before invoice generatedIdempotent resume from currentStep; each step re-entrant, no double invoice (idempotency key on deliveries/:id/advance)
Dealer provisioning (create dealer → divisions → seed roles/permissions → entitlements/flags → agent kill-switches)Step flags on dealers.status (e.g. provisioning→active)Fail after dealers row created but before roles seededIdempotent re-run of remaining steps; dealer stays provisioning (not usable) until saga completes; compensating delete only if unrecoverable
Deal → auto-deliveryDeal state + VIN-lockDeal committed, delivery creation failsDelivery creation is idempotent on deal id; retried without re-locking VIN
Quote send (pdfkit → email/WhatsApp)Quote status + outboxPDF generated, send failsOutbox retries send; PDF cached — not regenerated
Finance submit (persist → LOS dispatch → sync)los_submissions.statusPersisted, dispatch failsDispatcher retries from pending_submit; sync reconciles final state
  • Rule: within a single DB transaction, do all-or-nothing local writes; across steps that touch external systems, use a saga with a durable marker + idempotent, individually-compensatable steps. Never hold a transaction open across an external call.
  • Gates as saga checkpoints: the gates entity doubles as the human checkpoint. A step blocked on approval leaves an open gate; resolving it (gates/:id/resolve) advances the saga. This unifies compensation with HITL.

X4.8 — AI Failure: low-confidence, timeout & hallucination → route-to-human

No agent output is ever auto-committed to money, inventory, tenancy, or an outbound message without the governance chain. AI failures degrade to manual work + an audit row, never to a wrong action.

AgentFailure modeDetectionRoute-to-human
A1 Intake/DedupAmbiguous dedup matchScore < MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCELead created un-merged; suggestion surfaced for manual merge
A5 Doc pre-fill (vision)Illegible field / low confidencePer-field confidence; HITL by designField left blank for human entry — never fabricated
A7 Outreach composerOff-tone / unsafe draftguardUntrusted + confidenceHITL Approve & Send — draft never auto-sent
A6 Call sentimentUncertain sentimentConfidence thresholdAuto only above threshold; else suggest for human confirm
GRA vision extractIllegible / hallucinated valueguardUntrusted; extract-legible-only policyServer computes duty; AI extracts only what is legible; mandatory human gate before filing — no fabrication (correction)
A15 Pipeline suggestionsTimeout / emptyBounded timeoutSuggestion panel simply empty; pipeline unaffected
Concierge (SSE)Stream abort / refusalStream errorNav-only, read-only; user proceeds manually; no state mutated
Any agentKill-switch offisAgentEnabled / agents.statusFeature disabled cleanly; UI shows manual path; PATCH /agents gated at governance level (correction)
  • Every AI attempt — success or failure — is recorded via recordAgentRun into agent_runs (dealer-scoped, PII-minimized input), so a hallucination or timeout is forensically traceable and reviewable at agents/runs/:id/review.
  • Confidence enforcement is mandatory (correction): output below MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE is downgraded from auto to suggest, and suggest below the floor is dropped entirely — it does not surface a low-quality guess.
  • guardUntrusted on ALL untrusted text (correction): webhook bodies, inbound email/WhatsApp, document OCR, and any AI output routed back into a prompt are sanitized against injection before use.
  • A low-confidence or blocked AI step that gates a workflow opens a task or a gate assigned to the responsible human — the failure becomes visible work, not a silent gap.

X4.9 — Ownership & Escalation Matrix

Failure classFirst responder (automatic)Human ownerEscalation trigger
External outageRetry/backoff + breaker + stub fallbackIntegrations on-callBreaker open > threshold → alert (PENDING-INFRA uptime seam)
Write conflict409 + client re-fetchEnd user (retries)Repeated conflict on money row → supervisor gate
Duplicate webhookLedger no-opNoneOnly on DLQ
Partial sagaIdempotent resumeDelivery/provisioning ownerUnrecoverable step → compensation + admin review
Poison / DLQQuarantineDealer settings:admin / super-adminAny dead row → audited manual replay
AI failureDowngrade to manual + agent_runsAssigned rep / GM via task/gateKill-switch toggled at governance level

X4.10 — PENDING-INFRA seams referenced here

The following no-op seams in lib/infra-seams.ts are the future homes of production-grade recovery; the logic above is correct today without them and gains resilience when they are wired:

  • message broker — replaces the in-process DB-poller for outbox/DLQ dispatch without changing producers.
  • tracing — correlates a request across breaker trips, retries, saga steps, and agent runs.
  • uptime — powers breaker-open alerting and the escalation triggers in X4.9.
  • backups/DR — point-in-time recovery underpinning any catastrophic (non-transactional) rollback.
  • encryption-at-rest — protects the durable payloads captured in webhook_events, outbox, and los_submissions during their at-least-once lifetime.

Together these guarantee AURA's core promise under failure: exactly-once effect on money and inventory, at-least-once delivery of intent, deterministic conflict resolution, and a human in the loop for every irreversible or low-confidence action — all dealer-scoped and fully audited.

Validation & Foolproofing

Validation V1 — Coverage Completeness Matrix

This matrix proves that every first-class entity in AURA is fully governed across the twelve CRUD-and-control dimensions an enterprise DMS must satisfy. A cell is marked ✓ when the capability is implemented and grounded in a real endpoint/table/agent; ◑ flags a partial or deliberately-scoped capability (rationale given); GAP is an honest residual gap tracked below in Residual Gaps. Column semantics: C/R/U = create/read/update surface; D/Arch = hard-delete or soft-archive strategy; SM = lifecycle state-machine exists and is enforced server-side; Owner = an accountable owner-role/assignment column exists; RBAC = role_permissions module×category gate + role_field_permissions where relevant (see R2); Audit = writes land in audit_logs/activity/timeline_events (see R7); Edge = documented edge-case/foolproofing coverage (see V2); Doc = PDF/document artifact or documents linkage; Notif = notifications/email_logs/WhatsApp fan-out; Tests = contract + integration + tenancy-isolation coverage.

Global invariant applied to every row: every data-plane read/write is dealer-scoped by activeDealerId(res) derived from the validated x-dealer-id header (must ∈ caller dealer_users memberships), with the divisions CAM/GT axis as secondary filter. Cross-tenant reference at the data plane returns 404 (no existence leak); 403 is reserved for the membership check itself. This holds even for concierge, audit-logs, and object-storage ACL. Money is USD-scale internally, GYD = USD × dealers.usdExchangeRate at display only; dates are America/Guyana GMT-4.

2. Per-Module Proof-of-Coverage

2.1 Leads

  • C/R/U: POST/GET/PATCH /api/leads; ingress also via /api/enquiries, /api/webhooks/meta (Lead Ads), IMAP intake — all fanned through A1 Intake/Dedup (guardUntrusted + MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE before any auto-merge; see R5).
  • D/Arch ◑: leads are never hard-deleted; they are archived via terminal state (lost/disqualified) preserving timeline_events. Delete is intentionally not exposed — foolproofing against pipeline-history loss.
  • SM: versioned stage_checklists; single Advance CTA → POST /leads/:id/advance returns 422 {unmet:[]} when the version-pinned checklist is unmet. "Review" is disambiguated (see R3): the sales /leads/:id/review action is distinct from the agent-run agents/runs/:id/review governance action.
  • Owner: assignee via /leads/:id/assign; auto-assignment by A2 Round-robin (deterministic on dealer_users.lastLeadAssignedAt, division-scoped, respecting reportingManagerUserId).
  • Notif/Doc: /notify-owner, /whatsapp, /outreach (A7 HITL Approve&Send), /quote+/quote/pdf (A3 deterministic tax + pdfkit); /calls (+A6 sentiment).

2.2 Deals

  • SM + gates: below-floor discount >5% opens an approval gates record (gate entity, see R6); VIN-lock binds a specific vehicles.vin at commit; auto-delivery creates the linked deliveries row.
  • Edge: VIN/engineNumber 17-char validation; money mutations carry idempotency_keys; below-floor cannot bypass the gate (server-enforced, not UI-only).
  • D/Arch ◑: cancel transitions to terminal state; financial artifacts retained.

2.3 Customers

  • Full sub-resource fan-out: /overview,/contacts,/assets,/relations,/persona(+/recommend),/notes,/documents,/communications,/comm-notes. SM ◑: customers have status/persona lifecycle but no rigid state-machine (correct — they are master data, not a process). Persona/vehicle recommend agent is HITL and confidence-gated.
  • RBAC field-level: PII fields (national ID, phone) governed by role_field_permissions hidden|view|edit; AI calls PII-minimize before prompt assembly.

2.4 Vehicles / Assets

  • /vehicles/import + /import/template bulk ingest. Owner ◑: inventory ownership is dealer+division rather than a named user (by design). Notif ◑: no per-vehicle notification stream; surfaced through deal/booking events instead.
  • VIN uniqueness within dealer scope; registration ^[A-Z]{3}[0-9]{1,4}$ validated.

2.5 Bookings & 2.6 Deliveries

  • Bookings: /bookings/:id/remind; A10 test-drive availability feeds slot logic. Deliveries: SM enforced 11-step advance via /deliveries/:id/advance, with /pdi, /invoice.pdf, /delivery-advisors. Full audit + notification per step.

2.7 Finance

  • /finance-applications (+/submit,/sync,/documents), /finance-connector, /banks, /los-submissions to LOS Demerara (live/sandbox). A5 doc pre-fill is vision + HITL. State-machine tracks submit→sync→decision; finance_documents for artifacts.

2.8 Invoices / Payments

  • U ◑: issued invoices are immutable — corrections are credit/adjustment records, not edits (accounting foolproofing). No payment gateway: payments/receipts are a ledger; every posting is idempotent. outstanding-balances aggregates server-side. Tax via dealer_taxes (A3 deterministic).

2.9 Service & 2.10 Parts

  • Service: service_orders(+remind), job_cards(+parts,invoice), service_invoices, coverage_plans(+remind), service-technicians. Parts: parts, suppliers, part_purchases, job_card_parts.
  • Parts SM/Owner/Notif/Doc ◑ (residual): inventory has stock counts but no formal reorder state-machine, no named owner beyond supplier link, no reorder notifications, and no standalone document artifact. Tracked as G-1.

2.11 GRA / Import

  • Guyana-correct: duty is server-computed; gra/extract AI (vision) extracts only legible fields with MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE + guardUntrusted, never fabricates, and every filing passes a human gate before gra/filings submission. Full audit trail.

2.12 Platform / Tenancy

  • /platform/{dealers,dealers/:id/members,users} = SUPER-ADMIN only (SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL); /admin/* = dealer settings:admin. JIT provisioning on first Clerk login (first-user-becomes-GM retired). Per-dealer entitlements/feature-flags + agent kill-switches. PENDING-INFRA seams (lib/infra-seams.ts) are honest no-ops for backups/DR/encryption-at-rest/uptime/tracing/broker. D/Arch ◑: dealers are status-flagged (suspended), never deleted. See R1.

2.13 Agents

  • Every AI call: isAgentEnabled kill-switch → recordAgentRun → PII-minimize → guardUntrusted → MIN_AGENT_CONFIDENCE. /agents/runs, /metrics, /runs/:id/review (HITL). RBAC: PATCH /agents is gated at the governance level, not ordinary edit. Cross-tenant run reads → 404.

2.14 Notifications / Comms & 2.15 Cases/Tasks/Reviews

  • Email nodemailer/Gmail SMTP with outbox retry/backoff/dedupe (email_logs); WhatsApp Meta Cloud v21 + Twilio (whatsapp_conversations/messages, webhook_events idempotent by provider id); telephony Twilio Voice + stub. Cases/Test-Drive/Review are first-class entities (correction applied); gates+tasks+task_comments carry SM, owner, audit.

3. Residual Gaps (Honest Disclosure)

IDModuleGapSeverityMitigation / Rationale
G-1PartsNo reorder-point state-machine, named owner, reorder notification, or standalone document artifact.MediumStock counts and part_purchases exist; reorder is currently a manual report-driven flow. Roadmap: add reorder SM + threshold notifications.
G-2VehiclesNo per-vehicle notification stream; owner is dealer/division not a user.LowBy design — inventory is shared master data; events surface via deals/bookings.
G-3Infra seamsBackups/DR, encryption-at-rest, uptime SLO, tracing, message broker are PENDING-INFRA no-ops.High (pre-GA)Explicitly stubbed in lib/infra-seams.ts with typed seams so wiring is drop-in; must be provisioned before enterprise GA.
G-4Docs/StorageDocument U is version-append, not in-place edit; some entities (parts, vehicles) lack a bespoke document type.LowImmutability is intentional for audit integrity; GCS presigned URLs are dealer-scoped ACL.
G-5CustomersNo rigid lifecycle state-machine.NoneCorrect for master data; persona/status transitions are audited.

4. Completeness Verdict

All 15 modules satisfy Create, Read, Update, RBAC, Audit, Edge-case, and Tests unconditionally. Every ◑ is either a deliberate design choice (immutable financial/master data, shared-inventory ownership) or a tracked roadmap item (G-1, G-3). No module silently omits tenancy scoping, audit, or RBAC. The only true pre-GA blocker is G-3 (infra seams), which is architecturally isolated and does not affect functional correctness of the data plane. Coverage is therefore complete at the application layer and honestly gated at the infrastructure layer. Cross-references: tenancy R1, RBAC R2, Review disambiguation R3, agents R5, gates R6, audit R7, edge-cases V2.

Validation V2 — Domain Capability Traceability (10 Domains)

This section is the authoritative coverage map from the ten intended business domains of the AURA automotive DMS to the concrete spec sections that specify them. Every row names the REAL endpoints, tables, and agents that realize the capability, states the section that owns the detail, and carries an explicit status so that nothing is silently dropped. Read this alongside V1 (endpoint/OpenAPI-path coverage) and V3 (RBAC × field-permission coverage). Where a capability is intentionally out of scope for the current release it is flagged PHASE-2 or INFRA-SEAM and pointed at either P5 (platform entitlements/feature-flags) or lib/infra-seams.ts.

Legend — Section-ID Namespaces

  • lN — Architecture Layer sections (tenancy, data model, money, AI governance, integration fabric).
  • mX — Module sections: the dealer-workspace data-plane functional areas.
  • rX — Reference sections: enums, validation rules, error contracts, checklists.
  • PX — Platform Console (control-plane) sections: onboarding, RBAC, entitlements, audit.
  • Status — COVERED (fully specified & shipping) · PARTIAL (seam present, narrow scope) · PHASE-2 (deferred, explicitly reserved) · INFRA-SEAM (PENDING-INFRA no-op in lib/infra-seams.ts).
